*Shashwat Kumar,Arafat Rahman,Anuj Srivastava,P. -A. Absil*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出将单纯形乘积空间上的优化问题通过光滑且逐元素严格凸的重新参数化转化为流形上的无约束优化，并证明流形上的二阶KKT点映射为原问题的弱二阶KKT点，进而设计出优于投影梯度下降的黎曼梯度下降算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多实际问题（如低秩离散概率分布的张量分解、SRVF函数配准）可建模为单纯形乘积空间上的优化问题，但该空间非光滑且约束复杂，导致传统投影梯度法效率低且精度有限。

Method: 利用逐元素严格凸的光滑映射将乘积单纯形重新参数化为流形，将原约束问题转化为无约束流形优化；严格证明流形上的二阶KKT条件与原问题的弱二阶KKT条件等价，并基于此提出黎曼梯度下降算法。

Result: 实验表明所提RGD算法在收敛速度和精度上均优于投影梯度法，且在曲线配准任务中能更忠实保持函数形状。

Conclusion: 光滑重新参数化结合黎曼优化是处理单纯形乘积空间问题的有效手段，不仅提升优化性能，还为相关应用提供了更可靠的求解框架。

Abstract: We consider optimization problems defined on product spaces of simplices. Examples of this class of problems include learning low-rank discrete multivariate probability distributions via simplex constrained tensor decomposition and performing functional data registration under the Square Root Velocity Function (SRVF) representation. In this work, we demonstrate the feasibility of replacing the product simplex with a smooth, elementwise strictly convex reparameterization, resulting in an unconstrained optimization problem on a manifold. We show that performing such a reparameterization results in the second order Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) points on the smooth manifold being mapped to the weak second order KKT points on the product simplex. This leads to a Riemannian Gradient Descent (RGD) algorithm for solving the reparameterized problem, which outperforms Projected Gradient Descent (PGD), and provides a more faithful representation of the original function shapes while performing curve registration.

</details>


### [50] [From Simple QA to Deep Research: A Verifiable Benchmark Constructed through Iterative Task Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02163)
*Can Wang,Haoran Chen,Haowen Gao,Hao Ding,Zhaoyang Liu,Zhiying Tu*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 介绍了一个包含500个深度研究任务的自动化构建基准，采用迭代流程生成带DAG结构和检查点的任务，支持细粒度、稳定且对齐人类的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度研究基准依赖专家编写或已有材料，自动化构建难以保证一致和可追溯的验证，需要一种既能自动扩展又能确保评估质量的基准构建方法。

Conclusion: 该自动化基准构建方法有效解决了验证一致性问题，并提供公开数据、实现和结果，为深度研究评估提供了可靠资源。

Abstract: Deep research benchmarks require expert-level tasks and reliable evaluation grounded in task-specific knowledge. Existing benchmarks rely heavily on expert authoring or pre-existing human-authored materials, while fully automatic construction struggles to ensure consistent and traceable verification. To address this gap, we introduce a verifiable benchmark of 500 deep research tasks spanning 31 topics and 10 major categories, with three query forms designed to probe complementary capabilities required for deep research. The benchmark is constructed automatically using an iterative Explorer-Formalizer-Challenger pipeline that progressively transforms simple questions into deep research tasks. Each task is represented as a directed acyclic graph (DAG) of atomic steps and associated checkpoints, enabling the query, DAG, and rubrics to evolve together in a controlled manner. Experiments demonstrate that the benchmark clearly discriminates among models and query types, while its fact-grounded pointwise rubrics enable fine-grained, human-aligned, and stable evaluation. Our data, implementation, and results are publicly available.

</details>

TL;DR: 提出IBA-Bench基准和IBA-Agent框架，用于评估和提升智能体从隐含历史交互中推断用户约束并执行任务的行为对齐能力，实验表明现有LLM智能体在此任务上表现欠佳，而IBA-Agent显著提升效果。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化智能体基准多依赖静态偏好或固定交互日志，无法捕捉用户偏好随时间演变的特点，且忽视偏好条件化任务执行，存在知识到行动的鸿沟。

Method: 构建基于纵向交互历史（含噪声、隐含线索和时间不一致性）的IBA-Bench基准，评估智能体在满足隐含用户约束下执行任务的能力；提出IBA-Agent框架，通过广泛检索和轨迹级对齐来调和冲突优先级。

Conclusion: 隐含行为对齐是LLM智能体个性化中的关键难题，IBA-Bench提供了更贴近现实的评估方式，IBA-Agent则展示了通过检索和轨迹对齐改善个性化执行的有效路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models have enabled increasingly capable autonomous agents, yet personalization remains critical for making such agents practically useful. Recent benchmarks have begun evaluating personalization in agents, but they largely rely on static preference snapshots, fixed interaction logs, or question answering over predefined user profiles. Such designs fail to capture the complexity of evolving user preferences and neglect preference-conditioned task execution-a discrepancy we term as the knowledge-to-action gap. To address this challenge, we introduce IBA-Bench, a benchmark for implicit behavioral alignment constructed from longitudinal interaction histories that contain noise, implicit cues, and temporal inconsistencies. Unlike prior work, IBA-Bench evaluates whether an agent can execute tasks while satisfying implicit user constraints inferred from historical interactions. We further propose IBA-Agent, an agent framework that reconciles conflicting priorities through broad retrieval and trajectory-level alignment. Experiment results on IBA-Bench show that effective personalization remains a significant challenge for state-of-the-art LLM agents, and the proposed IBA-Agent substantially improves behavioral alignment in complex scenarios across nine application domains.

</details>


### [52] [PAC Approximation and DIRECT Optimization for Parametric Markov Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02184)
*Zhiming Chi,Ying Liu,Andrea Turrini,Lijun Zhang,David N. Jansen*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于场景方法（scenario approach）的PAC近似框架，用于高效合成参数马尔可夫决策过程（pMDPs）中PRCTL性质满足值的多项式近似，并结合统计模型检验处理黑盒模型；在此基础上集成DIRECT全局优化算法，给出条件最优性间隙保证，并通过2997个基准实验验证了优化组件的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确计算pMDPs中参数估值到性质满足值的有理函数f计算代价高昂，尤其当最优策略随参数变化时；现有方法难以在保证置信度的同时高效处理大规模参数空间，且缺乏对参数优化的有效支持。

Method: 采用场景方法：对参数配置进行采样并求解线性规划，得到具有PAC保证（误差界margin，置信度下除ε比例参数域外成立）的多项式近似；将该框架与统计模型检验结合以支持黑盒模型；基于近似函数，集成DIRECT（划分矩形）算法进行无导数全局优化，并在Lipschitz和PAC好集假设下推导条件最优性间隙界。

Result: 在2997个基准上的实验表明，DIRECT变体解决的实例数少于场景优化器，但在共同成功的实例上常获得略优的目标值且通常运行更快，结果与场景值之差保持在PAC误差界内。

Conclusion: 所提出的PAC近似与DIRECT优化组合为pMDPs参数综合与优化提供了高效且可保证置信度的方法，兼顾近似精度和优化性能，适合实际工程应用场景。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the parameter synthesis and optimization problem for parametric Markov decision processes (pMDPs), the extension of classical MDPs where exact probability values are replaced by parametric expressions. Computing the rational function $f_{\lsf}$ that maps parameter valuations to the satisfaction value of a PRCTL property $\lsf$ is a computationally expensive task, particularly for pMDPs where the optimal policy may vary across the parameter space. We adopt the \emph{scenario approach} to efficiently synthesize a probably approximately correct (PAC) approximation $\ApproxFunOfProperty{f}$ of $f_{\lsf}$: by sampling parameter configurations and solving a linear program, we obtain a polynomial approximation whose error margin $\margin$ is guaranteed, with prescribed confidence, for all but an $\errorRate$-fraction of the parameter domain under the sampling distribution. We further show how this PAC framework can be combined with statistical model checking (SMC), enabling the analysis of black-box parametric models. Building on the PAC approximation, we integrate the DIRECT (DIviding RECTangles) algorithm for derivative-free global optimization over the parameter space. We establish conditional optimality-gap guarantees: under explicit Lipschitz and PAC-good-set assumptions, the difference between the true optimum $f_{\lsf}(\parameters^{*})$ and the value found by DIRECT is bounded by a partition-diameter term and, in the PAC case, an additional approximation-error term. An empirical evaluation on 2997 benchmarks focuses on the new DIRECT-based optimization component. The results show that DIRECT variants solve fewer instances than the scenario optimizer, but on their common successful instances they often return slightly better objective values and usually run faster, while remaining close to the scenario values within the PAC margin.

</details>


### [53] [PosterMELD: Multi-Agent Paper-to-Poster Generation for Controllable Design Diversity with Editable Print-Ready Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02218)
*Haojie Hu,Chenhao Dang,Yaojia Liu,Hengrui Kang,Conghui He,Weijia Li*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: PosterMELD是一个基于模板的多智能体管线，用于将多模态论文转换为可编辑的PPTX/PNG海报，通过容量感知槽位、确定性门控与VLM审查实现高打印就绪率（81.3% PRR）和低生成成本（每请求0.38美元）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统仅对完整输出评分而隐藏请求级失败；直接图像生成不可编辑元素；编码智能体工作流成本高。

Method: 采用容量感知槽位指导写作，确定性门控与VLM审查将失败路由到有界修复；输出PPTX和PNG，并支持显式设计控制以生成同论文变体。

Abstract: Scientific poster construction compresses a long multimodal paper into a readable, editable canvas. Existing systems hide request-level failures by scoring only completed outputs; direct image generation is not element-editable, while coding-agent workflows are costly. PosterMELD is a template-conditioned multi-agent pipeline: capacity-aware slots guide writing before rendering, and deterministic gates plus vision-language model (VLM) review route failures to bounded repair. Each accepted request exports editable PowerPoint (PPTX) and Portable Network Graphics (PNG) artifacts; explicit design controls yield same-paper variants. Across 621 papers, Print-Ready Rate (PRR) counts requests passing geometric, readability, asset-integrity, and obvious-factual-error checks, with native editability reported separately. A frozen VLM assigns conditional Craftsmanship-Harmony-Expressiveness (CHE) scores to print-ready outputs. PosterMELD attains 81.3% PRR, 3.4 times P2P's rate and 5.2 times PosterGen's, and the highest conditional CHE among generated methods with multiple print-ready outputs. Native editability and explicit design controls are retained at a mean cost of USD 0.38 per request, 3.5% of Codex+Skill's. Code and resources are available at https://github.com/Shannon4Science/PosterMELD.

### [54] [Self-Certification of Representation Adequacy: Sequential Certification at Minimum Task Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02267)
*Zijie Huang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文针对智能体基于历史压缩表示行动时的结构风险，提出一个四层理论框架，从静态、顺序和边界三个层面系统定义并解决表示充分性的自我认证问题，包括一次性外部验证、最优停止认证策略及边界示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 构建四层理论：静态层通过贝叶斯风险分组恒等式定义决策论充分性，并以全变差阈值定价一次性外部验证；顺序层将认证建模为以任务损失为货币的最优停止问题，通过覆盖线性规划定义环境级认证复杂度常数，提出Certification Track-and-Stop策略并证明其下界；边界层给出核切换示例，指出固定核保证扩展到表示修订所需的未解定理。

Result: 证明了任意δ-正确策略的信息-任务损失下界，并给出Certification Track-and-Stop策略使其代价渐近匹配该下界；两个主要定理的完整证明在附录中提供。

Abstract: Agents that act on a compressed representation of their history face a structural risk: if the representation aliases histories with different optimal actions, no rule measurable with respect to the representation can avoid an irreducible per-round loss, and the agent may be unable to detect this from its own transcript. This paper develops a four-layer theory of self-certification of representation adequacy. The static layer defines decision-theoretic adequacy through a Bayes-risk grouping identity and prices a one-shot external verification by an exact total-variation threshold. The sequential layer poses certification as an optimal-stopping problem in the currency of task loss: we define an environment-wise certification complexity constant through a covering linear program, prove an information-task-loss lower bound for every delta-correct strategy, and give a Certification Track-and-Stop policy whose cost matches the bound asymptotically. A final boundary layer gives an explicit kernel-switching example and identifies the open theorem needed to cover policy switching or representation repair; it does not claim that the fixed-kernel guarantees extend to representation revision. The proofs of the two main theorems are given in full in the appendices.

</details>

*Victor Ojewale,Ro Encarnación,Suresh Venkatasubramanian,Danaé Metaxa*

TL;DR: 提出了MonitrLLM，一种将完整对话记录与用户自定义任务意图和结果评估联系起来的新型LLM评估基础设施，并通过26名大学生的两周试点研究验证了其价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法存在缺口：基准测试缺乏自然性，对话语料库缺乏用户反馈，界面反馈机制缺乏任务目的，无法将交互轨迹与用户定义的结果联系起来。

Abstract: Benchmark suites assess model capability on controlled tasks; large-scale conversation corpora capture naturalistic use without user feedback; and in-interface feedback mechanisms record satisfaction without task purpose. Together, they leave a critical gap in LLM evaluation: no existing infrastructure routinely links interaction trajectories to user-defined outcomes. We introduce MonitrLLM, open-source infrastructure for community-centered LLM evaluations that links full conversation transcripts to user-reported task intent and outcome assessments, treating all three as primary evaluative signals rather than optional metadata.
  To demonstrate the value of this approach, we conducted a two-week feasibility pilot with 26 college students using ChatGPT, collecting 206 evaluation reports with full conversation transcripts. The findings from our pilot demonstrate the value of connecting conversation trajectories with user-reported outcomes. For instance, despite reporting high average satisfaction (4.19/5) with their LLM interactions, participants also experience a substantial 23.1% failure rate on their goal tasks. We also find that multi-turn conversations are reported as failing at 2.5 times the rate of single-turn exchanges, a pattern that reframes extended interaction as a signal of difficulty rather than engagement. We conclude by discussing the value of incorporating direct user feedback with observational data for robust LLM evaluations, and the possibilities for infrastructure that enables this goal.

</details>


### [56] [Optimizing Minimax Regret in Uncertain MDPs with Small Sets of Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02509)
*Sterre Lutz,Daniël Vos,Matthijs T. J. Spaan,Anna Lukina*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 针对不确定马尔可夫决策过程（UMDP），提出k-自适应策略综合问题，在预生成有限k个策略的前提下，以最小化最大遗憾为目标，并给出精确求解算法KAPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的序贯决策常面临环境模型不确定性。为每个可能的MDP单独优化策略可能违反操作、监管或可解释性限制，而使用单一策略又会牺牲性能。当模型不确定性在执行前才被消除时，需要从预先准备的有限策略集中选择最合适的一个。

Method: 形式化k-自适应策略综合问题，证明其NP-hard；设计KAPS算法，采用嵌套分支定界框架，结合问题特定的边界与启发式规则，联合优化哪些MDP共享策略以及策略本身。

Result: 在多个UMDP基准实验上，从1个策略增加到2个策略时遗憾值下降最显著；单策略场景下，KAPS在解质量上与现有方法相当，且能更频繁地证明最优性。

Conclusion: KAPS提供了一种可证明最优的精确求解方法，能有效处理有限策略集下的鲁棒决策问题，且实验显示少量策略即可显著降低最大遗憾，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: Sequential decision-making in real-world applications often involves uncertainty about the environment's model. Uncertain Markov decision processes (UMDPs) represent the possible environments as a set of MDPs with shared states and actions but potentially different transition probabilities and rewards. Optimizing a single policy across all possible MDPs may sacrifice performance, while preparing an individually optimized policy for every MDP may violate operational, regulatory, or interpretability constraints on the number of policies that can be prepared and deployed. We consider settings in which model uncertainty is resolved shortly before execution, allowing the most suitable policy to be selected from a limited set prepared in advance. We introduce $k$-adaptable policy synthesis, which optimizes such a set of $k$ policies under a minimax-regret objective. We prove that the problem is NP-hard and develop KAPS, an exact nested branch-and-bound algorithm with problem-specific bounds and heuristics. KAPS jointly optimizes which MDPs share a policy and the policies themselves. Experiments across various UMDP benchmarks show that the largest reduction in regret consistently occurs when increasing from one to two policies. In the single-policy setting, KAPS is competitive with existing methods in solution quality and proves optimality substantially more often.

</details>


### [57] [AtumAI: A Principled Framework for Agentic Generation of Datacenter Control-Plane Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02569)
*Qiushi Lin,Chaojie Zhang,Íñigo Goiri,Aditya Akella,Ricardo Bianchini,Jovan Stojkovic*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出了AtumAI框架，将智能体AI应用于数据中心控制面策略生成，通过形式化任务编译和进化式设计发现循环，实现了形式化、可迁移和系统化的策略搜索，在多个任务上超越专家基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心控制面策略设计难度大且耗时，现有智能体AI缺乏形式化、可迁移性和系统性，导致搜索效率低且易陷入局部最优。

Method: AtumAI包含两个核心组件：数据中心任务编译器（将自然语言目标自动编译为形式化、可搜索的规范）和进化式设计发现循环（结合扩散模型、进化算法和代理模型扩展搜索空间）。

Result: 在负载放置、资源扩展和功耗管理三类控制面任务中，AtumAI生成的策略一致优于专家设计的基线。

Conclusion: AtumAI通过形式化任务表述和智能搜索机制，显著降低了新任务的上手成本，并将策略设计从数月的工程工作简化为描述编写，展示了智能体AI在系统策略生成中的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: The efficiency of a datacenter rests on its control plane policies. Designing these policies is increasingly hard: the hardware-software stack grows fast, the design space is vast and interdependent, and prototyping a single policy takes months. Agentic AI promises to automate this search. Off the shelf, however, it falls short on three fronts. It is not formal: with no structured, searchable statement of the problem, the search has little structure to exploit and hard constraints are not guaranteed. It is not transferable: each task is solved from scratch, so nothing learned on one task carries to the next. Finally, it is not systematic: relying on the LLM as the sole source of candidates, it explores a narrow slice of the design space and settles into local optima. We introduce AtumAI, a framework that generates datacenter control-plane policies with agentic AI, making the process formal, transferable, and systematic. From a goal stated in plain language, AtumAI autonomously proposes, tests, and refines candidate policies until one satisfies the request. It does so through two components. The Datacenter Task Compiler automates problem formulation: it compiles the request into a formal, machine-checkable, and searchable specification of the task's objectives, constraints, decision variables, and evaluation methodology. The Evolutionary Design Discovery Loop then searches this specification, expanding the search beyond the LLM itself via a diffusion model, an evolutionary algorithm, and a surrogate model. Together, they reduce onboarding a new task from months of engineering to writing its description. We evaluate AtumAI on three control-plane tasks with distinct problem scopes, design spaces, and trade-offs: workload placement, resource scaling, and power management. Across all tasks, the policies generated by AtumAI consistently outperform expert-engineered baselines.

</details>

### [58] [AutoFOAM: The Self-Refining Autonomous OpenFOAM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00003)
*Arun Govind Neelan,A Seshaditya*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: AutoFOAM是一个基于大型语言模型的自进化智能体，能根据自然语言指令自动创建、评估、运行和进化OpenFOAM仿真，降低CFD使用门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源CFD求解器（如OpenFOAM）需要大量专业知识和繁琐的配置文件设置，限制了其快速原型设计和广泛使用。

Method: 基于Qwen-coder 2.5-14B微调，使用252个文本提示覆盖7个求解器、13个参数化网格模板和y+数值策略；核心为7阶段进化循环，并采用RAG增强重试、词典级修补和提示多样 paraphrasing 三种防退化机制。

Result: 能够自动完成OpenFOAM仿真全流程，加速快速原型设计，并有效防止自训练中的模型退化。

Conclusion: AutoFOAM通过生成式AI与严格流场仿真的结合，降低了CFD高级工作流的使用门槛，推动其民主化。

Abstract: Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) plays an important role in modern engineering, but using open-source solvers such as OpenFOAM requires considerable knowledge and skills, as well as time-consuming configuration file setup. To reduce this burden, we propose AutoFOAM - a self-evolving large language model (LLM) agent that creates, evaluates, runs, and evolves its own OpenFOAM simulations based solely on natural-language instructions. Our model is pre-trained on the Qwen-coder 2.5-14B, which is then fine-tuned on 252 text prompts targeting 7 OpenFOAM solvers, 13 parametrized mesh templates, and a y plus-aware numerical policy. The crucial element of the algorithm is a sophisticated evolution loop composed of 7 stages. To prevent model degeneration under repeated self-training, the agent employs three complementary anti-collapse streams: RAG-augmented retry context, surgical dictionary-level patching, and prompt-diversity paraphrasing. By bridging generative artificial intelligence with rigorous fluid simulations, AutoFOAM accelerates rapid prototyping and democratizes advanced CFD workflows.

</details>


### [59] [Progressive$^2$: A Teacher-Student Progressive Co-Evolving Knowledge Distillation Method for Substantial Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00129)
*Tiancong Cheng,Ying Zhang,Zhiwen Yu,Yifang Yin,Bin Guo*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出渐进式知识蒸馏框架Progressive²，通过教师网络逐步增强和学生网络逐步缩小的双向渐进策略，解决师生能力差距大时蒸馏性能下降的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识蒸馏在师生模型能力差距过大时性能显著下降，难以满足服务器端模型压缩到客户端需求的场景。

Method: 教师侧按从原始到丰富的语义顺序渐进式选择更多层参与蒸馏，并设计多特征融合适配器（基于Lipschitz连续性理论保证训练稳定性）；学生侧逐步缩小网络规模，与教师形成迭代协同进化。

Result: 渐进策略可独立部署以平衡精度与训练效率，师生联合训练可进一步提升整体性能。

Conclusion: Progressive²作为灵活框架，能有效缓解能力差距导致的蒸馏退化问题，实现更好的压缩效果。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) is a widely utilized technique for transferring knowledge from a large model (the teacher) to a smaller model (the student). Owing to its flexibility and broad applicability, KD has been extensively applied in the compression of server-side models to meet the Quality of Service (QoS) requirements of client users. Despite significant advancements, the performance of distillation is substantially compromised when a large disparity exists between the capabilities of the server and the requirements of the client. To alleviate this problem, we propose a novel distillation approach, named Progressive$^2$, which operates through the combination of a progressively stronger teacher and a progressively smaller student. On the side of the teacher, rather than involving all layers simultaneously, we progressively select additional layers for distillation following a raw-to-rich semantic progression, establishing a systematic learning curriculum. Furthermore, we design a teacher-side multi-feature fusion adapter for the teacher to improve training stability, which is theoretically supported by the framework of Lipschitz continuity. On the side of the student, rather than directly training a tiny model, we gradually reduce the size of the network to facilitate an iterative co-evolution with the teacher. Progressive$^2$ serves as a flexible framework; the progressive strategy of the teacher can be deployed independently to achieve an optimal balance between accuracy and training efficiency, while the joint integration of the teacher and the student yields further improvements in overall performance.

</details>


### [60] [Rethinking Pretraining for Specialized Design Data: Evidence from the JONES-19 Cultural Design Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00135)
*Alexandros Haridis,Charles Zhou*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 在JONES-19设计数据集上，从头训练CNN并辅以多尺度裁剪可接近ImageNet预训练的性能，表明专业设计领域中小型高质量数据集比大规模通用预训练更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计档案以图形格式编码专家知识，为设计启发的机器学习提供了区别于典型计算机视觉基准的测试平台，但现有研究缺乏对训练策略的系统评估。

Method: 在JONES-19数据集上比较两种训练策略：ImageNet预训练（通用视觉先验）与从头训练，后者结合重复局部采样（多裁剪）增强。

Result: 通用先验提升判别性能，但结合多裁剪的从头训练能有效恢复这些增益；局部设计驱动的表征对高度结构化数据足够有效。

Conclusion: 在专业设计领域，精心策展的小型高质量数据集可能比大规模数据收集更有用，且更能揭示设计领域的本质特征。

Abstract: Design and architectural archives encode expert human knowledge in graphical formats, providing a critical testbed for design-inspired Machine Learning (ML) challenges absent with typical computer vision benchmarks. Building on JONES-19, a small-size image dataset based on The Grammar of Ornament (London, 1857), we evaluate the discriminative performance of Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) in two model training strategies: (a) ImageNet pretraining for domain-general "visual common sense," and (b) learning from scratch on the design data in JONES-19. We find that while domain-general priors improve discriminative performance, learning from scratch augmented with repeated local sampling (multi-crop) effectively recovers these gains. For highly structured design data, local design-driven representations provide sufficient foundation for learning, challenging a reliance on massive general-purpose pretraining. These findings suggest that in specialized design domains, careful curation of smaller high-quality datasets that capture empirical and formal design principles may prove more effective and informative on the nature of a particular design domain than prioritizing large-scale data collection.

</details>


### [61] [Response Magnitude as a Dominant Signal for Held-Out CRISPRi Perturbation Effect Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00152)
*Mehrdad Shoeibi,Niloofar Yousefi*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 在严格保留靶基因的设定下，CRISPRi扰动幅度预测的主要可解释信号可归结为四个确定性标量函数；线性回归与随机森林优于深度编码器，且幅度信号可跨细胞类型零样本迁移，但需注意评估终点与靶基因效应的语义差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明简单基线常媲美或超过深度扰动预测模型，但机制不明。本文旨在VCC基准上严格拆分靶基因，揭示驱动性能差距的低维信号，并检验其跨细胞类型的迁移性，以指导模型设计。

Method: 以非靶向对照的log Anderson-Darling距离为靶点，从2000维输入中提取四个确定性标量幅度因子；对比深度MLP编码器、线性回归、随机森林，并通过两个预设对照分离行对齐与维度贡献；利用两个外部CRISPRi筛选进行零样本迁移，重建单细胞靶基因终点评估。

Result: 四个标量即可强预测幅度；深度编码器塌缩至边际均值，标准补救无效；四标量线性回归超越最优x-only经典模型，随机森林进一步超越深度编码器；迁移中幅度预测正向，表达预测为负或不可解析；暴露幅度于编码器可提升迁移，但仍不敌四标量线性回归；Anderson-Darling列度量转录组响应广度而非靶基因效应强度。

Abstract: Predicting the magnitude of a CRISPRi perturbation's transcriptomic effect on held-out target genes is an important open problem in single-cell biology. Recent work has documented that simple baselines often match or exceed deep perturbation predictors on related protocols. We study this phenomenon on the Virtual Cell Challenge (VCC) benchmark under a strict held-out target-gene split, identify the specific low-dimensional signal that drives the gap, and characterize how it transfers across cell types. The target is the log Anderson-Darling distance from non-targeting controls, which is strongly predictable from four deterministic scalar functions of the 2,000-dimensional input. A deep MLP encoder with direct access to the full input collapses toward the marginal training mean, and standard remedies do not close the gap. A linear regression on the four magnitude scalars alone exceeds the strongest x-only classical model, while a Random Forest on the input plus the four scalars substantially outperforms our deep proof-of-concept encoder. Two pre-specified controls attribute the magnitude gain to per-row alignment rather than added dimensionality. Under zero-shot transfer to two external CRISPRi screens evaluated against a target-gene endpoint rebuilt from single-cell data, magnitude-only predictors transfer positively whereas expression-only predictors are negative or unresolved. Exposing magnitude to the deep encoder improves transfer over its expression-only counterpart, yet the encoder does not outperform a four-scalar linear regression on the same features. We also find that the Anderson-Darling column distributed with these screens measures transcriptome-wide response breadth rather than target-gene effect strength, so evaluating transfer against it scores a different outcome.

</details>


### [62] [AutoCause: A Python framework that automates expert decisions in environmental time-series causal discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00198)
*Marco Ruiz,Miguel Arana-Catania,David R. Ardila,Rodrigo Ventura*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出AutoCause开源Python工作流，通过记录决策、提供默认审计规则并支持专家覆盖，将环境时间序列因果发现过程标准化，实现可复现和可审计的分析；在145个数据集上评估，多数投票支持的链接在合成基准上精度更高，但河流拓扑数据上未显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境时间序列因果发现中，专家在方法选择、独立性检验、滞后范围、样本量、多重检验控制和结果解释上的主观决策不一致，导致图形不可比较、不可复现和不可审计。

Method: AutoCause以Python工作流整合四个因果发现方法（分属三个类别），外加非因果参考模型；记录每个决策，从扩展的因果审计模块生成默认值，允许领域知识覆盖；通过方法计数支持度对链接进行分级。

Result: 在来自DGP-Atlas、TimeGraph和CausalRivers的145个数据集上，不同方法恢复参考图的互补部分；多数方法支持的链接在合成基准上比单一方法链接更精确，但在河流拓扑对比中不占优势。

Conclusion: AutoCause将不一致的专家实践转化为可审计、可重复的分析流程，而因果解释仍由分析师负责；代码开源可用。

Abstract: Environmental time-series causal discovery requires expert decisions about method choice, conditional-independence tests, lag horizons, sample-size adequacy, multiple-testing control, and evidence interpretation. Applied inconsistently across datasets, these choices yield graphs that cannot be compared, reproduced, or audited. We present AutoCause, an open-source Python workflow that records each decision, derives defaults from an extended causal-audit module, and admits domain-informed overrides. The workflow wraps four established causal-discovery methods from three families, adds non-causal reference models, and grades links by method-count support. On 145 datasets from DGP-Atlas, TimeGraph, and a topology-derived CausalRivers reference, the methods recover complementary parts of the reference graphs. Majority-supported links are more precise than single-method links on the synthetic benchmarks but not against river topology. AutoCause converts inconsistent expert practice into an auditable, repeatable analysis; causal interpretation remains with the analyst. Available at https://github.com/marcoruizrueda/autocause.

</details>


### [63] [A Physics-Chemistry-Informed Neural Network (PCINN) for Real-Time Spatial-ALD Coverage Prediction and Reliable Kinetics Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00212)
*Ning Hu,Chang Liu,Yunlei Jiang,Yuan Dong*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出物理-化学信息神经网络(PCINN)作为空间ALD的混合代理模型，实现CFD级精度(测试R²_log=0.998)与实时速度(单次查询约7ms，比CFD快5×10⁴倍)，仅需30个训练样本覆盖四个数量级的覆盖率范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间ALD的工艺窗口扫描受限于高保真CFD计算成本过高，而解析模型无法捕捉气帘等输运调制效应，亟需兼具精度与速度的代理模型。

Method: PCINN采用非黑箱架构：小型神经网络仅学习操作条件到近壁浓度的映射，已知表面动力学作为硬编码可训练化学层沿基底轨迹积分；通过单标量瓶颈保证稀疏数据下的准确性、可解释性与可逆性，并集成Fisher信息与剖面似然的完整可辨识性分析。

Result: 吸附能E_ads和解吸速率k_des可稳健辨识；k_ads在单温度下不可独立辨识(仅k_ads*c_wall可辨)。四温度下指前因子nu与E_ads沿斜率0.065 eV/decade的弱可辨识退化谷耦合，此斜率解析为k_B T_eff ln(10)，并作为可靠性诊断指标——七化学失配矩阵验证其对任意单一Arrhenius失配不变，仅当第二热激活过程出现时才偏移，故斜率偏离指示未建模的位点异质性。

Conclusion: 数据来自模拟且逆过程采用相同动力学形式，验证了流程自洽性与可辨识性边界，而非真实工艺参数；PCINN为空间ALD提供实时高保真代理与内在可靠性诊断工具。

Abstract: Spatial atomic layer deposition (SALD) is a leading atmospheric-pressure, high-throughput route to industrial ALD, but design and control are limited by the cost of predicting surface coverage: high-fidelity CFD is far too slow for operating-window scans, while analytic models miss transport modulation such as the gas curtain. We present a physics-chemistry-informed neural network (PCINN), a hybrid surrogate with CFD-level accuracy at real-time speed: a query returns coverage in about 7 ms, roughly 5x10^4 times faster than a CFD solve, reaching a test R^2_log = 0.998 (leave-one-out R^2_raw = 0.974) from only 30 training cases spanning four orders of magnitude in coverage.
  The architecture is not a black box: a small network learns only the operating-condition to near-wall concentration closure, while the known surface kinetics is a hard-coded, trainable chemistry layer integrated along the substrate trajectory. This single-scalar bottleneck keeps it accurate under sparse data, interpretable and invertible.
  We add a full identifiability analysis (Fisher information, profile likelihood). The adsorption energy E_ads and desorption rate k_des are robustly identifiable; k_ads is not separately identifiable at a single temperature (only k_ads*c_wall is). Across four temperatures the prefactor nu and E_ads bind along a weakly identifiable degeneracy valley of slope 0.065 eV/decade, derived analytically as k_B T_eff ln(10) and turned into a reliability diagnostic: a seven-chemistry mismatch matrix shows it is invariant under any single-Arrhenius mismatch and shifts only when a second thermally activated process appears, so a slope departure flags unmodelled site heterogeneity.
  Data come from simulation with known ground truth inverted by the same kinetic form, so the study verifies pipeline self-consistency and the identifiability boundary, not real parameters.

</details>


### [64] [Similarity-Aware Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00246)
*Madhavan Citalamangalam Kumaran,Midhun Parakkal Unni,Vicky Kouni,Haripriya Harikumar*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出一种保留感知的定位方法，同时考虑参数对遗忘数据和保留数据的重要性，并引入保留相似评估集，在CIFAR-10和ResNet18上降低附带损害并提升卸载指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于定位的机器卸载方法仅依据遗忘集重要性选择参数，忽略了参数对保留数据的作用，导致语义相似的保留样本受损。

Method: 设计保留感知的定位方法，联合评估参数对遗忘集和保留集的重要性；构建基于嵌入空间余弦相似度的保留相似评估集来衡量附带损害。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和ResNet18的11种实验设置下，该方法持续降低附带损害，同时改进标准卸载指标。

Conclusion: 保留感知定位能有效实现相似性感知的机器卸载，兼顾卸载效果与保留数据完整性。

Abstract: Machine unlearning removes the influence of user-specified training examples from a trained model, avoiding the need to retrain it from scratch. Localization-based methods improve unlearning efficiency by identifying a subset of influential model parameters. However, existing approaches select parameters based solely on forget-set importance, neglecting their role in retained dataset and often causing collateral damage to semantically similar retained examples. We address this limitation with a retain-aware localization method that considers parameter importance to both forgotten and retained data. We also introduce a retain-similar evaluation set, constructed using cosine similarity in the model embedding space, to directly measure collateral damage. Across eleven experimental settings on CIFAR-10 dataset and ResNet18 model, our method consistently reduces collateral damage while improving standard unlearning metrics, demonstrating the effectiveness of retain-aware localization for similarity-aware machine unlearning.

</details>


### [65] [More Debate, Same Evidence: Structural Limits of Homogeneous Multi-Agent Groundedness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00243)
*Yuelyu Ji*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文测试了多智能体LLM评审团在事实核查任务中的效果，发现相比单智能体，其准确率变化从+8.5到-4.4个百分点不等，其中两个数据集有可靠提升、一个可靠下降、三个结果不确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究假设多智能体评审团通过交换批评能提升判断质量，但这一假设在事实核查（groundedness verification）场景中缺乏系统验证。

Method: 使用同质三智能体评审团，在六个公开事实核查和幻觉检测基准上评估，并以固定单智能体为参考，比较系统级准确率差异。

Result: 准确率差异范围为-4.4到+8.5个百分点；两个数据集有增益、一个损失、三个结果在统计上不显著。

Conclusion: 多智能体评审团的效果依赖具体数据集，并非普遍优于单智能体；且差异反映整体系统性能，无法单独归因于辩论机制。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) judges are increasingly organized as multi-agent panels under the assumption that exchanging critiques improves judgment quality. We test this assumption for \emph{groundedness verification}, where a judge must determine whether a claim is supported by the supplied evidence. We evaluate a homogeneous three-agent panel on six public fact-verification and hallucination-detection benchmarks. Relative to a fixed single-agent reference, the panel's system-level accuracy difference ranges from $+8.5$ to $-4.4$ percentage points: two datasets show reliable gains, one shows a reliable loss, and three are statistically inconclusive. Because the reference and panel use different model variants, these differences characterize the complete systems rather than isolate a causal debate effect.

</details>


### [66] [Geometric Self-Supervised Pre-training for Neural Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00270)
*David Aguado,Daniel Fuertes,Carlos R. del-Blanco,Fernando Jaureguizar*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出一种针对路由问题的几何自监督预训练框架，通过等距变换学习空间不变性和全局相对距离分布，显著提升模型在超大规模TSP上的零样本泛化能力，并实现较精确求解器两个数量级的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经组合优化（NCO）在解决TSP等路由问题时效率高，但强化学习模型向高维实例泛化能力差；受计算机视觉和自然语言处理中自监督预训练成功的启发，作者尝试将其应用于路由图，但路由图仅含2D坐标，缺乏复杂拓扑属性，因此需专门设计预训练方法。

Method: 设计几何自监督预训练框架，对路由图施加旋转和轴向反射等等距变换，使模型在策略优化前学习鲁棒的结构表征，捕获空间不变性和全局相对距离分布。

Result: 相比从零训练的基线，该策略在TSP1,000零样本外推场景中旅行长度改进7.23%，且计算效率极高，在超大规模下相比精确求解器Concorde提速达两个数量级。

Conclusion: 几何自监督预训练能有效提升NCO模型对大规模TSP的泛化性，兼具性能与效率优势，代码和预训练模型已公开。

Abstract: Neural Combinatorial Optimization (NCO) techniques have emerged as a highly efficient alternative to traditional exact algorithms for solving routing problems such as the Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP). However, the generalization capabilities of these Reinforcement Learning-based models are severely hindered when scaling to high-dimensional instances. This issue has been mitigated in other domains, like computer vision and natural language processing, by adopting a self-supervised pre-training strategy. Nevertheless, its application to routing graphs, which lack complex topological attributes beyond 2D spatial coordinates, remains a challenge. In this paper, we propose a geometric self-supervised pre-training framework specifically designed to capture spatial invariance and global relative distance distributions. By applying isometric transformations, such as rotations and axial reflections, the model learns robust structural representations prior to the policy optimization phase. Empirical results demonstrate that this strategy consistently outperforms models trained from scratch (baselines), achieving a 7.23\% improvement in tour length for massive zero-shot extrapolation scenarios (TSP1,000). Furthermore, the proposed model exhibits remarkable computational efficiency, delivering speedups of up to two orders of magnitude over the exact solver Concorde at massive scales.
  The source code and pre-trained models are publicly available at https://github.com/davidaguadocosano/TSP-GeoPretrain.git.

</details>


### [67] [Trust and Its Betrayal under Three Representational Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00321)
*Mihnea C. Moldoveanu,Joel A. C. Baum*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文将信任建模为虚拟认知状态，提出三种形式化表示，并分析共同信任的不可能性与背叛的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信任不同于预测，其失望（背叛）具有质的差异，现有模型未充分刻画这一特征。

Method: 将信任定义为虚拟条件句（能力与诚实），分别用词典序假设、刘易斯-斯塔尔纳克球系序数接近、条件概率系统中的强信念三种表示建模，并考察共同信任的固定点。

Result: 三种表示均表明有限深度共同信任可实现，但无限完成态存在困难；背叛在三种表示中分别导致层级序失效、接近序受污染、条件假设转移但信念结构保持连贯。

Conclusion: 共同信任的不可实现性与表达方式相关，且背叛的影响机制因模型而异，强信念表示显示出更强的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Trust is a propositional attitude of a distinctive kind: to trust is to rely on another under conditions where reliance could be disappointed, and the disappointment of trust---betrayal---differs qualitatively from the disappointment of a prediction. We treat trust as a \emph{subjunctive} epistemic state: $A$ trusts $B$'s competence when $A$ believes that \emph{were $P$ true, $B$ would know it}, and $B$'s integrity when $A$ believes that \emph{were $B$ to know $P$, he would disclose it to $A$}. We develop three representations of this state---as lexicographic \emph{assumption} as \emph{ordinal closeness} in a Lewis--Stalnaker sphere system , and as \emph{strong belief} in a conditional probability system and for each we ask whether the Brandenburger--Keisler impossibility on common belief survives when the assumption of rationality is replaced by an assumption of trustworthiness. The three representations agree that every \emph{finite} depth of common trust is realizable while the \emph{completed} common-trust fixed point is the locus of difficulty, but they differ sharply in \emph{how} the difficulty manifests, and---our organizing finding---in how each survives a concrete betrayal. W show that the same betrayal refutes an agent's \emph{level ordering} under the lexicographic representation, contaminates her \emph{closeness ordering} in proportion to the betrayer's deliberateness under the ordinal representation, and merely \emph{shifts her operative conditioning hypothesis} while leaving her belief structure coherent under the strong-belief representation.

</details>


### [68] [Learning the Pareto Frontier of Predictive Models under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00632)
*Yiming Dong,Jiwei Zhao,Yang Young Lu*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出Frontier Learning框架，通过拼接白盒模型内部表示和黑盒模型预测输出，训练轻量级监督模型，在分布偏移下确保性能不低于任何单一策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练模型在分布偏移下，零样本、微调和直接训练等策略各有优劣，没有统一最优方案，需要一种能综合利用多种模型互补信息的框架。

Method: 将候选模型（白盒与黑盒）视为互补信息源，拼接其内部表示或预测输出作为统一特征，再在带标签目标数据上拟合带正则化的轻量级监督学习器；该假设类包含零样本、微调和直接训练为特例，保证经验风险不劣于任何基线。

Result: 在模拟实验及DomainNet/VisDA视觉域适应、MIMIC-IV-Notes临床死亡预测中，Frontier Learning匹配或超越最强单一策略，且在无单一基线可靠时增益最大。

Conclusion: Frontier Learning是一种稳健的模型复用框架，能有效应对分布偏移，充分利用不同访问权限模型的互补优势。

Abstract: Modern machine learning pipelines increasingly rely on reusing pretrained and foundation models across downstream tasks. These pretrained models can differ not only in performance but also in how they can be used: some only provide black-box predictions, while others may permit white-box access to internal representations that can be probed or fine-tuned. When deployed to the target domain in the presence of distribution shift, no single strategy, including zero-shot application, fine-tuning, or directly training a target-specific model, is uniformly the best.
  In this work, we propose Frontier Learning, a framework that treats a library of candidate models spanning different training histories and access regimes as complementary sources of information rather than mutually exclusive alternatives. Frontier Learning constructs a unified target-domain feature by concatenating internal representations from white-box candidates as well as prediction outputs from black-box candidates, then fits a lightweight, regularized supervised learner on this concatenated representation using labeled target data. Because the resulting hypothesis class contains predictors obtained by zero-shot reuse, fine-tuning, and direct training as special cases, empirical risk minimization over the frontier learner is guaranteed to be no worse, on the training sample, than any individual baseline.
  We evaluate the framework in simulations spanning varying degrees of source-target compatibility and in two real-world distribution-shift settings: visual domain adaptation on DomainNet/VisDA and clinical mortality prediction across intensive care unit domains using MIMIC-IV-Notes. Across all settings, Frontier Learning matches or outperforms the strongest individual reuse strategy, with the largest gains arising precisely when no single baseline is reliable across the range of shift considered.

</details>


### [69] [Adaptive Quantum Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Differential Equations with Applications to Fluid Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00850)
*Fabio Pereira dos Santos,Renato Portugal,Júlio de Castro Vargas Fernandes,Lucas Timotheo Sanches*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出一种混合量子-经典框架，通过自适应配点采样和损失感知注意力机制增强量子物理信息神经网络（QPINNs），在基准流体和反应扩散系统中实现至少60%的精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PINNs在高维或多尺度系统中存在精度与效率瓶颈，且量子PINNs常被认为受限于量子电路表达能力，但实际中优化问题同样关键，需同时解决谱偏差和优化瓶颈。

Method: 采用动态优先采样高残差或陡梯度区域的配点，结合可训练的损失加权方案平衡物理残差、边界条件和数据保真度，并集成变分量子电路与量子梯度估计技术。

Result: 在多种微分方程测试中，该方法显著缓解谱偏差，针对基准流体流动和反应扩散系统，在特定条件下解精度提升至少60%。

Conclusion: 单纯增加模型表达能力不足以解决复杂PDE，QPINNs仍受经典PINNs的结构优化限制；所提框架为量子增强科学机器学习提供可扩展路径，但需进一步改进优化策略。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have emerged as a versatile approach for solving nonlinear partial differential equations (PDEs), yet achieving high accuracy efficiently using these techniques remains challenging for high-dimensional or multiscale systems. Here, we present a hybrid quantum-classical framework that enhances Quantum PINNs (QPINNs) through adaptive collocation point sampling and loss-aware attention mechanisms. By dynamically prioritizing points in regions with large PDE residuals or steep solution gradients, our method mitigates the spectral bias inherent in conventional PINNs. Current Quantum Physics-Informed Neural Networks are commonly assumed to be limited by the expressive power of quantum circuits. In our work, we observed that, across diverse differential equations, optimization - not only expressivity - can be an important bottleneck. Furthermore, a trainable loss-weighting scheme balances contributions from physics residuals, boundary conditions, and data fidelity during training. Integrating these strategies with quantum computing techniques (including variational quantum circuits and quantum gradient estimation) can yield at least a 60% improvement in solution accuracy under specific regimes for benchmark fluid flows and reaction-diffusion systems. Finally, we argue that merely increasing model expressivity is insufficient for resolving complex PDEs via QPINNs, as they remain constrained by the structural optimization limitations of classical PINNs. This framework provides a scalable pathway for quantum-enhanced scientific machine learning, bridging physics-based modeling with emerging quantum computational capabilities.

</details>


### [70] [TaPR: Test-Aware Policy Refinement for Feedback-Conditioned Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00494)
*Aofan Liu,Jingxiang Meng,Fangxin Liu,Yongbiao Chen*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出Test-aware Policy Refinement (TaPR)框架，将执行反馈转化为密集的每轮测试通过率奖励，以提升多轮代码智能体的自我修复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习范式主要使用单轮结果奖励进行评估和优化，这混淆了初始代码生成与反馈驱动的修复能力，忽略了中间轮次的细粒度执行信号，且无法有效评估策略是否真正获得了自我修复能力。

Method: TaPR框架在多轮交互协议下，将执行反馈转化为密集的每轮测试通过率奖励，使奖励信号在每一轮都能提供有效的梯度，从而优化策略的多轮修复能力。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench的219个代码生成问题上，六种模型的池化三轮成功率(Pass@3)提升2.44个百分点；在7B/8B高余量切片上，池化准确率从30.25%提升至33.56%（+3.31个百分点），配对试验中有42次改进和13次回退；消融实验显示密集奖励在前十步均提供非零反馈，且在Hard子集上达到更高的峰值。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出奖励分解框架和轮次感知评估协议，将首次生成质量与多轮修复能力解耦，从而更准确地评估和提升智能体的自我修复能力。

Abstract: Multi-turn code agents rely on execution feedback to repair incorrect programs, yet standard reinforcement learning paradigms optimize and evaluate policy performance primarily using single-shot outcome rewards. This misalignment conflates initial code generation with feedback-driven refinement, discards granular execution signals across intermediate turns, and fails to evaluate whether the policy actually acquires self-repair capabilities. We propose Test-aware Policy Refinement (TaPR), a framework that transforms execution feedback into a dense per-turn test-pass-ratio reward under a consistent multi-turn interaction protocol. Across six models on 219 code-generation problems from LiveCodeBench, TaPR improves the pooled three-turn success rate (Pass@3) by 2.44 percentage points. In the predefined 7B/8B high-headroom slice, pooled accuracy increases from 30.25% to 33.56% (+3.31 pp), with 42 improvements and 13 regressions in paired trials. On a matched Qwen3-8B ablation, the dense reward supplies nonzero feedback in all of the first ten steps and reaches a higher Hard-subset peak than outcome-only GRPO within the tested budget, although GRPO nearly matches pooled Pass@3 by step 300. Our primary contribution is a reward-decomposition framework and a turn-aware evaluation protocol that decouple first-shot generation quality from multi-turn repair competence.

</details>


### [71] [Through the LENS: Local Geometric Decomposition of Vision-Language Model Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00561)
*Shalom Kachko,Raz Lapid,Margarita Vald,Almog Dubin,Moshe Sipper*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出LENS方法，用混合因子分析器将VLM激活分解为局部低秩高斯邻域，揭示LLaVA和Qwen3-VL的深度依赖融合轨迹，且局部邻域可作为可解释和因果干预单元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性方法多识别全局线性方向，无法捕捉全局高维但局部低维的跨模态交互结构，需引入局部几何分析工具。

Method: 采用混合因子分析器（MFA）对VLM激活建模为多个局部低秩高斯邻域，结合自动多模态标注流水线为邻域生成语义描述，并通过插值、系数空间检索进行因果验证。

Result: 在LLaVA和Qwen3-VL上发现不同融合模式；MFA干预在多数条件下优于差分均值和VL-SAE（最佳达5.7倍）；检索R@1从14.9%提升至48.6%；消融实验证明轨迹稳定性。

Conclusion: 局部几何邻域是分析跨模态表征的有效可解释与因果单元，支持对VLM融合机制的新理解。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) process image patches and text tokens in a shared residual stream, but the local geometry through which the two modalities interact remains poorly understood. Most interpretability methods identify global linear directions, which may miss representations that are globally high-dimensional but locally low-dimensional. We introduce LENS (Local Explanation of Neighborhood Subspaces), a method that decomposes VLM activations into local low-rank Gaussian neighborhoods using a Mixture of Factor Analyzers. Applied to LLaVA-1.5-7B and Qwen3-VL-8B, LENS reveals distinct depth-dependent fusion trajectories consistent with each model's fusion mechanism: LLaVA progressively mixes modalities at later layers, whereas Qwen3-VL mixes them early, partially re-segregates them, and recombines them near the output. An automated multimodal labeling pipeline assigns concise semantic descriptions to these neighborhoods. Interpolating activations toward neighborhood centroids causally redirects generation within and across modalities and outperforms difference-in-means and VL-SAE in most evaluated conditions; in one LLaVA vision-to-vision setting, MFA achieves 5.7 times the VL-SAE score. Human evaluation finds MFA steering competitive with prompting and substantially stronger than the other intervention baselines. Finally, the MFA coefficient space improves Qwen3-VL image-to-rendered-text retrieval at the deepest evaluated layer from 14.9% to 48.6% R@1. Ablations show that the reported fusion trajectories are stable across component counts, local ranks, and modality-purity thresholds. These results support local geometric neighborhoods as useful interpretable and causal units for analyzing cross-modal representations in the evaluated VLMs.

</details>


### [72] [Data-Driven Pinball-Loss Selection for Vertically Distributed Elastic-Net SVMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00949)
*Xiaofei Wu,Kai Qi,Rongmei Liang*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出一种数据驱动的弹性网支持向量机，通过学习单纯形约束的权重组合多个候选pinball损失，等效于具有数据相关参数的单pinball损失，并给出理论保证与高效求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统pinball损失SVM的对称性参数需预先固定，缺乏数据适应性，影响鲁棒性与泛化性能。

Method: 将候选pinball损失加权求和，权重受单纯形约束并通过弹性网正则化学习；推导经验Oracle不等式；设计列分区变量分裂求解算法，支持高维数据并行训练。

Result: 全局最小化器目标不超过最优固定候选（正则项消失时），否则有显式上界；求解器以O(1/T)平方步长残差收敛，且列分区与集中训练在精确算术下迭代和结果一致。

Conclusion: 方法在预测性能、数值等价性和多进程可扩展性上得到实验验证，兼顾鲁棒性与数据自适应性。

Abstract: The pinball-loss support vector machine is robust, but its asymmetry parameter is usually fixed in advance. We propose a data-driven elastic-net support vector machine that learns simplex-constrained weights over candidate pinball losses while retaining one classifier. The weighted loss is equivalent to a pinball loss with a data-dependent effective parameter. An empirical oracle inequality shows that, when weight regularization and simplex truncation vanish, the classifier objective at a global minimizer does not exceed that of the best fixed candidate; otherwise, the excess is explicitly bounded. For high-dimensional data, we develop a column-partitioned variable-splitting solver. It converges with a best-iterate $O(1/T)$ squared-step residual rate. Under common initialization and global parameters, any column partition produces, in exact arithmetic, the same iterates and solution as centralized training. Experiments assess predictive behavior, numerical equivalence, and multi-process scalability.

</details>


### [73] [Interpretable machine learning for predicting splitting strength of asphalt concrete: insights from SHAP analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00956)
*Jianglei Xing,Xiao Tan,Dongzhao Jin,Pengwei Guo,Yuhuan Wang,Huiya Niu*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出可解释机器学习框架预测沥青混凝土劈裂强度(ST)，基于296样本和14输入变量，对比6种模型，TabPFN性能最优（RMSE=0.28，R²=0.88），SHAP识别9个主导变量占92%贡献，并量化了提升ST的参数范围，开发GUI平台增强实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沥青混凝土劈裂强度是关键力学指标，传统经验方法预测精度有限且缺乏可解释性，难以支持数据驱动的混合料设计，需要一种兼具高精度和可解释性的预测框架。

Method: 建立296样本数据库，选取14个输入变量（沥青性质、集料级配、纤维特征），开发TabPFN、ANN、SVR、RF、XGBoost、LightGBM六种模型，其中5个用NSGA-II优化超参数，TabPFN用默认配置；采用SHAP进行全局和局部解释，并开发GUI平台。

Result: 六种模型均表现良好，TabPFN在测试集上最优（RMSE=0.28，MAE=0.21，MAPE=18.01%，MAD=0.14，R²=0.88，综合评分0.91）；SHAP显示Ag9.5、FT、Ag4.75、AC、Du为最关键变量，9个变量贡献92%；量化了提升ST的参数区间（如Ag9.5<66.8%，AC<5.4wt%等）。

Conclusion: 该框架能准确预测劈裂强度并揭示关键影响因素和最优参数范围，GUI平台显著提升可访问性和工程实用性，为沥青混合料数据驱动设计提供了有效工具。

Abstract: This paper presents an interpretable machine-learning framework for predicting the splitting strength (ST) of asphalt concrete and supporting data-driven mixture design. A database consisting of 296 samples was established, and 14 input variables related to asphalt properties, aggregate gradation, and fiber characteristics were selected for modeling. Six machine-learning models, namely TabPFN, ANN, SVR, RF, XGBoost, and LightGBM, were developed and compared. Hyperparameter optimization was performed for five models using NSGA-II, while TabPFN was directly applied with its default configuration. The results show that all six models achieved satisfactory predictive capability, whereas TabPFN delivered the best overall performance on the testing set, with the lowest RMSE of 0.28, MAE of 0.21, MAPE of 18.01%, MAD of 0.14, the highest R^2 of 0.88, and the highest composite score of 0.91. SHAP analysis further revealed that nine dominant variables accounted for 92.0% of the total average contribution, among which Ag9.5, FT, Ag4.75, AC, and Du were the most influential. In addition, favorable parameter ranges for improving ST were quantified, such as Ag9.5 < 66.8%, Ag4.75 < 45.0%, AC < 5.4 wt.%, AV < 3.6%, and Du > 134.7 cm. Finally, a GUI platform integrating prediction and SHAP-based explanation was developed to improve the accessibility and practical applicability of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [74] [Evolutionary Curriculum Learning Improves Biological Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00697)
*Richard Zhu,Kento Nishi*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出演化课程学习（ECL），按进化距离从小到大逐步训练VAE，在蛋白质变异预测和RNA序列生成任务上均优于均匀采样基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准生物VAE训练将所有序列视为可交换，忽略了同源序列间的进化结构，导致模型未能利用进化邻近度这一重要归纳偏置。

Method: ECL以锚点序列为中心，按幂律扩展计划逐步增大采样序列的进化距离，从近缘到远缘渐进式暴露训练数据。

Result: p53和PTEN的ClinVar AUROC分别提升至0.989和1.000（基线不稳定）；RNA家族协方差模型位分在12/15次运行中超过匹配基线，但家族级显著性不足。

Conclusion: 进化距离是生物序列模型训练课程排序的有效归纳偏置，渐进扩展优于固定邻域采样和均匀随机采样。

Abstract: Variational autoencoders (VAEs) trained on multiple sequence alignments (MSAs) have emerged as powerful generative models for biological sequences, with applications ranging from disease variant prediction to functional RNA design. However, standard biological VAE training treats all sequences as exchangeable, ignoring the rich evolutionary structure that organizes homologous sequences from evolutionarily close to highly divergent. We propose Evolutionary Curriculum Learning (ECL), a training strategy that exploits this structure by progressively exposing the model to sequences of increasing evolutionary distance from sampled anchors, following a power-law expansion schedule. Applied to two architecturally distinct VAE models and two biological domains--protein variant effect prediction with EVE and RNA family sequence generation with RfamGen--ECL improves downstream task performance across five random seeds per configuration. Mean ClinVar classification AUROC rises from 0.981 to 0.989 for p53; for PTEN, ECL attains 1.000 in every seed whereas the baseline is unstable (mean 0.905, falling as low as 0.54). For RNA, ECL raises mean covariance-model bit scores on all three families tested and exceeds its seed-matched baseline in 12 of 15 training runs, though with only three families the effect cannot be established as significant at the family level. Ablation experiments show that progressively expanding the sampled sequences by evolutionary distance outperforms fixed-size neighborhood sampling in addition to uniform random sampling. Evolutionary distance is therefore a useful inductive bias for ordering the training curriculum in biological sequence modeling.

</details>


### [75] [Hierarchical Solomonoff Induction: An Unbounded Machine Learning Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01005)
*Nathan Young*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 通过将德菲内蒂交换性定理应用于所罗门诺夫归纳，提出分层所罗门诺夫归纳（HSI），该模型在保持所罗门诺夫归纳最优性的同时，显式建模给定数据集下的外推预测，并证明其误差边界与收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 所罗门诺夫归纳缺乏对训练数据集的显式描述，无法自然建模大语言模型式的从数据外推任务；本文旨在构建一个既保留所罗门诺夫归纳理论理想性、又能条件化于既有序列的贝叶斯层次模型。

Method: 利用德菲内蒂定理将交换性分布引入所罗门诺夫先验，构造超先验覆盖所有所罗门诺夫先验；扩展Wood等人的混合半测度等价性证明，论证对混合再混合仍等价于原始所罗门诺夫归纳；并推导预测误差上界与超先验中生成器复杂度的关系。

Result: 证明HSI与所罗门诺夫归纳完全等价；HSI的期望额外误差受生成器在超先验中的复杂度约束，随数据集增长额外误差均值收敛至0，达到渐近最优预测。

Conclusion: HSI可作为给定数据集条件下序列预测的理想无界模型，其地位等同于所罗门诺夫归纳之于单序列预测，兼具理论最优性与数据条件化能力。

Abstract: Solomonoff Induction, or SolInd, provides an ideal unbounded model of a priori sequence prediction but cannot naturally describe extrapolation from a given training dataset, as performed by Large Language Models. We apply de Finetti's theorem on exchangeable distributions to SolInd to produce what we call Hierarchical Solomonoff Induction, or HSI, which maintains a hyperprior over all Solomonoff priors that can be conditioned on previously observed sequences.
  We extend Wood et al.'s proof that universal mixtures of semimeasures are equivalent to SolInd to show that universal mixtures of these mixtures are also equivalent, proving that HSI=SolInd. We also prove that HSI's excess error on any distribution, compared to its true generator, is bounded by that generator's complexity in the hyperprior. This result is directly comparable to SolInd's prediction error being bounded by the Kolmogorov complexity of the sequence being predicted, and forces HSI's average excess error to converge to 0 as a dataset grows, leading to optimal prediction in the limit. We claim that HSI is an ideal unbounded model of sequence prediction given a dataset in the same way that SolInd is ideal over individual sequences.

</details>


### [76] [Logit-Origin Centering for Singleton Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01074)
*Mayank Sharma,Rohit Kumar Mourya,Pratik Mazumder*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文首次指出现有全测试时适应（FTTA）方法在严格流式（逐样本到达）表格数据场景下性能严重退化，并提出轻量级方法PLOC，仅通过维护一个运行均值来平移logit空间，无需标签或权重更新，在五个表格基准和多种架构上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据在现实场景中广泛使用，但深度模型在分布偏移下性能不佳。现有FTTA方法继承自计算机视觉的批处理假设，在严格流式（批大小为1）时因批统计量不可用或估计不准而失效。作者认为单样本FTTA不仅是小批量变体，而是一个独特的可辨识性问题，需专门设计。

Method: 提出Prequential Logit-Origin Centering (PLOC)：保持源模型冻结，在每一步对logit空间进行平移，仅存储过去logit的单个运行均值。无需标签、不估计先验、不更新权重。另提供延迟变体，应用静态平移以完全保持源排序和AUROC。

Result: 在五个表格基准、三种架构（MLP、FT-Transformer、TabTransformer）和五个独立源检查点上，PLOC显著优于强表格基线和基于熵的基线。

Conclusion: PLOC为严格流式表格FTTA提供了有效且轻量的解决方案，证明了针对该独特可辨识性问题专门设计方法的必要性，并展示了其在不同架构和检查点上的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Tabular data is used extensively in many real-world use cases. Deep learning models have been developed to deal with tabular data, but generally perform poorly when the test data distribution differs from that of the training data. Researchers have proposed test-time adaptation approaches to deal with this problem. The fully test-time adaptation (FTTA) setting involves adapting deployed classifiers to shifted target distributions using only unlabeled test data. Leading FTTA methods inherit a batch-dependent approach from computer vision literature. This paper demonstrates for the first time that such approaches degrade sharply in strict streaming regimes where examples arrive and must be classified one at a time. This occurs because at a batch size of one, batch-level statistics become unavailable or poorly estimated. We argue that singleton tabular FTTA is not merely a small-batch variant of ordinary FTTA, but a distinct identifiability problem where only the location of the model's score stream remains directly observable. To address this, we propose Prequential Logit-Origin Centering (PLOC), a lightweight approach that keeps the source model frozen and shifts the logit space at each step. PLOC stores only a single running number (the mean of past logits), requires no labels, estimates no priors, and bypasses weight updates entirely. A deferred variant applies a static shift that preserves the source ranking, and thus the AUROC, exactly. Evaluated across five tabular benchmarks, three architectures (MLP, FT-Transformer, and TabTransformer), and five independent source checkpoints, PLOC significantly outperforms strong tabular and entropy-based baselines.

</details>


### [77] [When Do Surrogate Updates Improve Decisions? A Local Theory of Trajectory-Wise Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01130)
*Yuyang Shen*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文针对轨迹训练与下游任务奖励不匹配的问题，提出理论框架，给出单步及多步迁移界限、梯度共线条件、轨迹选择遗憾界及近似-校准权衡，并通过实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多模型通过轨迹损失更新，但评估基于下游任务奖励，导致训练目标与评估指标不一致，理论上缺乏对这种不匹配的量化分析。

Method: 定义轨迹的learnability和decision utility，推导单步迁移界（分解为一阶梯度错位与二阶曲率）及路径式累积扩展；分析可达梯度非零时的正共线条件；用校准差距界定轨迹选择的决策遗憾，并提出候选差异细化；建立嵌套更新空间的近似-校准权衡。

Result: 理论给出四个主要结果，包括一阶/二阶迁移界、共线等价条件、遗憾界及其细化、权衡关系；网格世界和LLM后训练实验与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 该理论框架系统地刻画了轨迹损失与决策效用之间的迁移关系，为模型更新策略提供了理论指导，并通过实验验证了其有效性。

Abstract: A broad range of models face the mismatch where they are updated through trajectory losses but are evaluated by downstream task reward. Here, a trajectory is a training instance that induces a surrogate loss whose reduction might not track the model's decision utility update. Theoretically, we ask when one step of trajectory training reduces both population surrogate loss and decision risk, and how transfer accumulates along repeated updates. To formalize this, we first fix a checkpoint and a restricted update space, and define the reductions in population surrogate risk and decision risk induced by a trajectory as its learnability and decision utility, respectively. On this basis, our theory yields four main results. First, a one-step transfer bound separates their discrepancy into first-order gradient misalignment after nonnegative calibration and second-order curvature; and a pathwise extension accumulates the same terms over repeated updates. Second, when the accessible surrogate gradient is nonzero, universal first-order transfer over every accessible direction holds exactly when the accessible surrogate and decision gradients are positively collinear. Third, the calibration gap bounds the decision regret of learnability-based trajectory selection, while a candidate-difference refinement tightens this guarantee by retaining only directions that affect pairwise rankings. Finally, we establish an approximation--calibration trade-off across nested update spaces. Controlled gridworld and LLM post-training experiments yield results consistent with our predictions.

</details>


### [78] [Policy Optimality Measurement for Multi-Vehicle Decision-Making: From Extrinsic Indicators to Intrinsic Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01133)
*Ye Han,Lijun Zhang,Dejian Meng*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出一种基于信息论的诊断框架，利用MCTS作为渐近最优基线，通过前向KL散度定义策略最优性分数，并在横向和纵向维度上解耦，以暴露多智能体强化学习策略的内在缺陷和盲点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶中多智能体强化学习评估依赖外在统计指标（如奖励曲线、成功率），这些指标无法揭示策略退化与算法盲点，需要一种能深入评估内在策略质量的模型无关标准。

Method: 以完全收敛的蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）作为渐近最优仲裁者，构建理论地面真值基线分布；基于前向KL散度设计有界策略最优性分数，并对该分数进行横向与纵向上语义解耦，形成细粒度诊断工具。

Result: 在最新MARL架构和探索机制上的时空诊断表明，该方法能显著揭示隐藏的方向性偏差、识别时间平均策略陷阱，并将超参数调优转化为可视化轨迹优化过程。

Conclusion: 该框架为基准测试内在多智能体策略质量提供了严格且模型无关的标准，弥补了外在指标评估的不足。

Abstract: Evaluating Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) policies in autonomous driving fundamentally relies on extrinsic statistical indicators (e.g., reward curves and success rates), which often mask intrinsic policy degradation and algorithmic blind spots. To break this black-box evaluation, this letter proposes a novel information-theoretic diagnostic framework. By leveraging a fully converged Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) as an asymptotic oracle, we establish a theoretical ground-truth baseline distribution. We formulate a bounded policy optimality score ($\mathcal{M}_{opt}$) using the forward KL divergence to rigorously penalize fatal collaborative omissions. Crucially, we semantically decouple this metric into lateral and longitudinal dimensions, creating a granular "semantic microscope". Extensive spatial and temporal diagnostics on state-of-the-art MARL architectures and exploration mechanisms demonstrate that our framework conclusively exposes hidden directional biases, identifies temporal average-policy traps, and transforms heuristic hyperparameter tuning into a visually trackable trajectory optimization. This framework establishes a rigorous, model-agnostic standard for benchmarking intrinsic multi-agent policy quality.

</details>


### [79] [CADIR: A Cross-Backend Editable Intermediate Representation for Agentic CAD Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00891)
*Yu Liu,Jingzhe Ni,Yiming Chen,Junqi Huang,Ruofeng Tong,Min Tang,Peng Du*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出CADIR，一种基于OCCT内核的可执行中间表示，通过构造图和几何签名匹配实现跨CAD平台的历史可编辑重建，并支持文本/图像检索和代理生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CAD生成方法要么使用后端特定的顺序脚本（隐式依赖），要么输出静态几何，难以同时保留构建历史、稳定的拓扑引用和跨系统的特征级可编辑性。

Method: CADIR基于OCCT（通过OCP）提供显式组合建模操作和细粒度执行诊断，执行时记录构造图；引入几何签名匹配（Geometric Signature Matching）以在参数变化和系统差异下识别对应边/面，支撑FreeCAD、SolidWorks、Fusion 360的自适应重建；另提出构造图检索方法（支持全图与子图）以服务文本/图像查询的代理。

Result: 实验显示CADIR在几何保真度和执行可靠性上优于现有表示；构造图检索进一步提升生成质量；跨后端编辑实现多CAD环境下的可靠重建与重建后编辑。

Conclusion: CADIR作为一种可执行中间表示，有效解决了CAD生成中历史保留与跨系统可编辑性的矛盾，并验证了构造图检索对生成与编辑的增益。

Abstract: Large language models have made it possible to generate executable computer-aided design (CAD) programs from natural-language descriptions or images. However, existing methods represent modeling processes as backend-specific sequential scripts with implicit dependencies or as static geometry, making it difficult to simultaneously preserve construction history, stable topological references, and feature-level editability across different CAD systems. We present CADIR, an agent-friendly executable intermediate representation for CAD generation and cross-backend editing. Built on the OCCT geometry kernel via OCP, CADIR provides explicit, compositional modeling operations and fine-grained execution diagnostics. During program execution, CADIR records modeling operations, parameter dependencies, constraints, and topology selections in a construction graph. To enable reliable cross-backend reconstruction, we introduce Geometric Signature Matching, which identifies corresponding edges and faces despite parameter changes and backend differences, allowing adapters to reconstruct native editable feature histories in FreeCAD, SolidWorks, and Fusion 360. Building on this representation, we further propose a construction-graph retrieval method for text and image queries that supports both full-graph and subgraph retrieval, enabling agents to leverage complete models and modeling substructures. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CADIR achieves higher geometric fidelity and execution reliability than existing CAD representations, that construction-graph retrieval further improves model generation quality, and that cross-backend editing enables reliable model reconstruction and post-reconstruction editing across multiple CAD environments.

</details>


### [80] [AdaHAT: Adaptive Hard Attention to the Task in Task-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01252)
*Pengxiang Wang,Hongbo Bo,Jun Hong,Weiru Liu,Kedian Mu*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaHAT，一种具有自适应注意力机制的网络架构，通过考虑参数对旧任务的重要性和当前网络容量，允许静态参数自适应更新，从而缓解任务增量学习长序列中的网络容量问题，优于HAT等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 架构类方法在长任务序列中，为防止遗忘，越来越多参数变得静态，导致网络容量问题，限制了学习新任务的能力。

Method: AdaHAT扩展HAT，使用自适应硬注意力机制，基于旧任务参数重要性和当前网络容量动态决定哪些静态参数可被更新，平衡稳定性和可塑性。

Result: 在多个数据集上，AdaHAT在平均任务性能上优于HAT等基线，尤其在长序列中优势明显，表明其有效缓解容量问题。

Conclusion: AdaHAT通过自适应平衡稳定性与可塑性，提高了长序列任务增量学习的性能，验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting is a major problem in task-incremental learning, where neural networks tend to overwrite previously learned knowledge when trained on new tasks. A number of architecture-based approaches have been proposed to address this problem. However, the architecture-based approaches suffer from another problem related to network capacity when the networks learn long task sequences: As a network is trained on an increasing number of new tasks in a long task sequence, a growing proportion of active parameters becomes static to prevent forgetting of previously learned knowledge. In this paper, we propose Adaptive Hard Attention to the Task (AdaHAT) with an adaptive attention mechanism which allows adaptive updates to static parameters by taking into account the information about previous tasks on both the importance of these parameters to previous tasks and the current network capacity. Based on this idea, we develop a new neural network architecture incorporating our proposed AdaHAT mechanism. AdaHAT extends an existing architecture-based approach, Hard Attention to the Task (HAT), to better support task-incremental learning over long task sequences. We conduct experiments on a number of datasets and compare AdaHAT with task-incremental learning baselines including HAT. Our experimental results show that AdaHAT achieves better average performance across tasks than these baselines, especially on long task sequences, demonstrating the benefits from balancing the trade-off between stability and plasticity of a network when learning such sequences of tasks, alleviating the network capacity problem. Our code is available at pengxiang-wang.com/projects/continual-learning-arena.

</details>


### [81] [Search-GRT: Guided Retrieval Training of Search Agents to Optimize for Complex Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00974)
*Aounon Kumar,Sudipta Paul,Vivek Kulkarni,Vijay Srinivasan,Srinivas Chappidi*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出引导式检索训练（GRT），通过在强化学习训练中利用真实信息限制检索过程，缓解稀疏奖励问题，显著提升大语言模型在多跳问答中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在复杂多跳问答任务中使用搜索引擎时，因早期检索错误导致级联误差；强化学习虽可改善搜索能力，但训练中奖励稀疏，模型难以有效学习。

Method: GRT在强化学习训练阶段，利用真实信息（ground truth）限制检索文档范围，聚焦于相关文档集，提供更强的学习信号，从而帮助模型生成更准确的子查询并综合答案。

Result: 在多种问答任务上一致优于Search-R1等现有方法；多跳问答任务性能提升超40%，且训练效率更高，用更少训练步骤达到更优性能。

Conclusion: GRT通过约束检索空间有效解决了稀疏奖励问题，提升了大语言模型搜索代理的问答能力，尤其在多跳场景中效果显著，并提高了训练效率。

Abstract: The effective use of search engines by large language models (LLMs) remains a significant challenge, particularly in complex, multi-hop question-answering (MHQA) tasks. These tasks require the model to decompose questions into subqueries, retrieve relevant information, and synthesize answers from multiple sources, often leading to cascading errors due to poor retrieval in early stages. Reinforcement learning (RL) has shown promise in improving LLMs' search capabilities, but it often suffers from sparse rewards during training, hindering the model's ability to learn effectively. To address these challenges, we introduce Guided Retrieval Training (GRT), a novel method that improves the performance of a search agent by restricting the retrieval process during RL training using ground truth information. By focusing on a curated set of relevant documents, GRT provides the model with a stronger learning signal, mitigating the problem of sparse rewards and improving its ability to generate accurate subqueries and synthesize correct answers. Our experimental results demonstrate that GRT achieves consistent performance improvements over existing methods, such as Search-R1, across a wide range of question-answering (QA) tasks. Notably, GRT excels in MHQA tasks, achieving over 40% improvements in performance. Additionally, GRT enhances training efficiency by achieving better QA performance with fewer training steps.

</details>


### [82] [Passing Coarse Marginal Checks Can Be Cheap: Persona Mixtures and Imprecise Treatment-Response Estimates in an LLM Persona Panel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00979)
*Yohei Nakajima*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本研究检验了16个轻量级人格条件GPT-4.1配置在重复策略博弈中的表现，发现其边际响应在多数条件下接近人类数据，但变异主要源于提示索引，且治疗效应估计存在不确定性；p13结果未被前瞻性家族控制，仅作为复制目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型越来越多地被用作合成研究参与者，但通常仅通过边际响应与人类数据的相似性进行验证；本研究旨在更严格地评估固定人格条件面板在重复博弈中的行为有效性及其统计缺陷。

Method: 使用固定面板的16种人格条件GPT-4.1配置，在四个重复博弈单元中运行预注册的广参考条件均值检验；采用对称狄利克雷敏感性分析和有限机会插件估计；同时进行措辞与位置操作及标签冲突测试；事后进行外部审查和零调用重分析。

Result: 面板在四个重复博弈单元中通过三个，唯一未通过者低于下限0.011；提示索引变异占比为47%-96%取决于不确定性假设；持续概率对比为+0.083和+0.078，置信区间包含零；措辞操作将合作从0/40提升至37/40；p13未被前瞻性控制且检验效能不足；零调用重分析改变了解释。

Conclusion: 该固定模型提示面板的结果不能确立人类可替代性；注册边际标准可通过而未精确估计治疗响应对象；p13应被视为复制目标而非发现，且研究暴露了家族错误、依赖、构造和边界不确定性缺陷。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as synthetic research participants and are often validated by whether their marginal responses resemble human data. We study a fixed panel of sixteen lightweight persona-conditioned GPT-4.1 configurations in repeated strategic games. The panel met preregistered broad-reference condition-mean criteria in three of four repeated-game cells; the sole miss was 0.011 below the lower reference bound. Variation was strongly prompt-indexed, but its share depended on uncertainty assumptions: fixed-panel symmetric-Dirichlet sensitivities produced median between-prompt shares of 63%-71% under Jeffreys alpha=0.5 and 47%-53% under alpha=1, while finite-opportunity plug-in estimates were 85%-96%. Aggregate continuation-probability contrasts were +0.083 and +0.078, with conservative simultaneous 95% intervals [-0.171, +0.330] and [-0.181, +0.330]. The treatment jointly changed the continuation process and its textual representation. A separate wording-and-position operation shifted cooperation from 0/40 to 37/40 in the bare configuration, and a label conflict also revealed representation control. The original persona-level p13 result was not prospectively family-controlled, while a post-adjudication exact gate was structurally underpowered; p13 is therefore a replication target rather than a finding. External review exposed family-error, dependence, construct, and boundary-uncertainty defects, and zero-call reanalysis changed the interpretation without rewriting the historical record. The registered marginal criteria could be passed without precisely estimating the treatment-response object. A public capsule verifies 4,916 confirmatory Phase 3-5 runs with no live model calls. The results concern one fixed model-prompt panel and do not establish human substitutability.

</details>


### [83] [Training nGPT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01284)
*Ilya Loshchilov,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出nGPT（归一化Transformer）在混合Mamba-2-Transformer MoE模型上的实用训练配方，通过多种技巧使14B参数模型在约一半训练token下达到相同验证损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 归一化Transformer通过将参数和激活约束到单位超球面实现超球面表示学习，但缺乏在复杂现代架构（如混合Mamba-2-Transformer MoE）上的实用训练指南，本文旨在填补该空白。

Method: 训练配方包括Logit梯度预条件、对数学习率衰减、GatedAdamW、角更新控制及可选探索机制，并应用于最多14B总参数的多种模型规模。

Result: 与相同架构未归一化模型（AdamW训练）相比，nGPT模型实现相同验证损失所需训练token减少约一半，且配方在含14B参数的模型中可扩展。

Conclusion: nGPT配合所提训练配方能在混合MoE架构上显著提升训练效率，减少数据需求，且具备规模可扩展性。

Abstract: The normalized Transformer (nGPT) realizes hyperspherical representation learning by constraining model parameter vectors and activation vectors to the unit hypersphere. In this paper, we describe a practical training recipe for nGPT and evaluate it on modern hybrid Mamba-2--Transformer Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models. The recipe introduces Logit Gradient Preconditioning, Logarithmic Learning Rate Decay, GatedAdamW, angular update control, and optional exploration mechanisms. Compared with an unnormalized model of the same hybrid MoE architecture trained with AdamW, the 14B-total-parameter nGPT model reaches the same validation loss using approximately half as many training tokens. The recipe scales across the models considered, which contain up to 14B total parameters.

</details>


### [84] [SCHEDBench: A Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Constraint Faithfulness in Natural-Language Combinatorial Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00991)
*Shrenil Shaun Sharma,Avi Sharma*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文提出SCHEDBench，一个用于评估大语言模型在自然语言表面形式变化下保持组合调度约束满足能力的基准，包含1132个实例和多种变体，发现现有模型对语义等价的表述不够鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估未系统检验LLM对调度问题中约束满足的忠实性，尤其是在表面形式变化时模型可能违背硬约束，缺乏标准化基准。

Method: 基于经典调度实例和求解器验证的可行性与最优性，通过领域模板、主题实体、词法句法重述和约束级变化生成自然语言问题，并评估13个前沿及开源LLM的约束满足表现。

Result: 模型对语义等价的渲染不具可靠不变性；表面形式变化降低可行性并引发超出噪声水平的硬约束违反；约束重排序是最敏感的孤立变化轴。

Conclusion: LLM在调度约束忠实性上对表面形式变化敏感，SCHEDBench可作为评测工具，推动更鲁棒的约束遵循能力研究。

Abstract: This paper introduces SCHEDBench, a natural-language benchmark for evaluating combinatorial scheduling constraint faithfulness under surface-form variation. Grounded in canonical scheduling instances and solver-derived feasibility and optimality, SCHEDBench assesses whether large language models (LLMs) generate schedules with the same constraint-feasible behavior across varied natural-language (NL) surface forms. SCHEDBench spans 1,132 instances across job-shop scheduling problems (JSP), single and multi-mode resource-constrained project scheduling problems (RCPSP), nurse rostering/scheduling, and curriculum timetabling problems of varying difficulty. Instances are templated into natural language problems using domain-specific templates, themed entities, lexical-syntactic template rephrasing, and constraint-level surface-form variation, with reference solutions verified for feasibility and objective optimality. Across thirteen frontier and open-weight LLMs, we find that models are not reliably invariant to semantically equivalent renderings of the same scheduling problem. Surface-form variation reduces feasibility and induces above-noise shifts in per-instance hard-constraint violations on matched instances. Among the tested isolated axes, constraint reordering yields the clearest above-noise sensitivity.

</details>


### [85] [AlphaG-OPD: Reliability-Gated Sibling Counterfactuals for On-Policy Distillation in Symbolic Alpha Factor Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01303)
*Yaoyu Su*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出AlphaG-OPD框架，通过结构化的在线策略蒸馏将因子终局奖励转化为局部动作指导，在不改变奖励、轨迹平衡等核心组件的前提下提升跨市场因子挖掘性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号化alpha因子发现只能对完整表达式打分，无法为中间结构决策提供直接标签；GFlowNets虽保持多样性和奖励比例分布，但其轨迹级目标不比较中间状态下未选择的兄弟动作，导致局部决策缺乏有效监督。

Method: 设计三部分结构：I选择教学位置，枚举当前策略访问的合法语法兄弟节点；II通过四个共享后缀评估三个候选兄弟，仅当匹配比较结果具备胜者一致性和正经验下置信界时接受KL有界目标；III通过有界回放、分数索引过期和前向梯度平衡强化教学强度与时长，无需额外因子评估。

Result: 在CSI300、CSI500、CSI1000和S&P 500上，完整方法在多个随机种子下实现强劲的跨市场表现，等物理分数四臂消融验证了配对教学、可靠性门控和巩固策略各自的有效性。

Conclusion: AlphaG-OPD将终端奖励转化为局部动作指导，在不改变现有框架核心组件的情况下显著提升因子发现的跨市场鲁棒性和性能，为结构化策略蒸馏提供了一种有效范式。

Abstract: Symbolic alpha factor discovery can score a completed expression, but it provides no direct label for the structural decisions that produced it. Generative flow networks (GFlowNets) preserve a diverse, reward-proportional distribution over complete expressions, yet their trajectory-level objective does not compare unchosen sibling actions at an intermediate state. We introduce AlphaG-OPD, a structural on-policy distillation framework that turns terminal factor evaluations into local action guidance. Its design separates three decisions. Component I determines where to teach by exposing grammar-valid siblings at partial abstract-syntax-tree (AST) states visited by the current forward policy. Component II determines what is reliable enough to teach: it evaluates three supported siblings under four shared suffixes and admits a KL-bounded target only when their matched comparisons exhibit sufficient winner agreement and a positive empirical lower confidence bound (LCB). Component III determines how strongly and for how long to teach by consolidating accepted targets through bounded replay, score-indexed expiry, and forward-gradient balancing, without additional factor evaluations. Terminal reward, Trajectory Balance, the backward policy, grammar, and factor-pool rules remain unchanged. An equal-physical-score four-arm ablation tests paired teaching, reliability gating, and consolidation. Across China's CSI300, CSI500, and CSI1000 and the U.S. S&P 500, the complete method delivers strong cross-market performance over multiple random seeds.

</details>


### [86] [On the Identifiability of Masked Prediction: Mode Blindness and Mask Schedules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01383)
*Yichao Cai,Javen Qinfeng Shi*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文研究了掩码预测中，掩码调度如何决定联合分布的可识别性，发现大上下文主导的掩码会对全局模式权重失明，而低可见性掩码和正的全掩码质量可恢复识别性，并通过理论、枚举、梯度和真实语料实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码预测通过拟合调度加权的条件分布族来学习表示，但尚不清楚何时近似最优的条件预测能够唯一确定底层联合分布，尤其是在数据具有两个分离良好的全局模式且超出快速混合恢复保证范围时。

Method: 提出ε-可识别性模量量化给定超额风险下的最大分布误差，建立精确信息分解将模式权重敏感性与可见上下文下的残差模式不确定性关联，分析不同掩码调度（大上下文、低可见性、正全掩码质量）对可识别性的影响。

Result: 大上下文主导的掩码在超额风险指数小时仍保持宏观分布误差，即对模式权重盲视；低可见性掩码恢复敏感性，正全掩码质量无需数据假设即可锚定联合分布。枚举、梯度训练和真实语料实验分别验证了理论速率、模式盲视与恢复现象，并显示自然文本介于两种认证机制之间。

Conclusion: 掩码预测的可识别性由掩码调度单独决定，设计掩码时应避免过度依赖大上下文，并确保包含足够的低可见性掩码或正全掩码质量，以保证联合分布的可靠恢复。

Abstract: Masked prediction learns representations by fitting a schedule-weighted family of conditional laws, but it remains unclear when near-optimal conditional prediction pins down the underlying joint law. We study this question for data with two well-separated global modes, outside the reach of rapid-mixing recovery guarantees, and show that the answer is decided by the mask schedule alone.
