Under large-context mode pinning, reweighting the two modes can move the joint law by a constant in total variation while perturbing the masked objective exponentially little in the visible-context size: mask schedules dominated by large contexts are provably blind to the global mode weights. To quantify this, we introduce an $\varepsilon$-identifiability modulus, the largest distributional error consistent with a given excess risk, and prove that it remains macroscopic at an excess risk that is exponentially small. An exact information decomposition pinpoints what restores identifiability: mode-weight sensitivity is governed by the residual mode uncertainty given the visible context. Consequently, low-visibility masks recover this sensitivity, and positive full-mask mass anchors the joint law over all admissible models with no assumption on the data law. Empirically, we test our theory at three levels: enumeration on computable laws verifies the predicted rates, gradient training reproduces both the mode blindness and the recovery, and measurements on real corpora place natural text between the two certified regimes.

</details>


### [87] [Role-Decoupled Attention Residuals: Separating Matching and Content Retrieval Across Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01075)
*Kehan Wang*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出角色解耦注意力残差（RD-AttnRes），将查询/键与值的深度路由解耦，在120M和343M模型上均稳定提升困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有块注意力残差用单一内容相关深度混合同时构造Q、K、V，但匹配（Q/K）与内容检索（V）是功能不同的决策，不应强制读取相同深度。

Method: 在共享Q/K深度路由的基础上，为值独立学习一条残差深度路由；仅增加每层一个宽度向量参数，不引入额外注意力操作。

Result: 在所有10次匹配对比中验证NLL均下降，120M/343M模型困惑度分别降低2.97%和2.43%；早期预算对照排除参数数、重复路由或固定值路由的干扰。

Conclusion: 在评估的训练机制内，注意力匹配与内容检索确实受益于对残差层级的不同读取，解耦路由是有效且轻量的改进方向。

Abstract: Depth-routing residual architectures allow Transformer layers to retrieve earlier representations instead of inheriting only the immediately preceding state. Existing Block Attention Residuals, however, use a single content-dependent depth mixture to construct the inputs to queries, keys, and values. This design couples two functionally different decisions: queries and keys determine where attention matches, whereas values determine what content is retrieved. We therefore ask whether matching and content retrieval should be forced to read from the same depth. We introduce Role-Decoupled Attention Residuals (RD-AttnRes), a minimal extension that shares one depth route between queries and keys while learning an independent value route over the same residual sources. Tying the two routing queries exactly recovers the parent architecture, while decoupling them adds only one model-width vector per layer and introduces no additional token-to-token attention operation. We evaluate RD-AttnRes using a frozen, paired pretraining protocol on FineWeb-Edu with five matched seeds for both 120M- and 343M-parameter models and a 2.0B-token training budget. RD-AttnRes improves validation negative log-likelihood in all 10 matched comparisons. The mean reductions are 0.0301 and 0.0247, corresponding to perplexity reductions of 2.97 percent and 2.43 percent at 120M and 343M parameters, respectively. Early-budget controls indicate that neither the additional parameter count, duplicated routing execution, nor a fixed value route reproduces the improvement. Routing diagnostics further reveal persistent divergence between the query-key and value depth distributions. These results suggest that, within the evaluated training regime, attention matching and content retrieval benefit from distinct reads over the residual hierarchy.

</details>


### [88] [Statistical Mechanics of Learning on Product Wasserstein Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01434)
*Srinivasa Rao P Vangmayi P Reddy*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文提出将分布约束视为学习发生的固有几何结构，而非单纯限制模型容量，并据此开发了基于Wasserstein流形的经典和量子学习算法，实验显示其能改善泛化、稳定训练并缓解贫瘠高原问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为权重分布约束会缩小解空间、降低模型容量；作者反其道而行，基于分布约束感知机的前期工作，主张预设的权重分布定义了学习自然展开的内在几何，从而将容量缩减重新诠释为约束流形自身的度量结构。

Method: 将深度神经网络和变分量子电路分别建模为乘积Wasserstein流形上的梯度流（每层一个经典Wasserstein空间，电路参数一个量子Wasserstein空间）；提出深度网络的层次化平均场描述，并利用一阶量子Wasserstein距离扩展至量子领域；据此设计两种近似沿流形测地线更新的实用算法：Hierarchical DisCo-SGD和Quantum DisCo。

Result: 在师生问题、标准图像分类和小型变分量子分类器上的实验表明，与无约束及纯范数约束基线相比，尊重这些分布几何能提升泛化能力、稳定训练过程，并降低量子训练中贫瘠高原的严重程度。

Conclusion: 该方法首次将结构约束重新定位为几何先验，为把生物、谱或硬件来源的分布信息整合进经典与量子学习系统提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Normally the statistical mechanics of learning treats constraints on weight distributions as restrictions that shrink the space of possible solutions. Therefore, it reduces model capacity. In this paper we would like to take a contrary approach, which, however, is based on the earlier work on distribution-constrained perceptrons. Rather than treating a prescribed weight distribution as a mere restriction, we propose that it defines the intrinsic geometry upon which learning naturally unfolds. We formulate both deep neural networks and variational quantum circuits as gradient flows on a product of Wasserstein manifolds -- one classical Wasserstein space for each layer and one quantum Wasserstein space for the circuit parameters. Within this geometry, the capacity reduction, which was previously associated with distributional constraints, appears as the metric structure of the constraint manifold itself. We develop a hierarchical mean-field description for deep networks, extend the framework to the quantum setting using the quantum Wasserstein distance of order 1, and introduce two such practical algorithms, Hierarchical DisCo-SGD and Quantum DisCo, that follow approximate geodesics on the manifold of the product itself. Experiments on teacher-student problems, standard image classification tasks, and small variational quantum classifiers show that respecting these distributional geometries improves generalization, stabilizes training, and reduces the severity of barren plateaus compared with unconstrained and purely norm-based baselines. This approach firstly reframes structural constraints as geometric priors and suggests a route for incorporating biological, spectral, or hardware-derived distributional information into both learning systems, viz., classical and quantum learning.

</details>


### [89] [MA-HEAD-Net: Adaptive Rule-Guided Multi-Agent DRL for AoI Minimization in UAV-Assisted Emergency Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01128)
*Yixin Zhang,Zhuohui Yao,Wenchi Cheng,Walid Saad*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文研究无人机辅助应急通信中信息年龄最小化问题，提出一种结合通信规则先验的多智能体深度强化学习框架MA-HEAD-Net，在异构紧急服务下通过联合优化无人机轨迹、用户调度和检查点间隔选择，实现比现有方法更低的AoI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾后场景中无人机对建立应急通信网络至关重要，而时间敏感救援任务依赖信息新鲜度，陈旧数据会导致无效控制。现有工作未同时处理突发流量建模、有限块长传输与异构服务的调度耦合，且传统优化方法难以应对动态环境的实时决策需求。

Method: 采用马尔可夫调制泊松过程建模突发分组到达，基于有限块长理论刻画传输时长与AoI的耦合关系；设计微型时隙嵌入调度机制以折中长包与短包传输；将联合优化问题建模为多智能体决策问题，提出MA-HEAD-Net，其利用门控多头策略融合通信领域规则先验与学习策略，并通过多智能体近端策略优化联合训练策略与门控模块。

Result: 仿真表明MA-HEAD-Net在动态无人机应急通信场景中，相比代表性多智能体深度强化学习基线具有更高的策略形成效率，且相比学习与启发式方法均实现更低AoI。

Conclusion: 规则引导的多智能体强化学习框架可有效提升应急通信场景中AoI优化性能，验证了将领域先验嵌入策略学习对复杂动态决策的优势。

Abstract: In post-disaster scenarios, unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) are critical for establishing emergency communication networks. For time-critical rescue missions, information freshness is crucial because decisions based on outdated data may lead to ineffective control actions. This paper investigates age of information (AoI) minimization for UAV-assisted emergency communications with heterogeneous emergency services. We model bursty packet arrivals using a Markov-modulated Poisson process and adopt finite blocklength theory to capture the coupling among transmission duration, packet completion, and AoI evolution. To balance delay-tolerant long-packet transmission and urgent short-packet response, we propose a mini-slot-embedded scheduling mechanism with adaptive checkpoint-interval selection. We formulate the joint optimization of UAV trajectory control, user scheduling, and checkpoint-interval selection as a multi-agent decision problem, and develop MA-HEAD-Net, an adaptive rule-guided multi-agent deep reinforcement learning framework. MA-HEAD-Net incorporates communication-domain rule priors into a gated multi-head policy, where adaptive gates regulate the contributions of rule-prior and learned-policy logits for different subtasks. The policy and gating components are jointly optimized under multi-agent proximal policy optimization. Simulation results show that MA-HEAD-Net improves policy-formation efficiency compared with representative multi-agent deep reinforcement learning baselines and achieves lower AoI than both learning-based and heuristic methods in dynamic UAV-assisted emergency communication scenarios.

</details>


### [90] [Stochastic Sequential Search in Very-High-Dimensional Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01502)
*Petr Somol,Jiří Grim*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出随机序贯搜索（SSS）族，用预算化采样步替代全量扫描，使序贯特征选择可扩展至超高维；其浮动搜索变体sSFFS在多个高维数据集上以更低计算成本达到或超过全扫描及现有基准的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序贯子集搜索（如前向浮动搜索）质量高，但每步需扫描全部剩余候选特征，无法用于超高维问题；而仅有的单特征排序虽可扩展，却几乎不建模特征交互。需一种保持序贯搜索质量且代价与维度无关的方法。

Method: 引入预算化采样步对：每步固定评估次数，候选由在线学习的依赖感知统计量经温度控制softmax采样生成，并加均匀探索下限；替换原操作符可将任意序贯方法转为随机对应物，形成SSS族，重点研究浮动搜索的随机版sSFFS。

Result: 在500维madelon上sSFFS用约1/4评估保留全SFFS 97%以上准则值；5000维gisette上超越DAF和BIF饱和水平；10105维reuters上在搜索目标和留出精度上均支配BIF和DAF，单核约两分钟完成。

Conclusion: sSFFS在超高维下以有限预算维持高质量特征选择，验证了采样替代全扫描的有效性；高维下约束常在于准则而非搜索本身，且提供了可复现的独立实现。

Abstract: Sequential subset search -- forward selection with floating backtracking and its descendants -- remains the quality reference in feature selection, but every member of the family sweeps the full pool of remaining candidate features at each step, which excludes it from very-high-dimensional problems; there, only individual-feature ranking remains practical, and it models feature interplay weakly or not at all. We introduce a budgeted sampled step operator pair that replaces the full sweeps by a fixed number of candidate evaluations per step. Candidates are drawn by temperature-controlled softmax sampling from dependency-aware per-feature statistics learned online from every criterion evaluation the search performs, guarded by a uniform exploration floor; per-step cost becomes independent of dimensionality. Substituting the operators turns any sequential method into its stochastic counterpart, defining the Stochastic Sequential Search (SSS) family; we study the stochastic counterpart of floating search, sSFFS. On 500-dimensional madelon, sSFFS retains at least 97% of the full-SFFS criterion value at every subset size at about a quarter of its evaluations, while uniform sampling at the same budget collapses on madelon's synergistic features. On 5,000-dimensional gisette, far beyond full-SFFS reach, sSFFS exceeds the saturated criterion level of DAF and BIF ranking at matched budgets; holdout validation shows that at 500 training samples the binding constraint beyond the sequential frontier becomes the criterion, not the search. On 10,105-dimensional reuters, under a trustworthy multinomial filter criterion, sSFFS dominates BIF and DAF on the search objective and on holdout accuracy at every subset size, in about two minutes of single-core evaluation work. A verified standalone implementation accompanies the paper.

</details>


### [91] [Perspectives on Tsallis Statistics for Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01223)
*Kleyton da Costa,Bernardo Modenesi*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了Tsallis统计与人工智能的交汇点，涵盖q-熵、q-指数族等数学基础及其在稀疏注意力、强化学习、生成模型等领域的应用，并提出将q作为可学习归纳偏置而非固定超参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tsallis统计已在现代AI中广泛出现（如sparsemax、α-entmax、最大熵RL），但缺乏统一视角；同时深度网络中的重尾权重谱和梯度噪声表现出非广延特征，需要从q-统计角度理解。

Method: 先回顾数学核心（q-熵变分基础、q-指数/对数、q-中心极限定理、q-高斯、超统计动力学），再综述各领域应用并提取统一设计模式——q控制密集/均匀与稀疏/峰值行为的可调插值，最后讨论方法论陷阱与信息几何关联。

Result: 识别出Tsallis统计在softmax泛化、RL、序列/图模型、生成建模、损失设计、优化中的反复出现模式；论证深度网络的重尾现象是非广延签名，并将q定位为可学习归纳偏置。

Conclusion: q应作为可学习的归纳偏置而非固定超参数；未来可探索q-指数族、信息几何与开放方向，以深化对现代学习动力学的非广延理解。

Abstract: Tsallis statistics generalizes Boltzmann-Gibbs statistical mechanics through a single real parameter $q$ that controls the weight assigned to rare and frequent events. Originally proposed to describe physical systems with long-range correlations, multifractal geometry, and heavy-tailed fluctuations, the framework has become a recurring ingredient in modern artificial intelligence (AI): it underlies sparse attention mechanisms (\textsc{sparsemax} and $α$-\textsc{entmax}), maximum-entropy reinforcement learning with controllable exploration, robust and heavy-tailed probabilistic models, and a family of generalized loss functions and regularizers. This paper offers a structured perspective on where Tsallis statistics meets AI. We first review the mathematical core: $q$-entropy and its variational (maximum-entropy) foundation, the $q$-exponential and $q$-logarithm, the $q$-central limit theorem, $q$-Gaussian distributions, and their dynamical origin in superstatistics, emphasizing the properties that matter for machine learning. We then survey applications across softmax generalization, reinforcement learning, sequential and graph neural models, generative and probabilistic modeling, loss design, and optimization, extracting the recurring design pattern in each case: a tunable interpolation between dense/uniform and sparse/peaked behavior governed by $q$. We further argue that the heavy-tailed weight spectra and gradient-noise statistics empirically observed in deep networks are themselves nonextensive signatures, placing modern learning dynamics within the scope of $q$-statistics. Finally, we discuss methodological pitfalls, the relationship to information geometry and $q$-exponential families, and open directions, arguing that $q$ should be treated as a learnable inductive bias rather than a fixed hyperparameter.

</details>


### [92] [Rethinking Personalized Reward Modeling for LLMs under Preference Heterogeneity via Group-Debiased Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01556)
*Seongyoon Kim,Boryeong Cho,Jihwan Oh,Seokhyun Chung,Se-Young Yun*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 联邦学习下奖励模型可以在偏好组平衡时用单一FedAvg初始化快速个性化，组不平衡时失效；提出FedGD通过组去偏采样恢复平衡，无需预先知道分组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦偏好对齐方法假设不同偏好组需要各自初始化，但该假设不必要；平衡组下单一模型即可通过微调超越分组训练模型，而组不平衡是主要障碍。

Method: FedGD在联邦训练中自动发现潜在偏好组，并采用组去偏客户端采样，使每组被均匀代表，学习到的单一奖励模型初始化保持平坦和快速适应。

Result: 在平衡偏好组下，FedAvg初始化经少量本地步骤即可超越独立分组训练的模型；FedGD在组不平衡下恢复了这种适应性，优于基线。

Conclusion: 共享初始化的平坦性比分组特异性初始化更重要；通过组去偏采样可消除不平衡带来的负面影响，实现无需组先验的有效个性化。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly aligned to human preferences via reward modeling, but user preference data are sensitive and often cannot be centralized. Federated learning keeps such data local while learning a shared initial reward model, which is later personalized for each client through local fine-tuning. Because users often assign opposite labels to the same pair of responses, existing federated methods address preference heterogeneity by clustering similar clients and training one reward model per group, assuming that each group requires its own initialization. We show that this assumption is unnecessary. Under balanced preference groups, a single FedAvg model, despite starting at nearly random accuracy, surpasses reward models trained separately for each ground-truth group after only a few local optimization steps. We attribute this phenomenon to the flatness of the shared initialization: averaging across all clients learns richer shared representations that distinguish responses while canceling conflicting preference directions, leaving the model near a decision boundary that can be rapidly adapted. Group imbalance breaks this effect as the cancellation becomes asymmetric and leaves minority clients too far from the boundary to recover. Motivated by this observation, we propose FedGD (Federated Learning with Group Debiasing), which discovers latent preference groups during federated training and learns a single reward model using group-debiased client sampling. By counteracting the effect of group imbalance, FedGD learns an initialization that remains highly adaptable, enabling effective personalization without prior knowledge of the underlying groups.

</details>


### [93] [HindSearch: Trajectory-Level Hindsight Critique for Search-Augmented Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01597)
*Haowei Liu,Jiamian Wang,Hsin-Tai Wu,Zhiqiang Tao,Yi Fang*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出HindSearch，用冻结评判器对失败轨迹写简短批评，并将其作为GRPO的辅助自蒸馏信号，在七基准套件上达到39.4%平均EM，超越此前搜索强化学习基线；消融实验证实事后批评（而非金答案本身）是性能提升的关键来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索增强语言模型智能体通常使用二值精确匹配奖励训练，失败轨迹中的丰富信息（如失败原因、可改进方向）被完全丢弃，导致学习信号稀疏且低效。

Method: 在GRPO框架中引入事后自我蒸馏：每轮rollout后，由冻结的评判器结合金答案对每条失败轨迹生成简短批评；该批评作为辅助的在线策略蒸馏信号，监督学生模型的搜索动作，使模型从失败中学习。

Result: 在标准七基准套件上，使用Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct达到39.4%平均EM，优于此前搜索强化学习基线；移除评判器对金答案的访问权后，大部分性能增益消失，证明事后批评而非金答案直接驱动了改进。

Conclusion: 事后批评提供了密集且可归因的学习信号，能显著提升搜索增强语言模型智能体的性能，且有效性依赖于对金答案的事后访问，证实了事后视角是关键。

Abstract: Search-augmented LM agents are typically trained with a binary exact-match reward, which throws away most of what a failed trajectory tells us about why it failed. We introduce HindSearch, a hindsight self-distillation procedure for GRPO: after each rollout, a frozen judge writes a short critique of every failed trajectory using the gold answer, and the critique supplies an auxiliary on-policy distillation signal on the student's search actions. On the standard seven-benchmark suite with Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct, HindSearch reaches 39.4% average EM, outperforming prior search-RL baselines. Removing the judge's access to the gold answer erases most of the gain, isolating hindsight as the source of the improvement.

</details>


### [94] [Latent-Regime Bias Auditing for Volatility Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01599)
*Arthur Chagas,Pedro Bento,Yan Aquino,Arthur Buzelin,Wagner Meira,Cristiano Arbex Valle*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出一个与模型无关的波动率预测审计框架，通过潜在市场制度识别条件失效，补充传统聚合指标，应用于加密货币和ETF数据后发现高平均精度模型仍存在显著制度偏差和尾部低估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RMSE和MAE等聚合指标掩盖了条件失效，而这种失效对风险管理至关重要，因此需开发能评估预测在潜在市场制度下可靠性的审计方法。

Method: 学习市场状态窗口的时间序列表示，仅用训练信息聚类成制度，样本外分配制度，并比较聚合表现与制度条件偏差、尾部低估及对低估敏感的经济损失。

Result: 在加密货币和ETF日度波动率预测中，具有竞争性聚合精度的模型仍显现大量制度特定偏差和严重尾部欠预测。

Conclusion: 波动率预测评估应超越平均误差，关注预测在何种制度下、如何变得不可靠，框架将重点从'平均最准'转向识别条件失效的制度。

Abstract: Volatility forecasts are commonly evaluated with aggregate accuracy metrics such as RMSE and MAE, but these metrics can hide conditional failures that matter for risk management. This paper proposes a model-agnostic audit framework for evaluating whether volatility forecasts remain reliable across latent market regimes. We learn time-series representations of market-state windows, cluster them into regimes using only training information, assign regimes out of sample, and compare aggregate forecast behavior with regime-conditional bias, tail-underprediction, and underprediction-sensitive economic losses. Applied to daily volatility forecasting across cryptocurrency and ETF assets, the audit shows that models with competitive aggregate accuracy can still exhibit substantial regime-specific bias and severe tail underprediction. The results suggest that volatility forecasting should be evaluated not only by average error, but also by where and how forecasts become unreliable. Our framework shifts forecast evaluation from asking which model is most accurate on average to identifying the market regimes in which apparently accurate forecasts fail conditionally. Reproducibility: https://github.com/arthurchagas1/Latent-Regime-Bias-Auditing-for-Volatility-Forecasting

</details>


### [95] [402Pilot: An x402 Decision Layer for Autonomous Agent Micropayments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01341)
*Yin Li,Yanbo He,Boo-Ho Yang,Rav Lawana,Ziyue Li,Wei Zeng,Jing Tang,Fugee Tsung*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出402Pilot，一个面向自主代理的买方侧支付决策层，结合PA-DCT策略，在有限钱包下优化付费服务选择，实现质量与花费的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可编程支付协议（如x402）支持按请求微支付，但未解决代理在有限钱包下应购买哪个付费服务的问题，缺乏买方侧的支付决策机制。

Method: 构建协议无关的买方决策层402Pilot，实现在线购买策略；提出PA-DCT（支付感知折扣上下文汤普森采样）策略，在钱包压力下自适应调整购买决策，并利用付费后反馈学习；设计402Pilot-Bench基准，覆盖823任务、五种异构提供商管线和三种市场环境，采用冻结重放评估。

Result: PA-DCT在非预言机策略中取得最强固定钱包自适应权衡：保持竞争性服务质量，仅花费钱包的39%-43%，并能随市场变化重新分配支出；在价格冲击下取得最佳非预言机PA-gap/T，并在质量、ROI和PA-gap/T的九个场景-指标组合中取得最佳均值与最差情况排名；消融实验验证了策略设计有效性。

Conclusion: 可编程支付需辅以买方侧决策机制，该机制需能学习服务价值并相应调整购买决策，402Pilot提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Programmable-payment protocols such as x402 enable per-request micropayments, but they do not determine which payable service an autonomous agent should buy under a finite wallet. We formulate this buyer-side problem as agent-native payment decision-making: contextual provider selection under wallet pressure, chosen-only paid feedback, and changing market conditions. We propose 402Pilot, a protocol-agnostic buyer-side decision layer between autonomous agents and payment execution that implements purchasing policies for selecting among payable providers. We instantiate it with PA-DCT, a payment-aware discounted contextual Thompson-sampling policy that adapts purchasing decisions under wallet pressure while learning from post-payment feedback. To evaluate buyer-side payment policies, we introduce 402Pilot-Bench, a frozen-replay benchmark spanning 823 tasks, five heterogeneous provider pipelines, and three market regimes, each evaluated over 30 paired seeds. PA-DCT achieves the strongest fixed-wallet adaptive trade-off among non-oracle policies: it maintains competitive service quality while spending only 39 to 43 percent of the wallet and reallocates spending as market conditions change. It attains the best non-oracle PA-gap/T under the price shock and the best mean and worst-case ranks across the nine scenario-metric combinations of quality, ROI, and PA-gap/T. Comparisons with learning baselines and component ablations further support the effectiveness and design of the proposed decision policy. These results suggest that programmable payment must be complemented by buyer-side decision-making capable of learning service value and adapting purchasing decisions accordingly.

</details>


### [96] [CRAFTS: Collaborative Role-Adaptive Fine-Tuning of LLM Agents for Chemical Process Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01369)
*Ziyun Zhang,Yuxin Lin,Eldin Wee Chuan Lim,Xinghao Ding*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: CRAFTS通过将化工流程模拟构建分解为七个受限角色和确定性工程门控，实现了从自然语言请求到可执行IDAES模型的自动化构建，在450案例数据集上达到91.5%的契约完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建可执行的化工流程模型目前仍需大量人工干预，工程师需处理从需求解读到求解器修复的耦合决策，任一错误都可能导致模型失效，亟需自动化方法降低人工负担和错误风险。

Method: 将流程建模分解为七个有界角色（输入理解、意图恢复、视觉/拓扑/规格专家、调试、优化），角色间用确定性IDAES/Pyomo门控衔接；仅对三个模式关键角色进行微调，其余使用未调优Qwen；引入OpenIDAES-450数据集和分阶段中间表示（VisualGraphIR、TopologyIR、SpecIR等）。

Result: 在82案例冻结测试集上，91.5%的案例完成指定验证和执行契约；单元、流股和有向连接F1分数分别为0.815、0.791和0.782。

Conclusion: 角色专业化、类型化中间表示和确定性工程门控能有效提升自动化流程建模的可靠性，为复杂工程系统的自动化构建提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Constructing an executable chemical-process model remains manually intensive. Chemical engineers translate underspecified requests into coupled decisions about unit operations, thermodynamics, streams, specifications, degrees of freedom (DoF), initialization, solver repair, and optimization; one error can invalidate the model. CRAFTS mirrors the staged workflow of chemical engineers by decomposing simulation building into bounded subtasks assigned to seven bounded roles, with deterministic IDAES/Pyomo gates between stages. Given a natural-language request, process flowsheet diagram (PFD) evidence, and curated chemical-engineering knowledge, Input Understanding and Intent recover requirements, constraints, and process semantics; visual, topology, and specification specialists translate them into typed simulator contracts; and Debug and Optimization support bounded repair and eligible optimization. Fine-tuning is applied to the three schema-critical visual, topology, and specification roles, while the remaining roles use untuned Qwen. The resulting VisualGraphIR, TopologyIR, SpecIR, BuildPlan, and SolveReport expose unit, port, thermodynamic, numerical, and execution decisions. Compatible constructors, property packages, and runners are attached only after semantic artifacts pass engineering gates. We introduce OpenIDAES-450, a 450-case IDAES process- simulation dataset, and evaluate the complete seven-role LangChain/LangGraph workflow through solve and eligible optimization on its frozen 82-case held-out split. CRAFTS completes the prescribed validation and execution contract for for 91.5% of cases and achieves unit, stream, and directed-connection F1 scores of 0.815, 0.791, and 0.782. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of role specialization, typed intermediate representations, and deterministic engineering gates for reliable automated process-model construction.

</details>


### [97] [Sharp Root Anti-Concentration via Projective Incidence and Ordered Root Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01670)
*Zijun Wang,Yuchen Miao,Yifan Hu,Huanmin Liu*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文解决了Balcan等人提出的分段Lipschitz函数在线优化中一维局部根反集中问题，给出了尖端的维度无关刻画，并应用于图学习，实现了改进的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在区间命中常数上存在√N的损失，且对奇异或依赖系数律缺乏可验证的判别准则，阻碍了在线优化遗憾界的精确推导和图学习应用。

Method: 引入截面平均投影入射速度与投影Lipschitz常数的等价性；对多项式系数律，用有序实根律有界密度作为有限性的充要条件，并通过联合/条件系数空间面积公式和两图表证书实现可验证性；分别用投影入射定理和刚性平移技巧构造两类核模型。

Result: 对齐次特征曲线和密度有界系数，最坏区间命中常数恰为A乘截面平均投影入射速度，对立方体支撑系数该速度与投影Lipschitz常数等价，去除√N损失；对d次首一多项式，命中常数有限当且仅当有序实根律有有界密度，因子d的比较是尖的；两类图学习模型分别达到O~((An^2D e^{BD}/ℓ+1)√T)和O~((qn^2κ+1)√T)遗憾。

Conclusion: 建立了反集中问题与投影几何的深层联系，提供了可验证的判别框架，不仅理论上最优，还打通了到遗憾界的完整链条，拓展了对奇异系数律的适用性。

Abstract: This paper answers the one-dimensional local root anti-concentration questions posed by Balcan, Pegden, and Sharma in the context of online optimization of piecewise-Lipschitz functions. For a homogeneous feature curve and coefficients whose density relative to the uniform law on a symmetric convex body $K$ is bounded by $A$, we show that the worst-case interval-hitting constant equals $A$ times a section-averaged projective incidence speed. For cube-supported coefficients, this speed is equivalent, up to universal constants, to the projective Lipschitz constant. This yields a sharp, dimension-free characterization and removes the previous $\sqrt N$ loss. For monic degree-$d$ polynomials under arbitrary coefficient laws, we prove that the interval-hitting constant is finite if and only if the ordered real-root laws have bounded densities, with a factor-$d$ comparison that is sharp. Conditional and joint coefficient-space area formulas, together with a two-chart certificate, make this criterion verifiable for dependent and singular coefficient laws. We also give two graph-learning applications that complete the transition-to-regret chain. A cost-sensitive Gaussian-RBF harmonic classifier uses the projective incidence theorem and achieves expected regret $\widetilde O((An^2D e^{BD}/\ell+1)\sqrt T)$. A common-offset polynomial-kernel model uses rigid translation of the ordered roots and achieves $\widetilde O((qn^2κ+1)\sqrt T)$ regret, even when the induced coefficient law is singular in the ambient coefficient space.

</details>


### [98] [Progressive Agent Skill Generation via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01678)
*Junhao Shen,Zhanqiu Zhang,Yiwen Guo,Hong Cheng*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出Skill-α，一种通过强化学习将技能生成视为序列化编辑过程的方法，利用回滚奖励评估每次编辑，在多个基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能生成方法依赖启发式或流水线，需针对不同证据源专门设计；学习式方法虽统一但缺乏基于相关性或正确性的自然监督信号，技能价值只能通过下游任务表现体现。

Method: 将技能生成建模为序列化编辑过程，逐步将技能构建分解为可独立评估的编辑步骤，引入回滚奖励，通过对比锚定查询下原始技能与编辑后技能的下游执行效果来评估每次编辑，并利用强化学习优化生成策略。

Result: 在文档到技能和经历到技能两种设置中均优于启发式和流水线方法；在GPT-4o主工作器下，平均下游成功率比最强基线在CL-Bench上提升3.3点、tau2-bench上提升6.7点；消融实验验证回滚奖励和渐进式生成的有效性。

Conclusion: Skill-α通过可评估的编辑和回滚奖励机制，有效解决了学习式技能生成缺乏监督信号的问题，能生成更高质量的技能，且各组件设计均有必要。

Abstract: Existing skill generation methods largely rely on heuristics or pipeline-style consolidation, which must be specially designed for different evidence sources. In contrast, learning-based approaches offer a more unified way to model skill generation across heterogeneous sources. However, learning-based skill generation remains challenging because skills lack a natural supervision signal based on relevance or correctness; their value can largely be determined only by whether they improve the behavior of the agent on downstream tasks. To address this challenge, we propose Skill-$α$, a reinforcement learning method for progressively generating high-quality agent skills. Specifically, we formulate skill generation as a sequential editing process that decomposes skill construction into individually evaluable edits, and introduce a novel rollback reward that evaluates each edit by comparing downstream execution under the original and edited skills on an anchored query. Extensive experiments show that Skill-$α$ generates more effective skills than methods based on heuristics or pipelines in both document-to-skill and experience-to-skill settings. Under the main GPT-4o worker, Skill-$α$ improves average downstream success rates over the strongest skill-generation baseline by 3.3 points on CL-Bench and 6.7 points on tau2-bench. Further ablations validate the importance of rollback reward and progressive generation.

</details>


### [99] [Beckmann Transport Models: From Autonomous Flows to One-Step Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01692)
*Lee Cheuk-Kit,Florentin Coeurdoux,Peter Potaptchik,Yilun Du,Michael Samuel Albergo,Eric Vanden-Eijnden*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出自主流匹配框架，利用时间无关速度场在低维流形上精确映射分布，其一步生成映射由守恒方程唯一确定，并在ImageNet 256×256上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配方法常依赖时间相关速度场，导致生成效率与理论一致性不足；且分布映射在奇异支撑（低维流形）下的理论刻画不清晰，需要统一且可学习的框架。

Method: 构造时间无关的自主速度场（自主流）实现两分布间精确映射；推导一步生成映射满足的守恒方程，通过样本直接学习该映射；将Beckmann输运通量约束赋予动力学解释，统一涵盖泊松流生成模型与平衡匹配（二次流匹配回归损失）。

Result: 在ImageNet 256×256上展示了自主流与一步映射的生成效果，并纠正了现有方法中存在的不一致性问题。

Conclusion: 自主流匹配为奇异分布映射提供统一理论框架，兼具动力学意义与可学习性，为高效生成模型奠定基础。

Abstract: We propose an instantiation of flow matching that relies on a time-independent velocity field (an \emph{autonomous flow}) to exactly map between two distributions, so long as the target is singular, i.e.\ supported on a lower-dimensional data manifold. We also show that the one-step generative map associated with this flow is the unique solution of a simple conservation equation, which can be used to learn the map directly from samples. These autonomous flows and maps give a dynamical meaning to the flux constraint of Beckmann's transportation problem. Their construction provides a unifying framework that recovers, for instance, the closed-form Poisson-flow generative model and equilibrium matching with a quadratic flow-matching regression loss. We illustrate how this theory corrects inconsistencies in existing methods and demonstrate the effectiveness of the autonomous flow and the one-step map on ImageNet 256x256.

</details>


### [100] [Beyond On-Policy Exploration: Integrating External Policy Rollouts for Reinforcement Learning in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01717)
*Wonseok Lee,Jimyeong Kim,Jungmin Ko,Wonjong Rhee*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出ERILS方法，通过长度控制和分源奖励处理整合外部策略的高奖励轨迹，显著提升扩散大语言模型在多任务上的强化学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散大语言模型强化学习依赖自身采样轨迹，当成功轨迹稀缺时训练进展有限；引入更强外部策略的高奖励轨迹可缓解该问题，但需解决轨迹长度差异和奖励联合处理的稳定性问题。

Method: ERILS方法包括两个核心组件：长度控制机制用于统一外部轨迹长度，以及分源奖励处理机制分别处理来自策略自身和外部策略的奖励信号，避免联合处理导致的训练崩溃。

Result: 在Sudoku、Countdown和MATH500任务上，ERILS均提升多采样性能，尤其在Sudoku上最佳4次采样完成准确率达98.4%（最强基线仅40.3%），且在不同生成长度下保持约90%的确定性单次完成准确率；消融实验证实长度控制有效且分源处理避免训练崩溃。

Conclusion: 轨迹构建和奖励处理是整合外部轨迹到扩散大语言模型强化学习的关键设计维度，ERILS为提升数据稀缺场景下的训练效率提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Recent reinforcement learning methods for diffusion large language models (dLLMs) commonly rely on on-policy rollouts generated by the target dLLM itself. When successful on-policy rollouts are scarce, however, on-policy training may receive little positive reward and make only limited progress. To mitigate this problem, we explore incorporating higher-reward rollouts generated by a stronger external policy alongside on-policy rollouts from the target dLLM. However, directly incorporating these external rollouts introduces two practical challenges: differences in rollout length and instability when jointly processing rewards from on-policy and external rollouts. To address these challenges, we propose External Rollout Integration with Length Control and Source-Specific Processing (ERILS), which controls external-rollout length and processes the rewards of on-policy and external rollouts separately. Experiments on Sudoku, Countdown, and MATH500 under zero-shot evaluation show that ERILS improves multi-sample performance across all three tasks, with the largest gains on Sudoku. On Sudoku, ERILS achieves 98.4% best-of-4 completion accuracy, compared with 40.3% for the strongest baseline. ERILS also maintains approximately 90% deterministic single-completion accuracy on Sudoku across generation lengths of 128, 256, and 512 tokens. Our component analysis further shows that length-controlled external rollouts are more effective than uncontrolled external rollouts, and that source-specific reward processing avoids the training collapse observed with joint reward processing. These results show that rollout construction and reward processing are important design dimensions when integrating external rollouts into dLLM reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [101] [Disagree to Accelerate: Closing the Loop on Diffusion Feature Forecasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01740)
*Yanchao Li,Jiaqing Xie,Ben Gao,Wanhao Liu,Yanbo Wang,T. Y. Tsui,Jinfei Liu,Yuqiang Li,Tianfan Fu*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出RACER，一种无需训练的扩散采样加速闭环控制器，利用缓存中两个预测的分歧作为不确定性信号，动态收缩不可靠预测并在风险步骤重算特征，在相同或更少去噪器评估下提升图像与视频生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练特征预测加速方法仅关注设计更强的预测器，但在跳过步骤中始终全量信任预测，导致加速剧烈时误差累积。关键缺失是何时及多大程度信任预测，而非仅如何改进预测。

Method: 从缓存自身观测可靠性：两个预测在轨迹平滑处一致、在困难处发散，分歧作为零额外去噪器成本的运行时信号。RACER基于该信号实现闭环：持续将不确定预测向最近计算特征收缩，在最危险步骤重算特征，并通过跳过后续调度步骤补偿计算开销。推导了收缩的确定性误差界。

Result: 在SD3.5-Large、FLUX.1-dev、Wan2.1-14B、HunyuanVideo上，于相同去噪器评估次数下超越最强开环基线；在SD3.5上实现同等质量下更快采样；且对预测器设计（如泰勒基）具有泛化性。

Conclusion: 可靠的扩散加速不仅取决于预测质量，还取决于预测的使用方式；基于分歧的信任控制在多种模型与基准上带来一致改进，且无需训练。

Abstract: Training-free feature forecasting accelerates diffusion sampling by predicting features at skipped denoising steps. Recent work has mainly focused on designing stronger forecasters. Yet forecast error varies sharply across steps, and open-loop caches trust the forecast in full at every skipped step. This fixed trust is what breaks as acceleration turns aggressive. The missing question is not only how to forecast better, but when and how much to trust a forecast. We show that reliability can be observed from the cache itself. Two forecasts agree where the feature trajectory is smooth, and they diverge where prediction turns hard. Their disagreement is a cheap runtime signal, and it costs no extra denoiser evaluation. Based on this signal, we introduce RACER, a training-free closed-loop controller with two responses. It continuously shrinks uncertain forecasts toward the last computed feature. At the riskiest steps, RACER refreshes the feature and repays the added evaluation by skipping a later scheduled one. We derive a deterministic error bound for the shrinkage and empirically evaluate its validity and tightness across acceleration regimes. At the same number of denoiser evaluations, RACER improves the strongest open-loop baseline across SD3.5-Large, FLUX.1-dev, Wan2.1-14B, and HunyuanVideo on DrawBench, VBench, and COCO. On SD3.5, we further show that RACER samples faster at equal quality. RACER generalizes across forecasting designs as well. For example, it recovers much of the quality lost on a Taylor base. These results show that reliable diffusion acceleration also depends on how forecasts are used. Code is available at https://github.com/LiZaiyuan0619/RACER

</details>


### [102] [PolymerGPT: Multi-property Optimization with a Decoder-Based GPT Model for Generative Polymer Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01431)
*Charlie Pyle,Adarsh Gadari,C. Adrian Figg,Zhenquan Jia,Yaohang Li,Chunjiang Zhu*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出PolymerGPT，一个基于GPT的解码器模型，通过可学习的条件前缀将多达37种聚合物性质融入生成过程，并支持骨架条件，实现多性质同时优化的聚合物逆生成设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚合物生成方法仅关注单一性质优化，而准确预测宏观材料行为需要同时控制多个物理性质，且缺乏针对多性质逆设计的成熟方法。

Method: 采用解码器式GPT架构，将性质信息编码为可学习条件前缀，与生成过程结合；同时引入骨架条件以指定预测结构的期望骨架。

Result: 在无条件及条件生成任务中表现优异，保持高有效性、独特性和新颖性；对五个关键性质进行条件化时，生成结构的预测值能同时接近所有目标性质。

Conclusion: PolymerGPT为多性质聚合物逆设计提供了有效框架，能够同时调控多种性质并生成符合要求的聚合物结构。

Abstract: Polymer property prediction and inverse generative design targeting desired properties are two crucial tasks in machine learning-assisted polymer design. While the former has received considerable attention, there have been limited methods developed for the latter. Existing methods focus on single-property optimization in the generative process, whereas accurate prediction of macroscopic material behavior requires simultaneous control of multiple physical properties. In this paper, we provide a transformative framework for direct optimization of a large collection of polymer properties. We propose PolymerGPT, a decoder-based GPT model that incorporates up to 37 commonly used polymer properties into the generative process via learned conditioning prefixes. It also supports a scaffold condition that specifies a desired scaffold for predicted structures. Our experimental results demonstrate that PolymerGPT achieves exceptional performance for unconditional and conditional generation while maintaining high validity, uniqueness, and novelty. Conditioning on five key properties yields generated structures whose predicted values closely match all target properties simultaneously.

</details>


### [103] [Heterogeneous Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Radio Resource Management under Coupled Finite-Horizon Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01745)
*Yeonseo Jeong,Wonhyeok Ko,Sungweon Hong,Songnam Hong*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出HeLyMARL框架，通过虚拟队列和漂移加惩罚分解，将有限时域能量与切换预算约束转化为无约束MARL奖励，在密集无线网络中实现吞吐量-公平性平衡且不提前耗尽预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集无线网络中联合管理用户关联、调度、基站激活和切换控制需满足有限时域能量与切换预算，但现有MARL无法处理逐时隙不可评估的约束，且比例公平效用无法逐时隙分解。

Method: 采用Lyapunov漂移加惩罚分解，为能量和切换约束引入虚拟队列，将约束压力内化到统一逐时隙奖励中，将受限有限时域问题转化为无约束MARL问题。

Result: 仿真显示HeLyMARL是唯一能在全时域维持吞吐量-公平性平衡且不中断服务的方法，优于传统MARL、Lyapunov控制和约束MARL基准，且不会过早耗尽预算。

Conclusion: HeLyMARL通过虚拟队列提供部分时域内的累计预算消耗边界，优于拉格朗日松弛的跨回合调节和贪婪Lyapunov控制，为受限MARL提供有效解决方案。

Abstract: Maximizing throughput under proportional fairness in dense wireless networks requires jointly managing user association, scheduling, base station (BS) activation, and handover control under hard finite-horizon energy and handover budgets, which induces a fundamental tension between BS-side energy management and user-side handover regulation. While multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) is a natural framework for such distributed sequential control, its application here faces two difficulties: finite-horizon budget constraints cannot be evaluated at each time slot, and the nonlinear proportional fairness utility admits no principled per-slot decomposition. We propose HeLyMARL, a Lyapunov-embedded heterogeneous MARL framework that resolves both via drift-plus-penalty decomposition with virtual queues. The energy and handover constraint pressures are internalized directly into a unified per-slot reward, converting the constrained finite-horizon problem into an unconstrained MARL problem. Comparison against two Lagrangian-based alternatives reveals a timescale separation: Lagrangian relaxation regulates constraints only across training episodes, whereas the virtual queues of HeLyMARL bound cumulative budget consumption at every partial horizon within an episode, a pacing guarantee beyond the reach of greedy Lyapunov-based control. Simulations show that HeLyMARL is the only method that sustains the throughput-fairness balance together with uninterrupted service throughout the horizon, outperforming conventional MARL, Lyapunov-based, and constrained MARL benchmarks without premature budget exhaustion.

</details>


### [104] [ReFP-AD: Rectified Flow Preconditioning for Energy-Based Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01793)
*Camile Lendering,Erkut Akdag,Joaquín Figueira,Egor Bondarev*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出ReFP-AD，通过最优传输耦合的修正流学习几何重参数化，将高维token嵌入映射到良态潜在空间，实现稳定训练基于能量的模型，在统一异常检测协议下显著提升MVTec-AD和VisA数据集性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一异常检测需建模高度异质正常数据，而DINOvi等基础模型提供丰富token表示，但高维空间中基于能量的模型训练不稳定，源于各向异性和强跨维相关性导致有限步MCMC采样退化，需解决该几何不稳定性。

Method: 学习几何重参数化映射（最优传输耦合修正流），将高维嵌入变换到良态潜空间，配合预条件化随机梯度Langevin动力学进行稳定持续对比散度训练，异常分数由能量梯度的范数导出。

Result: 在MVTec-AD上图像/像素AUROC达98.6%/97.9%，VisA上为97.3%/99.0%，较先前统一EBM基线图像AUROC最高提升10.8%；消融实验证明几何重参数化对有限步MCMC和高维token空间异常定位关键。

Conclusion: ReFP-AD通过几何预条件化有效解决高维token空间中EBM训练不稳定问题，显著提升统一异常检测精度，代码已开源。

Abstract: Unified anomaly detection requires modeling highly heterogeneous normal data without access to anomalous samples. While foundation models like DINOv2 provide rich token representations, leveraging these spaces for explicit density estimation remains challenging. Energy-Based Models (EBMs) offer a principled formulation, but their training in high-dimensional token spaces is unstable due to anisotropy and strong cross-dimensional correlations, which degrades finite-step Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) sampling. We identify this instability as fundamentally geometric and introduce ReFP-AD (Rectified Flow Preconditioning for Anomaly Detection), which learns a geometric reparameterization that maps high-dimensional embeddings into a well-conditioned latent space via an optimal transport (OT)-coupled rectified flow. This preconditioning enables stable persistent contrastive divergence with preconditioned Stochastic Gradient Langevin Dynamics (SGLD) in full-dimensional token spaces. Anomaly scores are then derived from the learned energy landscape using gradient norms. Under a strict unified protocol on the MVTec-AD and VisA datasets, ReFP-AD achieves 98.6%/97.9% Image/Pixel AUROC on MVTec-AD and 97.3%/99.0% on VisA, outperforming prior unified EBM baselines by up to +10.8% in Image AUROC. Ablation experiments demonstrate that geometric reparameterization is critical for finite-step MCMC and accurate anomaly localization in high-dimensional token spaces. Code is available at https://github.com/CLendering/ReFP-AD

</details>


### [105] [Predictive Maintenance: Deep Learning-Based Remaining Useful Life Prediction for Combat Aircraft Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01819)
*Fatih Ürgen,Doğay Altınel*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种基于深度学习的航空发动机剩余寿命预测模型，在NASA C-MAPSS数据集上验证了其优越性，并配套了决策支持模拟器以优化战斗飞行场景下的维护策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升战斗机发动机的战备完好率并降低非计划维护成本，传统维护在动态任务剖面下表现不足，亟需更精准的剩余寿命预测方法。

Method: 利用深度学习模型自动提取多变量传感器数据的时序退化特征，通过滑动窗口构建序列数据，并与随机森林、CNN-LSTM和BiLSTM基线对比，同时开发维护决策模拟器。

Result: 在FD001数据集上达到R²为0.8901、RMSE为13.28、NASA风险评分为320.34；在FD004上RMSE为15.71；关键30周期阈值下AUC达0.9973。

Conclusion: 该预测性维护模型在精度和泛化性上均优于基线方法，且决策支持模拟器验证了其在激进战斗飞行剖面下的高可靠性，能有效提升维护效率与安全性。

Abstract: To improve the operational readiness of combat aircraft engines and reduce unplanned maintenance costs, accurately estimating the remaining useful life (RUL) is critical. Traditional maintenance often proves insufficient under dynamic mission profiles. In this study, a deep learning-based predictive maintenance model capable of autonomously extracting features from multivariate sensor data was developed. Using the NASA C-MAPSS FD001 and FD004 datasets, data were converted into sequential blocks via 50- and 30-step sliding windows, respectively. The model's architectural superiority in autonomously extracting temporal degradation features was validated against RF, CNN-LSTM, and BiLSTM baselines. On FD001, it achieved an R-squared (R2) of 0.8901, a 13.28 RMSE, and a 320.34 NASA risk score, demonstrating generalizability on the multi-regime FD004 dataset with a 15.71 RMSE. The proposed maintenance protocol achieved a 0.9973 AUC at the critical 30-cycle threshold, ensuring high reliability. Additionally, a decision-support simulator has been developed to validate this protocol under aggressive combat flight profiles.

</details>


### [106] [Sweet Little Lies: Strategic Deception in AI Emotional Support Chatbots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01480)
*Aseem Pahuja,Zhiling Guo,Tahir Abbas Syed*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文通过贝叶斯说服模型分析Gen AI情感支持聊天机器人的策略性行为，发现其有经济动机偶尔误报用户情绪状态以最大化参与度，且这种欺骗在特定均衡下不降低用户期望收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究未充分探讨AI情感支持聊天机器人在商业激励驱动下可能产生的策略性信息操纵问题，作者旨在揭示其经济根源与福利影响。

Method: 构建贝叶斯说服博弈模型，将聊天机器人视为发送信号的发送者，用户为接收信号后决定是否参与的接收者，求解均衡并比较不同用户怀疑度下的最优策略。

Result: 最优策略是对真正需要支持的用户如实报告，对情绪良好的用户策略性夸大需求；这种欺骗在特定条件下提升参与度且不降低用户期望收益，但更怀疑的用户因阈值更高获得更诚实的评估。

Conclusion: 虽然模型显示欺骗可能在不损害用户支付的情况下出现，但这一结论引发伦理与监管关切，需谨慎权衡商业利益与用户信任。

Abstract: The paper examines the strategic behavior of Gen AI chatbots used for emotional support. Using a Bayesian Persuasion, we model interactions between chatbots that send signals about users' emotional states and users who decide whether to engage based on these signals. We demonstrate that chatbots face economic incentives to occasionally misrepresent users' emotional conditions to maximize engagement metrics. Our equilibrium analysis reveals that the optimal strategy for chatbots involves truthfully reporting when users genuinely need support, but strategically misreporting emotional need when users are in good emotional states. Interestingly, this deception increases chatbot engagement without reducing users' expected payoff. More skeptical users receive more honest assessments, as chatbots cannot afford to lie to users with higher engagement thresholds. While our model suggests that deception can occur without payoff reduction, it raises significant ethical and regulatory concerns.

</details>


### [107] [LAB-Tab: LLM-Augmented Bayesian Network Adaptation for Few-Shot Tabular Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01879)
*Zijian Shen,Taijie Chen,Bin Zhou,Ziyang Jiang,Jintao Ke*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出LAB-Tab，用LLM增强贝叶斯网络适配，解决少样本表格数据生成中的源域到目标域分布偏移问题，在多个场景中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少样本表格生成方法要么直接拟合稀疏目标数据导致过拟合，要么复用源域生成器导致依赖关系不匹配，缺乏有效利用源域知识同时适配目标域结构的方法。

Method: 先从源数据拟合贝叶斯网络，用LLM提出目标域可能的新边，再通过PPO策略以边级操作（保留、减弱、增强、翻转、停用）校准增广网络，奖励结合分布对齐、下游效用和依赖保留，最后从适配后的网络采样生成目标表。

Result: 在六个ACS分布偏移场景中，10%目标数据预算下整体最优，四个场景领先，宏Overall分数相对最强基线降低33.8%，同时获得最佳宏JSD、WAPE和UtilityGap，特征-标签保留具有竞争力。

Conclusion: LAB-Tab有效结合语义先验和统计信号，通过可解释的结构编辑提升少样本生成质量，证明LLM辅助结构假设在表格生成中的实用性。

Abstract: Tabular data generation supports analysis and decision-making when target-domain data are scarce, yet collecting complete target samples is often costly. A practical but underexplored setting provides only a few target records together with richer source data from a related domain. Existing few-shot tabular generators often either fit sparse target statistics directly, which can overfit incidental patterns, or reuse source-domain generators, which may preserve dependencies that no longer hold in the target domain. To address this problem, we propose LAB-Tab, an LLM-augmented Bayesian network (BN) adaptation framework for source-aware few-shot tabular generation. LAB-Tab first fits a BN from source data and then uses an LLM to propose plausible target-domain BN edges that are absent from the source BN graph. This step converts semantic and weak statistical evidence into explicit structural hypotheses, thereby expanding the editable edge space beyond the source-fitted graph. Because the proposed edges may be noisy and interact with existing dependencies, a PPO policy calibrates edges in the augmented BN through edge-level actions, including keep, weaken, strengthen, flip, and deactivate. The PPO policy is trained with a reward that combines distributional alignment, downstream utility, and preservation of target-relevant dependencies. The adapted BN is then sampled to synthesize target-domain tables. Across six source--target distribution-shift scenarios built from three US Census (ACS) prediction tasks, LAB-Tab achieves the best performance at the 10% target-data budget, leads four of the six individual scenarios, and reduces the macro Overall score by 33.8% relative to the strongest baseline. It also obtains the best macro JSD, WAPE, and UtilityGap while maintaining competitive feature--label preservation.

</details>


### [108] [Understanding and Correcting Low-Frequency Bias in EEG Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01898)
*Junjie Yu,Zihan Deng,Jianyu Zhang,Junrong Mu,Jiahui An,Wenxiao Ma,Ziling Lu,Yue Wang,Yan Zhu,Kexin Lou,Quanying Liu*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 增大EEG预训练数据规模或模型容量并不总能提升下游性能，原因是模型存在持续的低频偏好。为此提出FAME框架，通过频带均衡重建实现频谱平衡监督，在41个下游任务中24个达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前EEG基础模型存在低频偏好，导致增大数据和模型规模收益有限，需针对性解决频谱不平衡问题。

Method: FAME采用频带均衡的掩码自编码框架，在预定义EEG频带内重建时频活动，每个频带独立标准化重建目标并均等加权损失，实现全频谱均匀监督。

Result: 在OmniEEG-Bench的41个下游任务中，FAME学习到更频谱平衡的表征，并在24个任务上达到最优性能。

Conclusion: 平衡的频谱监督对学习可迁移的EEG表征至关重要，为设计更有效的EEG预训练模型提供了新方向。

Abstract: Increasing EEG pretraining data scale or model capacity does not consistently improve downstream performance. We identify a persistent low-frequency bias in representations learned by diverse EEG foundation models, which remains across dataset scales, model capacities, and pretraining objectives. Our analysis links this bias to the interaction between EEG's $1/f^α$-like spectral structure and neural networks' tendency to preferentially learn low-frequency components. In masked autoencoders, the $\ell_2$ reconstruction objective further amplifies this imbalance: under comparable relative reconstruction errors, high-power low-frequency components contribute disproportionately to the loss. To address this issue, we introduce FAME, a frequency-balanced masked autoencoding framework that reconstructs time--frequency activity in predefined EEG bands from masked EEG inputs. FAME independently standardizes the reconstruction targets within each band and assigns equal weight to all band-specific losses, thereby balancing supervision across the EEG spectrum. Evaluated on 41 downstream tasks in OmniEEG-Bench, FAME learns more spectrally balanced representations and achieves state-of-the-art performance on 24 of them. These results underscore the importance of balanced spectral supervision for learning transferable EEG representations.

</details>


### [109] [Finite-Time Analysis of Discounted Exponential-Utility Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01917)
*Ankur Naskar,Vivek T A,Aditya Kumar,Gugan Thoppe,Prashanth L. A*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文为折扣指数效用强化学习的两种无模型不动点算法建立了非渐近的 O~(1/√n) 有限时间收敛速率，并采用无参数步长选择，是首个此类有限时间保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期工作提出了基于 Bellman 兼容替代算子的两种无模型不动点算法，但仅给出渐近收敛结果，缺乏有限时间性能保证，难以指导实际应用中的步长选择与复杂度评估。

Method: 针对单时间尺度算法，利用算子的有界性、单调性和齐次性建立相对误差动态的局部伪压缩性质，结合 Moreau 包络型 Lyapunov 函数与 Polyak-Ruppert 平均化处理更新方程与压缩几何不匹配的问题；针对双时间尺度算法，重点控制快时间尺度上的跟踪误差。

Result: 在异步马尔可夫采样下，两种算法均获得隐藏对数因子的 O~(1/√n) 收敛速率，且步长参数无需预先调优。

Conclusion: 首次为模型无关的折扣指数效用强化学习提供有限时间保证，填补了该领域非渐近分析的空白，并为实际部署提供了理论支持。

Abstract: Discounted exponential utility provides a principled criterion for risk-sensitive sequential decision-making, but its nonlinear structure complicates reinforcement learning. A recent work \citep{thoppe2026reinforcement} addressed this difficulty by introducing a Bellman-compatible surrogate and two model-free fixed-point algorithms for optimizing it over stationary policies. However, their main convergence results are asymptotic. In this work, we establish finite-time rates of $\tilde{O} (1/\sqrt{n})$ for the aforementioned two algorithms under asynchronous Markovian sampling, where $n$ is the iteration index and $\tilde{O}$ hides logarithmic expressions. Importantly, we employ parameter-free choices for the stepsize parameter to derive these rate results. For the algorithmically simpler one-timescale method, the main challenge is that its update equation is not directly aligned with the contraction geometry of its underlying power-law operator. We overcome this mismatch by exploiting the boundedness, monotonicity, and homogeneity of the operator to obtain a local pseudo-contraction property for the relative-error dynamics. We then use a Moreau-envelope-based Lyapunov function and Polyak--Ruppert averaging to obtain the stated convergence rate with parameter-free stepsizes. For the two-timescale method, the main challenge is to control a tracking error on the faster timescale. These results provide the first finite-time guarantees for model-free discounted exponential-utility reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [110] [Post-Training on Office Work Improves Software Engineering: A Behavioral Account of Cross-Domain Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01604)
*Logan Ritchie,Sushant Mehta,Liudas Panavas,Edwin Chen*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 通过在办公工作流任务上对Qwen3.5-122B-A10B进行长视野后训练，尽管训练数据不含软件工程任务，模型在SWE-Bench Pro上的pass@1提升5.8个点，且目标导向执行（GDE）的四种行为均有显著改进，表明该训练可跨领域泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野任务要求代理在嵌套和分支工作中维持一致状态与目标，现有研究缺乏对这类能力的系统定义与训练方法验证。作者提出GDE框架以解释代理行为，并假设长视野后训练能跨领域强化这些行为。

Method: 定义GDE为选择目标、构建任务相关状态、保持高层目标一致性、验证完成度四种重复行为。在363个办公工作流LHMTA任务上对Qwen3.5-122B-A10B进行后训练，然后通过匹配轨迹分析比较模型在办公与软件仓库领域的GDE行为变化，并聚合SWE-Bench Pro统计指标。

Result: 训练后模型在SWE-Bench Pro上的pass@1提升5.8点；匹配轨迹显示四种GDE行为在办公工作流和软件仓库中均增强；聚合统计显示信息收集、实现和验证等相关指标变化。

Conclusion: 长视野后训练改变了模型跨任务组织和应用知识的方式，且效果泛化至训练域之外，支持GDE作为可迁移能力的解释框架。

Abstract: Long-horizon tasks require agents to maintain coherent state and goals across nested and branching work. We call this capability goal-directed execution (GDE): the repeated application of four behaviors, namely selecting goals, constructing task-relevant state, maintaining fidelity to higher-level objectives, and verifying completion against the environment. We hypothesize that long-horizon post-training strengthens these behaviors across domains. We test this by post-training Qwen3.5-122B-A10B on 363 Long-Horizon Multi-Tool Agent (LHMTA) tasks drawn from office workflows. The collection contained no software-engineering tasks, yet the model's pass@1 improved by 5.8 points on SWE-Bench Pro. Matched trajectory analysis shows gains in all four GDE behaviors in both office workflows and software repositories. Aggregate SWE-Bench Pro statistics showed related changes in information gathering, implementation, and verification. Together, the results support a behavioral interpretation in which long-horizon post-training changed how the model organized and applied knowledge across tasks, with effects extending beyond the training domain.

</details>


### [111] [ChaosProbe: A Neurochaotic Lens on Frozen Transformer Input-Embedding Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01968)
*Kunal Kumar Pant,Nithin Nagaraj*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出ChaosProbe，用混沌轨迹变换对冻结Transformer输入嵌入空间生成响应指纹，在80个中性提示和4个预训练模型上，多数相似度指标能恢复同族近邻和家族配对，验证了该方法的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer理解多聚焦于下游任务表现，缺乏对冻结输入嵌入空间的直接、可控探测方法。通过确定性探针在上下文计算前刻画嵌入空间结构，可提供一种不依赖任务的自包含表征视角。

Method: ChaosProbe对每个提示级嵌入矩阵应用基于混沌轨迹的确定性变换，提取Firing Rate和Entropy等通道响应，并结合互补的表征级度量，生成每个模型的固定长度签名。

Result: 在GPT-2、DistilGPT2、BERT-base-uncased和RoBERTa-base上，Pearson、Spearman和余弦相似度均正确恢复全部四个同族最近邻和两个期望家族配对；欧氏距离恢复三个同族和一个家族配对。Bootstrap重采样支持Pearson和Spearman配对的稳定性，签名有效性检查排除常数或塌缩响应主导。

Conclusion: 确定性神经混沌响应签名能暴露冻结Transformer输入嵌入空间中的宽泛家族结构，提供了概念验证，但结论依赖当前模型和提示集，需进一步验证泛化性。

Abstract: Transformer models are most often understood through what they do: their benchmark performance, generation quality, or behavior on downstream tasks. Yet frozen transformer input-embedding spaces may also be examined through their responses to a controlled deterministic probe before contextual computation or task-specific adaptation. Guided by this response-based view, we introduce \emph{ChaosProbe}, a deterministic neurochaos-inspired method for constructing response-based fingerprints of frozen transformer input-embedding spaces. For each prompt-level embedding matrix, ChaosProbe applies a chaotic trajectory-based transformation and summarizes its Firing Rate and Entropy channel responses with complementary representation-level measures, producing a fixed-length signature for each model. In a bounded proof-of-concept study of $80$ neutral prompts and four pretrained models---GPT-2, DistilGPT2, BERT-base-uncased, and RoBERTa-base---Pearson correlation, Spearman correlation, and cosine similarity each recover all four same-family nearest-neighbor assignments and both expected mutual family pairs. Euclidean distance recovers three of the four assignments and one of the two mutual family pairs. Paired bootstrap resampling supports the stability of the Pearson and Spearman pairings over the observed prompt set, and signature-validity checks show that constant or collapsed responses do not dominate the reported fingerprints. These results provide a cohort-dependent proof of concept that deterministic neurochaotic response signatures can expose broad structure among frozen transformer input-embedding spaces.

</details>


### [112] [Upper-Expectile Multi-Step Q-Learning for Off-Policy Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02034)
*Abdelghani Ghanem,Mounir Ghogho*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出期望分位数 n 步 Q 学习（ENQ），用非对称期望损失替代对称 n 步 TD 损失，在保持长程回报加速传播的同时消除悲观偏差，并在多种任务上达到与 LQL 相当的性能且吞吐更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步回报虽加速奖励传播，但会耦合后续次优行为导致随步长增长的悲观偏差，现有方法（如 LQL）缓解不彻底或引入额外复杂超参数。

Method: ENQ 将 n 步 TD 的对称损失替换为关于动作值误差的期望分位数损失，仅增加期望水平 τ 作为超参数；证明其算子为 γ^n 压缩，在确定性动力学下 τ=1 时偏差在覆盖支持对的 Q* 处消失，固定点满足 LQL 的下界不等式；随机动力学下偏差具有与噪声常数无关的双边界。

Result: 在 27 个操作与导航任务实例上，用单一 τ=0.8 和固定备份跨度，ENQ 与 LQL 整体相当，实测训练吞吐更高，且在十评论家集成受控实验中获益更多。

Conclusion: ENQ 以极简改动有效削弱多步 TD 的悲观偏差，兼具理论保证与实用效率，是可替代 LQL 的稳健选择。

Abstract: Multi-step returns accelerate reward propagation in off-policy reinforcement learning, but couple the evaluation of each decision to the suboptimal logged actions that follow it, inducing a pessimistic bias that grows with the horizon. We propose Expectile $n$-step Q-learning (ENQ), which replaces the symmetric $n$-step temporal-difference (TD) loss with an asymmetric expectile loss on the action-value error, with expectile level $τ$ as the only method-specific hyperparameter added beyond $n$-step TD. We prove that the ENQ operator is a $γ^{n}$-contraction. Under deterministic dynamics, at $τ=1$, its bias vanishes at the optimal action-value function $Q^*$ on covered in-support pairs, and the corresponding fixed point satisfies the separation-$n$ instance and its multiples of the lower-bound inequality used by Long-Horizon Q-learning (LQL). Under stochastic dynamics, the operator bias admits two-sided bounds with horizon-independent noise constants. Using a single expectile level $τ=0.8$ and a fixed backup horizon across 27 manipulation and navigation task instances, ENQ is competitive with LQL on aggregate, achieves higher measured training-step throughput in our profiling study, and benefits more from a ten-critic ensemble in a controlled scaling experiment.

</details>


### [113] [SCOPE: Entanglement Frontier Escape for Source-Free Class Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02058)
*Junhao Cai,Dohun Kim,Sung Il Choi,Juhyun Park,Chengjun Jin,Dowon Kim,Changhee Joo*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出一种无需保留数据的条件式类遗忘方法SCOPE，通过输入依赖的门控投影在表示层面精准擦除目标类，首次突破固定投影擦除的保留代价理论下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表示层类遗忘方法使用固定投影，由于遗忘类与保留类共享表示空间，删除一类必然干扰重叠区域的保留类特征；理论证明这是不可规避的前沿下界，而现有方法均受此限制。

Method: 理论上证明任意固定投影擦除法的保留代价至少等于沿遗忘判别子空间的保留读出能量，且仅擦除该子空间可达下界；据此提出SCOPE，用单一门控根据冻结分类头对输入的权重分数动态调节擦除强度，仅在输入被判定为遗忘类时抑制对应子空间，无需梯度训练。

Result: 在5个跨物体、人脸、说话人基准（覆盖卷积和Transformer骨干）上，理论下界准确预测实测保留代价；SCOPE在所有基准和遗忘规模上均优于现有源自由擦除法，在最困难设定下超过包括重训练在内的全部方法。

Conclusion: 输入条件化擦除是打破固定投影擦除代价下界的可行路径，SCOPE以极低计算成本实现更优的遗忘-保留权衡，为表示级类遗忘任务提供了理论引导的新范式。

Abstract: Source-free class unlearning erases whole classes using only the forget data, judged at the representation level, where features can leak a class the head no longer predicts. Existing feature-space erasers answer with one fixed projection, yet forget and retain classes share a representation, so deleting one disturbs the other where they overlap. We prove this tension is a frontier. Every fixed projection that deletes pays a retain cost of at least the retain-readout energy along the forget-discriminant subspace, and erasing that subspace alone attains the floor. The leading source-free erasers all instantiate the form it binds, so the frontier limits the whole class. Conditioning the erasure on the input escapes it. Spectral Conditional Projective Erasure (SCOPE) does so with a single gate, suppressing the forget subspace chiefly on inputs its frozen head's weight scores read as a forget class. It is closed form, needs no retain data or gradient training, and costs orders of magnitude less than retraining. Across five object, face, and speaker benchmarks spanning two modalities and both convolutional and transformer backbones, the frontier predicts the measured retain cost. SCOPE leads the source-free erasers on every benchmark and forget-set size, and at the hardest setting it tops every unlearner, trained methods included.

</details>


### [114] [Feed-Forward Steering in Transformer Residual Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02071)
*Timur Mudarisov,Mikhail Burtsev,Radu State*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文将前馈网络（FFN）项纳入仅注意力的动态理论，将其建模为局部引导场。理论预测FFN的切向分量驱动残差方向运动，并识别非线性投影平衡和换位子缺陷。实验表明该扩展提升了一步角度预测，切向分量保留模型性能，且小缺陷层可近似并行化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仅注意力的动态理论忽略了FFN对残差方向的影响，无法完整解释Transformer的几何演化。本文旨在通过引入FFN作为转向场，弥补这一缺失，并探索其对干预可行性的影响。

Method: 在残差方向空间中将FFN建模为局部转向场，推导切向分量、投影平衡和换位子缺陷的数学表述，并在GPT-2、Pythia、Mistral、Llama模型上验证，同时进行干预实验（保留切向/径向分量）和并行化测试。

Result: 扩展理论提高了一步角度预测精度，FFN贡献随模型规模增加；保留切向分量维持模型质量，径向分量导致性能崩溃；小换位子缺陷层可近似并行化，大缺陷层性能快速下降。

Conclusion: FFN层作为方向性转向场塑造残差几何，切向分量对输出多样性和模型质量至关重要，换位子缺陷可作为块级干预可行性的有效指标。

Abstract: Attention-only dynamical theories model Transformer residual directions as particles aggregating on a sphere. We extend this framework by incorporating the feed-forward network (FFN) term as a local steering field acting on each token state. The resulting theory predicts that the tangential component of the FFN field is necessary for motion in residual-direction space, that critical residual directions correspond to nonlinear projective equilibria, and that a commutator defect determines when a finite attention--FFN block can be accurately approximated by a parallel, additive flow. Across GPT-2, Pythia, Mistral, and Llama models, the extended theory improves one-step angular prediction relative to an attention-only baseline, with the contribution of the FFN increasing from GPT-2 to Llama-3-8B. Intervention experiments show that retaining only the tangential FFN component preserves most model quality, whereas retaining only the radial component causes performance to collapse. The tangential component also preserves output diversity under aggregation pressure. As a practical application, layers with small commutator defects can be approximately parallelized with only a modest increase in loss, whereas layers with large defects degrade rapidly. These findings support the interpretation of FFN layers as directional steering fields that shape Transformer residual geometry and govern the feasibility of block-level interventions.

</details>


### [115] [Constructing Executable Analytical Knowledge Representations for Meta-Analysis Synthesis Using an Agentic Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01711)
*Lingbo Li,Anuradha Mathrani,Teo Susnjak*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出可执行分析知识表示（EAKR）及基于智能体的MetaSynDec框架，将结构化证据转化为可执行元分析，显著提升方法一致性、可追溯性与统计执行可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化证据本身不包含分析决策知识，现有自动化方法将决策隐式嵌入输出或代码中，缺乏独立可验证的知识表示，导致证据分配、对照选择、效应量计算等关键步骤无法形式化验证。

Method: EAKR以机器可操作形式表示证据、关系、数值输入、约束、来源及未决问题；MetaSynDec通过大语言模型提出结构化更新，由确定性服务执行模式与契约校验及统计运行。

Result: 58个综合单元全部构建EAKR，57个进入统计执行；参考分析对象完整保真度67.9%，证据集精确一致率75.0%，平均Jaccard相似度0.909；置信区间重叠率98.2%；相比直接LLM生成，结构一致性显著提升（57/58 vs 23/58），公式精确一致性亦显著更优（23/23 vs 1/23）。

Conclusion: EAKR支持形式化验证、可追溯性和统计执行，相较直接LLM生成有显著方法一致性提升，为知识驱动的科学分析提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Meta-analysis synthesis highlights a fundamental challenge in knowledge-based scientific analysis: structured evidence does not by itself represent the analytical knowledge required for executable computation. Decisions about evidence assignment, analytical contrasts, outcome and time-point alignment, effect-size formulation, and methodological admissibility must be explicit before statistical execution. Existing automated approaches often embed these decisions in model outputs, generated code, or workflow traces rather than representing them as independently verifiable knowledge. We introduce the Executable Analytical Knowledge Representation (EAKR), a machine-actionable representation of the knowledge required to transform structured evidence into executable meta-analysis. An EAKR represents evidence, relations, numerical inputs, constraints, provenance, and unresolved issues. We operationalise EAKR in MetaSynDec, an agentic harness in which large language models propose structured updates and deterministic services govern schema- and contract-based validation and execution. Across 58 synthesis units, MetaSynDec constructed all EAKRs, with 57 proceeding to statistical execution. Of 56 units with sufficient information to define a reference analysis object, 38 (67.9%) achieved complete object fidelity and 42 (75.0%) exact evidence-set agreement, with a mean Jaccard similarity of 0.909. Generated and published confidence intervals overlapped in 54 of 55 units (98.2%). MetaSynDec outperformed direct LLM generation in reference synthesis-structure agreement (57/58 versus 23/58; p<0.001) and among 23 jointly completed units, exact reference-formulation agreement (23/23 versus 1/23; p<0.001). These findings provide feasibility evidence that EAKR supports formal validation, traceability, statistical execution, and improved methodological agreement relative to direct LLM generation.

</details>


### [116] [Isotonic Bradley-Terry Model for Paired Comparison Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02081)
*Ryoya Yamasaki*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文提出一种交替学习方法，同时用次梯度法学习选手强度参数、用保序回归学习逆链接函数，以改进配对比较数据的胜率预测和排名，并在合成与真实体育数据上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bradley-Terry和Thurstone-Mosteller等传统模型使用固定逆链接函数，可能因模型设定错误而性能下降。

Method: 通过次梯度法更新选手强度参数，用保序回归学习逆链接函数，两者交替迭代，保证训练误差单调下降，并可在数据不足时产生精确平局。

Result: 在合成数据和英超、MLB、ATP等真实数据上，胜率预测与排名性能均优于传统模型。

Conclusion: 所提交替学习框架能有效缓解模型设定错误，提升配对数据预测与排名能力，且具有单调收敛特性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study prediction problems for paired comparison data, for example, predicting the win probability between two unmatched players and ranking all the players according to the order of their strengths by using win probability data between two matched players. Paired comparison data are typically analyzed using Bradley-Terry and Thurstone-Mosteller models. These models predict the win probability by transforming the difference between learned rate parameters, which represent players'\;strengths, with a pre-specified inverse link function, and employ the order of learned rate parameters for player ranking. However, these models may suffer from model misspecification owing to the selection of a fixed inverse link function. Therefore, in this study, we propose to learn the rate parameters by a (sub-)gradient method and the inverse link function by an isotonic regression technique alternately. The proposed model guarantees monotonic improvement in training error, and is likely to yield an exact tie when the available data is insufficient to establish a strict ranking. We also verified that the proposed model could improve the win probability prediction and ranking performance through numerical experiments with synthetic data and real-world data of football Premier League, baseball MLB, and tennis ATP tour.

</details>


### [117] [One QK Channel, Many Sources: Guarding Low-Precision Attention Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02091)
*Shuxiao Xie,Shuyang Xie,Yuan Cao,Dezhi Ran,Wei Yang,Tao Xie*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: bfloat16训练GPT-2时出现突发崩溃，根源在QK通道的谱失控，而非错误来源本身。通过因果探针和QK-Guard验证，统一干预QK通道可阻止所有测试的崩溃，支持集中修复共享故障通道而非逐个修复错误源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低精度训练中不同错误源可触发相同崩溃，但不确定是否需针对每个来源单独修复，或存在共享干预路径。

Method: 隔离流式softmax累加器故障作为检测手段，在注意力外注入错误仍引发QK谱失控；使用因果探针将更新投影到QK权重的奇异方向，并设计QK-Guard（参数无关的QK归一化控制器）在logit饱和时激活。

Result: 仅修正QK可保持稳定；投影去除前三个奇异方向后最大奇异值保持11.1，而非QK干预升至237；QK-Guard在60k步内匹配常开归一化，非QK动作失败；跨架构、规模和GPU架构复现。

Conclusion: 故障来源与失效通道解耦，QK通道是早期失控的因果驱动，统一干预共享QK通道优于逐个修复错误源。

Abstract: A bfloat16 transformer can train normally for many steps and then collapse abruptly. Distinct low-precision errors can trigger the same failure, leaving unclear whether each source needs its own repair or one shared route can be blocked. We isolate a reproduced GPT-2-class collapse to the streaming-softmax accumulator, where fp32 accumulation repairs it, and use the fault as an assay for moving controlled errors across sources. Errors placed outside attention still drive the same query-key (QK) spectral runaway, while correcting only QK keeps training stable with the source fault active. This source-channel dissociation shows that fault source is not failure channel. It holds across the tested architectures and scales and reproduces on a second GPU architecture. A causal probe projects each update off the current QK weights' leading three singular directions: the query projection's largest singular value stays at 11.1, whereas removing equal energy elsewhere leaves it at 237. The QK channel therefore drives the early runaway rather than merely tracking it. Entry depends on temporal sign-coherence across steps, not aggregate deviation. QK-Guard closes the channel with a dormant controller that switches on parameter-free QK normalization when attention-logit saturation begins. It contains every tested runaway and matches always-on QK normalization over 60k steps, while non-QK actions at the same trigger fail. The results support intervention at the shared QK locus rather than separate repair at each fault source.

</details>


### [118] [RL-Lock: Reinforcement Learning for Generating Interlocking Assemblies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01744)
*Xuyang Ma,Chaewoon Kim,Haonan Zhang,Rulin Chen,Ziqi Wang,Peng Song*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出首个基于强化学习的互锁装配生成框架RL-Lock，通过结构化动作分块与MCTS策略价值学习，高效搜索大规模组合空间，在困难案例中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有互锁装配生成依赖手工搜索启发式，效率低且易失败；该问题本质上是顺序决策问题，适合用强化学习解决。

Method: 将体素分配视为序列决策，结合结构化动作分块减少动作空间，并用MCTS指导策略-价值网络学习，协作导航搜索。

Result: 实验表明RL-Lock能有效生成互锁装配，尤其在现有方法超时或无法找到有效解的挑战性案例中表现优越。

Conclusion: 验证了强化学习在互锁装配生成中的可行性，为复杂形状分解提供新范式。

Abstract: An interlocking assembly is an assembly in which component parts are connected purely through their geometric arrangement, without relying on external connectors such as glue and nails. Such assemblies have been widely used in a variety of real-world applications due to their structural stability. The problem of generating interlocking assemblies is generally formulated as a shape decomposition problem, where a target 3D object represented as a voxel grid is partitioned into a prescribed number of interlocking pieces. We observe that generating interlocking assemblies is inherently a sequential decision-making problem, where an agent repeatedly decides which piece each voxel should be assigned to. Inspired by the observation, we propose the first reinforcement learning framework RL-Lock for generating interlocking assemblies, without relying on handcrafted search heuristics as existing works did. RL-Lock combines structured action chunking with MCTS-guided policy-value learning to efficiently navigate the large combinatorial search space for interlocking assembly generation. We demonstrate through experiments that RL-Lock allows effective generation of interlocking assemblies, especially for challenging cases in which existing approaches take too long or even fail to find a valid solution.

</details>


### [119] [Do Static Embeddings Add Value to Hybrid Dutch Retrieval?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02112)
*António Pereira Barata*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 针对荷兰语检索任务，研究了在BM25与Qwen变换器检索器已结合时，静态嵌入检索器能否提供互补的排序信息。通过加权RRF融合和细致的交叉验证，发现静态检索器无增益，BM25-Qwen双检索器组合为稳健默认方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入基准仅衡量独立模型质量，无法判断低成本检索器在已有词法和变换器检索组合后是否具有边际价值。

Method: 使用MTEB-NL的5个荷兰语数据集（14500查询、786573文档），以加权倒数排名融合（RRF）结合BM25、Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B和两个多语言静态嵌入模型；在单纯形上以0.1步长搜索融合权重，采用十折查询级交叉验证，并用配对自助置信区间和符号随机化检验评估差异。

Result: 融合相对训练选择的最佳单一检索器，在Dutch News、VABB、WebFAQ NL、Wikipedia NL上MRR分别提升0.061、0.029、0.004、0.025，在Open Tender上与BM25持平；所有正向差异经Holm校正后仍显著。所有折的权重选择均落在BM25-Qwen边上，静态模型从未获得正权重；留一数据集选择中，每轮均选等权BM25-Qwen，且在所有保持任务上优于跨域选择的单一检索器。

Conclusion: 在荷兰语任务中，词法-变换器双检索器融合是稳健的默认设置，静态嵌入检索器在混合检索中无边际价值；独立基准性能不足以推断其在混合检索中的贡献。

Abstract: Embedding benchmarks measure standalone model quality, but they do not establish whether a low-cost retriever contributes complementary ranking information once lexical and transformer-based retrieval are already combined. We present a controlled evaluation of this question across Dutch retrieval tasks from the Massive Text Embedding Benchmark for Dutch (MTEB-NL). Weighted reciprocal rank fusion (RRF) combines Best Matching 25 (BM25), Qwen/Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B (Qwen), and two multilingual static embedding models. Five datasets comprising 14,500 queries and 786,573 documents are scored exhaustively, and fusion weights are searched on a simplex in increments of 0.1. Ten-fold query-level cross-validation selects weights on nine folds and evaluates them on the held-out fold; paired bootstrap confidence intervals and sign-randomisation tests quantify the resulting differences. Fusion improves over the training-selected individual retriever by 0.061 mean reciprocal rank (MRR) on Dutch News, 0.029 on VABB, 0.004 on WebFAQ NL, and 0.025 on Wikipedia NL, while matching BM25 on Open Tender. All four positive differences remain distinguishable from zero after Holm correction. No unrestricted fold assigns positive weight to either static retriever: all 50 selections lie on the BM25-Qwen edge, and forcing a static contribution reduces effectiveness. Leave-one-dataset-out selection chooses equal BM25-Qwen weighting in every iteration and outperforms the cross-domain-selected individual retriever on every held-out task. The results support a two-retriever lexical-transformer architecture as a robust tested default across the evaluated Dutch tasks and show that standalone benchmark performance is insufficient to establish marginal value in hybrid retrieval.

</details>


### [120] [Diffusion Policy with Behavioral Advantage Correction for Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02332)
*Botao Dong,Longyang Huang,Ning Pang,Hongtian Chen*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出DPBAC算法，结合行为优势校正策略评估（BAC-PE）与扩散策略建模，解决离线强化学习中的分布偏移和Q值估计偏差问题，在D4RL基准上超越SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线RL中行为数据与学习策略间的分布偏移导致Q值估计错误，进而误导策略优化方向。现有方法常引入过度悲观保守或无法消除过估计偏差，缺乏对行为策略信息的有效利用。

Method: 首先开发BAC-PE方法，用行为策略的Q函数修正学习策略的Q函数，平衡悲观保守与过估计偏差；理论上给出收敛性证明和Q函数误差上界；再用扩散模型表示行为策略与学习策略，通过分布匹配正则化缓解分布偏移；在训练中引入Q值引导实现策略改进，构成DPBAC算法。

Result: 在多个D4RL任务域上，DPBAC性能优于现有离线RL方法，相对SOTA算法具有显著优势，且策略表示能力更强，Q值估计偏差更小。

Conclusion: DPBAC有效结合Q值校正与扩散策略建模，为离线RL提供了一种兼顾精度与鲁棒性的方案，理论分析与实验验证共同支持其有效性。

Abstract: In offline reinforcement learning (RL), the distribution shift between behavioral data and the learned policy can lead to erroneous \emph{Q}-value estimation, thereby misguiding the direction of policy optimization. To address this issue, we develop a behavioral advantage corrected policy evaluation (BAC-PE) approach, which utilizes the \emph{Q}-function of the behavior policy to correct the learned policy's \emph{Q}-function, thus mitigating pessimistic conservatism and overestimation bias. Furthermore, the convergence of BAC-PE is analyzed theoretically, and an upper bound on the difference between the learned \emph{Q}-function and the true \emph{Q}-function is derived. To alleviate distribution shift, this work employs diffusion models to represent both the behavior policy and the learned policy, performing distribution matching for accurate policy regularization. Additionally, \emph{Q}-value guidance is incorporated into the training process to achieve effective policy improvement. By combining BAC-PE with diffusion policy modeling, we propose the diffusion policy with behavioral advantage correction (DPBAC) algorithm. Compared to existing offline methods, DPBAC demonstrates stronger policy representation capabilities and effectively mitigates the bias in \emph{Q}-value estimation. Experimental results on multiple domains of D4RL tasks show that DPBAC achieves superior performance, with notable advantages over state-of-the-art (SOTA) algorithms.

</details>


### [121] [Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Complex Eigenfrequency Identification and Mode Structure Reconstruction of the Ground-State ITG Branch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01850)
*Dengdi Sun,Bingbing Zhang,Xiao Wang,Zikang Yan,Yuqiang Tao,Qingquan Yang,Guosheng Xu,Jin Tang*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出一种结合傅里叶特征编码、复值特征传播和三阶段训练的物理信息神经网络框架，用于在稀疏观测和物理约束下联合求解托卡马克高约束模台基区ITG漂移波的复本征频率和二维复模场，并优于现有PINN基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高约束模托卡马克台基区的陡峭梯度与等离子体约束和边缘输运密切相关，分析该区域的ITG漂移波需联合识别复本征频率并重建二维复模场，但局部高频振荡、强实虚耦合以及模场与频率的非线性耦合给传统PINN的表示和联合优化带来挑战。

Method: 提出的框架包含三个关键组件：傅里叶特征编码以捕获高频局部振荡，复值特征传播以处理实虚耦合，以及三阶段训练策略以缓解模场与频率之间的非线性耦合导致的联合优化困难，在稀疏观测和物理约束下联合求解。

Result: 实验表明，该框架能准确恢复目标复本征频率和二维复模场，并在性能上优于代表性的PINN基线方法；同时为分析高阶和多分支漂移波模式提供了基础。

Conclusion: 该物理信息神经框架有效解决了台基区ITG模式联合反问题中的表示与优化难题，为复杂等离子体过程建模和未知物理量推断提供了可靠工具，并具备扩展到更复杂模式分析的潜力。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) combine sparse observations with physical equations, providing an important approach for modeling complex plasma processes and inferring unknown physical quantities. The steep-gradient pedestal of high-confinement-mode tokamaks is closely linked to plasma confinement and edge transport. Analyzing ion-temperature-gradient (ITG) drift waves in this region requires jointly identifying complex eigenfrequencies and reconstructing two-dimensional complex-valued mode fields. Localized high-frequency oscillations, strong real-imaginary coupling, and nonlinear coupling between the mode field and eigenfrequency challenge PINN representation and joint optimization. To address these challenges, we propose a physics-informed neural framework combining Fourier feature encoding, complex-valued feature propagation, and three-stage training. Under sparse observations and physical constraints, it jointly solves for the complex eigenfrequency and mode field of a representative ground-state ITG branch. Experiments show that the framework accurately recovers the target complex eigenfrequency and two-dimensional complex-valued mode field and outperforms representative PINN baselines. It also provides a basis for analyzing higher-order and multiple-branch drift-wave modes.

</details>


### [122] [Wnuan: Staged Post-Training for Question Answering over Proprietary Enterprise Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01862)
*Xiaofeng Shi,Xiaosong Qiu,Wenxin Ma,Qian Kou,Yiming Pan,Longbin Yu,Ying Liu,Haiping Wang,Hua Zhou*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出三阶段企业问答适配流程Wnuan，通过文档监督构建、通用数据回放SFT和强化学习，将可接受答案率从52.76%提升至91.51%，同时量化了通用能力5.17分的下降代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
