We find that capable language models adjust recommendations in response to stated utilities, revealing that the same underlying predictions can support markedly different decision policies. These findings show that effective deployment depends not only on improving predictions but also on making decision objectives explicit. More broadly, they suggest that language models for high-stakes applications should be understood and evaluated as probabilistic decision systems whose recommendations depend jointly on predictive performance and explicit utilities.

</details>


### [230] [GraphIR: Architecture-Level Search States for LLM-Guided Neural Architecture Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01633)
*Zhen Liu,Wanqi Zhou,Shuanghao Bai,Yuhan Liu,Jinjun Wang,Jingwen Fu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出GraphIR，一种架构感知的中间表示，通过计算骨架、变异表面和有效性边界三个视图，为LLM引导的神经架构搜索提供变异对齐的候选状态，在120题基准和六个下游任务上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM直接对可执行神经网络程序进行NAS时，代码级灵活性缺乏架构状态，导致LLM需从实现细节推断张量依赖、可编辑组件和兼容性约束，存在表示不匹配问题。

Method: GraphIR包含三个互补视图：计算骨架（张量流）、变异表面（可编辑模块和操作）、有效性边界（接口契约、传播形状和下游依赖），并构建NAS-Dependency基准覆盖六种依赖推理维度。

Result: 在NAS-Dependency诊断中，GraphIR在识别精确生产者出现、追踪依赖传播、诊断接口和失败风险方面表现突出；在六个下游基准（含CLRS）中取得最佳整体搜索性能，模型大小相当且端到端NAS效率良好。

Conclusion: 面向变异的架构状态为可执行神经程序与LLM引导的架构进化提供了有效接口。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) enable neural architecture search (NAS) directly over executable neural network programs. However, code-level flexibility does not provide the architecture state needed for effective mutation: LLMs must infer tensor dependencies, editable components, and compatibility constraints from implementation details. To address this representation mismatch, we propose GraphIR, an architecture-aware intermediate representation that supplements executable programs with a mutation-aligned candidate state. GraphIR organizes each candidate through three complementary views: a computation skeleton describing tensor flow, a mutation surface exposing editable modules and operations, and a validity envelope capturing interface contracts, propagated shapes, and downstream dependencies. To evaluate our method, we construct NAS-Dependency, a 120-question benchmark covering six complementary dependency-reasoning dimensions. The diagnostic shows that GraphIR is particularly effective at identifying exact producer occurrences, tracing dependency propagation, and diagnosing interface and failure risks. Across six downstream benchmarks including CLRS, GraphIR achieves the best overall search performance while maintaining comparable model size and favorable end-to-end NAS efficiency when integrated into OpenEvolve. These results show that a mutation-oriented architecture state provides an effective interface between executable neural programs and LLM-guided architecture evolution.

</details>


### [231] [Evaluating Forecasting Techniques for Hardware Errors on a Large-scale HPC System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01648)
*Kaiyuan Liao,Xiwei Xuan,Tanwi Mallick,Kevin Brown,Christopher D. Carothers,Kwan-Liu Ma*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 基于Theta超算7年生产日志，系统评估经典统计与深度学习模型在HPC硬件错误时序预测中的适用边界，发现预测效果高度依赖错误的时间结构：规律且结构稳定的错误可被LSTM和Transformer有效建模，而稀疏突发型错误难以预测，研究旨在提供经验性指导而非部署方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HPC系统硬件错误日志蕴含早期异常信号，但现有预测方法在硬件错误动力学上的应用边界不明确，缺乏对‘何时预测有效’的系统性实证认知。

Method: 使用Theta超算7年生产日志，对比经典统计模型与深度学习（LSTM、Transformer）的预测性能，并引入时间特征工程，按错误时序结构（规律型vs稀疏突发型）分层评估。

Result: 预测有效性强烈依赖错误序列的时序结构：LSTM与Transformer在规律性、结构性稳定的错误上精度高，而稀疏突发型错误预测仍失败。

Conclusion: 不提出可部署的故障预测框架，而是提供预测有效性的经验边界，并指明通过时序结构识别与特征增强提升HPC硬件错误预测精度的潜在方向。

Abstract: Hardware error logs in high-performance computing (HPC) systems provide early signals of abnormal behavior, yet there remain challenges in effectively forecasting these errors using modern predictive methods. This work investigates the boundaries of applying time series forecasting to HPC hardware error dynamics. We use seven years of production logs from the Theta supercomputer to evaluate the predictive efficacy of classical statistical and deep learning models. Our results show that forecasting effectiveness depends strongly on the temporal structure of the error series: regularly occurring and structurally stable errors can be modeled accurately, particularly by LSTM and Transformer architectures with temporal features, while sparse and burst-dominated errors remain difficult to predict. Rather than proposing a deployment-ready failure prediction framework, this study provides empirical guidance on when forecasting is effective and highlights potential directions for improving forecasting accuracy in HPC hardware error analysis.

</details>


### [232] [CraftAlign: Feature-Grounded Evaluation and Revision Guidance for AI Stories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01377)
*Yang Yang,Boyun Xu,Shaofeng Liang,Yun Han,Zining Zhong,Songning Lai,Kaishen Yuan,Yutao Yue*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: CraftAlign是一个框架，通过预测304个写作特征并利用能量模型对齐人类写作模式，为AI故事提供评估和重写指导，实验证明其能有效区分人机写作并提升改写质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型生成的故事常显公式化，而检测与修订方法局限于整体评分或局部修改，难以支持多种改写策略或全局性调整信息释放、因果组织和结局处理。

Method: CraftAlign包含两个学习模块：一个基于Qwen3.5-9B的特征估计器预测304个显式写作特征，一个类条件能量模型根据原提示评分特征配置；推理时应用结构化扰动，选择使特征向人类模式移动的变更，并转化为自然语言指导供编辑器整体重写。

Result: 实验显示CraftAlign能准确区分人类和AI写作模式，其指导在多个编辑器和人工研究中均优于修订基线。

Conclusion: CraftAlign通过可解释的多特征对齐和引导式重写，有效提升AI故事的自然度和人类风格一致性，为故事修订提供了新的框架思路。

Abstract: Large language models can now generate fluent and complete stories, yet many outputs still feel formulaic and unnatural because of cliches, over-explanation, linear causal progression, and stereotyped endings, an immediately recognizable AI flavor. Existing detection and evaluation methods often stop at source labels or holistic scores, while revision methods typically target predefined issues through localized edits, limiting their ability to support multiple plausible revision strategies or guide story-wide changes in information release, causal organization, and ending treatment. We introduce CraftAlign, a framework that aligns AI stories with the craft of human storytelling by both assessing Human/AI writing patterns and providing revision guidance. CraftAlign comprises two learned modules and an inference-time guidance pipeline. A feature estimator built on Qwen3.5-9B predicts 304 explicit writing features spanning style and narrative. A class-conditional energy model scores the resulting feature configuration against Human and AI writing patterns, conditioning on the original writing prompt when available. At inference time, CraftAlign applies schema-valid structured perturbations, selects changes that move the feature configuration toward the Human writing pattern, and converts them into natural-language guidance for a separate editor to rewrite the full story. Experiments show that CraftAlign accurately distinguishes Human and AI writing patterns and that its guidance outperforms revision baselines across editors and in a human study.

</details>


### [233] [KoVRE: Training an Efficient Embedding Model for Korean Visual Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01389)
*Yongbin Choi,Gyuho Shim,Youngjoon Jang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出KoVRE，一个面向韩语视觉文档检索的单向量检索模型，通过双语监督和训练策略优化，在不扩大骨干网络或使用多向量表示的情况下，显著超越更强的基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉文档检索模型和训练资源以英语为主，且高性能系统依赖大规模骨干网络或存储密集的多向量表示，缺乏针对韩语的有效解决方案。

Method: 使用708,729个韩英查询-页面配对训练，采用正感知硬负样本挖掘，并系统分析训练数据构成、硬负样本处理及基于重排序器的知识蒸馏策略。

Result: 在韩语视觉文档检索基准上，2B模型大幅优于基础骨干模型，并超过8B单向量模型和强多向量基线。

Conclusion: 针对性的双语监督和精心设计的训练策略，可以在不扩展骨干规模或多向量表示的情况下，构建跨文档领域高效且有效的韩语视觉文档检索模型。

Abstract: Visual Document Retrieval (VDR) directly matches text queries against document images, preserving visual and structural information that may be lost during text extraction. However, existing VDR models and training resources remain predominantly English-centric, while many high-performing systems rely on massive backbones or storage-intensive multi-vector representations. To address these limitations, we introduce KoVRE: Korean Visual Document Retrieval Embedding, a single-vector retriever for Korean visual documents, alongside a comprehensive training recipe. We train the model on 708,729 Korean and English query-page pairs using positive-aware hard-negative mining and conduct controlled analyses of training-data composition, hard-negative treatment, and reranker-based knowledge distillation. Across Korean visual document retrieval benchmarks, our 2B model substantially improves over the base backbone model, outperforming both its 8B single-vector counterpart and a strong multi-vector baseline. These results demonstrate that targeted bilingual supervision and our carefully designed training strategies can produce a highly effective Korean VDR model across diverse document domains, without requiring a scaled-up backbone or multi-vector representations.

</details>


### [234] [Reusing Rollouts under Policy Lag: Prefix-Normalized Policy Optimization for LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01418)
*Wenhao Zhang,Yibo Xie,Rui Wang,Jiahua Yang,Lei Jiang,Zibo Yang,Yawei Wang,Jiali Xu,jasperawang,Haoyang Long,Huan Xiong,alantzhao*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出Prefix归一化策略优化（PNPO），用几何平均似然比压缩累积重要性比值的动态范围，在离线策略强化学习中提升长上下文数学推理性能，四epoch时比GSPO高3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归rollout生成计算开销大，复用批次做多次更新可摊薄成本，但多次更新后策略偏离行为策略，精确的累积重要性比值乘积形式动态范围过大，需要更稳定的离线校正方法。

Method: PNPO在每个因果前缀上使用似然比的几何均值替代累积比值，保持位置对前缀的依赖同时压缩log权重尺度，在控制实验中用1或4个策略更新epoch诱导不同的离线程度进行对比。

Result: 4 epoch下PNPO在三个基准上的Avg@32平均峰值50.24，比GSPO高3.00个百分点；在匹配的2400次更新预算下，4 epoch PNPO 150个批次达到49.66，与1 epoch 600个批次的49.56相当。

Conclusion: 初步证据表明，训练进一步离线时PNPO相比GSPO有优势，但1 epoch时优势不明显，值得进一步研究。

Abstract: Autoregressive rollout generation is a major computational cost in reinforcement learning for large language models. Reusing each rollout batch for additional learner updates amortizes this cost, but later updates become increasingly off-policy as the learner departs from the behavior policy. At a token position, exact off-policy correction must account for both the current action and the probability of reaching its prefix. The cumulative importance ratio provides this correction, but its product form can produce an unwieldy dynamic range. We study Prefix-Normalized Policy Optimization (PNPO), which replaces the cumulative ratio with the geometric mean of likelihood ratios along each causal prefix, preserving causal-prefix dependence at each position while compressing the log-weight scale. In controlled long-context mathematical reasoning experiments, we induce two off-policy regimes by using one or four policy-update epochs per rollout batch. PNPO does not consistently outperform GSPO with one epoch. With four epochs, it attains the highest observed Avg@32 on each benchmark; the unweighted mean of the three independently selected benchmark peaks is 50.24, 3.00 percentage points above GSPO. Under a matched 2,400-update budget, four-epoch PNPO reaches a final macro Avg@32 of 49.66 after 150 rollout batches, comparable to the 49.56 reached after 600 batches with one epoch. These results provide preliminary evidence that PNPO can be advantageous as training moves further off-policy.

</details>


### [235] [Toward Plasticity-Preserving KL Regularization for Capability Retention in LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01743)
*Li Wang,Xiaodong Lu,Xiaohan Wang,Jiajun Chai,Wei Lin,Tianhao Peng,Guojun Yin*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出正确性条件KL正则化(CoKL),将全响应分布的正则约束收窄为正确性条件分布上的约束,在保持原有能力的同时减少对目标任务学习的干扰,实验证明其优于现有正则方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准全策略KL正则化约束整个响应分布,可能不必要地限制探索和目标学习,且当参考策略不完美时会导致严格的最优正确性差距。

Method: CoKL将保留约束从完整输出分布改为正确性条件响应分布,使用前向KL散度并推导出实用的有限组训练目标,在总体层面解耦正确响应的总概率与其条件分布。

Result: 在受控多解环境和多模型规模的持续后训练中,CoKL比现有正则方法更好地平衡目标任务提升与先前能力保留。

Conclusion: CoKL作为一种更精确的条件正则化框架,能有效避免全策略KL的局限,为LLM后训练中的能力保持提供了更优方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a central paradigm for large language model (LLM) post-training, but optimization toward new objectives can degrade capabilities already present in the base model. KL regularization is widely used to mitigate such forgetting by constraining policy drift toward a reference model. However, standard full-policy KL regularization constrains the entire response distribution and may unnecessarily restrict exploration and target-task learning. This raises a natural question: can a more precise constraint preserve existing capabilities while minimizing interference with learning new tasks? To this end, we propose \underline{Co}rrectness-Conditioned \underline{KL} Regularization (CoKL), a conditional regularization framework that narrows the preservation constraint from the full output distribution to correctness-conditioned response distributions. We instantiate CoKL with forward KL divergence and derive a practical finite-group training objective for RL-based LLM post-training. At the population level, CoKL decouples the total probability assigned to correct responses from their correctness-conditioned distribution, thereby regularizing the relative probability allocation among reference-supported correct responses without directly anchoring incorrect outputs or total correctness mass. We further show that full-policy forward and reverse KL regularization induce a strict optimal correctness gap when the reference policy is imperfect, whereas CoKL avoids this limitation. Experiments in controlled multi-solution environments and continual post-training settings across multiple model scales demonstrate that CoKL achieves a more favorable balance between target-task improvement and prior-capability retention than existing regularization methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/Lumina04/CoKL.

</details>


### [236] [A New Theory of Value for Post-AGI Economics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01432)
*Keyun Ruan*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出繁荣价值理论（FVT），以应对AGI可能颠覆传统经济价值理论后的经济学基础，主张将价值定义为对个人与社区在行星边界内繁荣能力的反事实、分配敏感贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AGI可能消除劳动、专业、信息等稀缺性，导致市场价格、劳动投入、显示偏好等与传统价值理论脱节，无法衡量人类与社会真实福祉，亟需新的价值理论。

Method: 综合AI经济学、福祉与能力理论、自动化、数字估值和生态经济学，区分价值创造与价值捕获，将价格、利润等视为部分信号；引入从稀缺到治理丰裕、从交易到转型、从零和到正和无限博弈的范式转变，基于繁荣指标（Flourishing Metrics）和繁荣回报（RoF）构建分层体系架构。

Result: 提出多维、维护能动性、再生性、在临界阈值不可补偿的价值框架，并设计适用于企业、政府、工作转型、AI治理和国民核算的分层架构。

Conclusion: 后AGI经济学的核心问题不是最大化产出，而是将充裕智能转化为持久、公平分配的人类、社会与行星繁荣。

Abstract: Artificial general intelligence (AGI) may weaken scarcities in labour, expertise, information, and productive capability that underpin established theories of economic value. If cognitive work becomes widely automatable, market price, labour input, revealed preference, profit, and gross output may diverge sharply from human and societal benefit. This paper develops Flourishing Value Theory (FVT) as a foundation for post-AGI economics. FVT defines value as the counterfactual, distribution-sensitive contribution of a system, institution, asset, or intervention to the durable capabilities of persons and communities to flourish within social and planetary constraints. It treats societal value as multidimensional, agency-preserving, regenerative, and non-compensatory at critical thresholds. Drawing on the economics of AI, welfare and capability theory, automation, digital valuation, and ecological economics, the paper distinguishes value creation from value capture and retains price, profit, productivity, and GDP as partial signals rather than final measures of progress. It develops the shifts from scarcity to governed abundance, transaction to transformation, and zero-sum rivalry to positive-sum and infinite-game dynamics, with collective expansion of consciousness as an agency-preserving form of regenerative value. Building on Flourishing Metrics and Return on Flourishing (RoF), it proposes a layered architecture for firms, governments, work transitions, AI governance, and national accounting. The central post-AGI economic problem is not how to maximise output, but how to convert abundant intelligence into durable, fairly distributed human, societal, and planetary flourishing.

</details>


### [237] [Beyond Routing Saturation: A Long-Horizon Class-Incremental Perspective on Expert Routing in Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01437)
*Huiyu Yi,Yongqi Xu,Bogang Zhang,Dunwei Tu,Xu Zhiming,Zhen-Hao Xie,Baile Xu,Furao Shen*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出FLEX基准（34任务长跨度MCIT），揭示专家路由中的任务识别挑战，并将渐进式LoRA路由形式化为多模态类增量学习（MCIL）框架，迁移4种CIL方法提升路由性能，严格LoRA匹配最高提升16.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MCIT基准存在文本指纹泄漏任务身份和短序列（4-10任务）导致路由饱和，掩盖了长跨度路由的核心难题，即任务识别问题未充分探索。

Method: 构建FLEX基准：削弱文本指纹、规范化外部模板、扩大专家池；将渐进式LoRA路由建模为软任务-类MCIL，每个任务为增量路由类，完整得分分布决定LoRA混合权重，硬路由为离散特例；并设计PureLoRA基线，适配4种CIL方法到4种MCIT框架。

Result: 插件式路由器在严格LoRA匹配上最高提升16.3个百分点，整体MacroScore最高提升4.6个百分点。

Conclusion: FLEX暴露了长跨度MCIT中的任务识别难题，MCIL公式为迁移CIL方法到专家路由提供了原则性接口，显著提升路由性能而不改动LoRA专家或生成流程。

Abstract: Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning (MCIT) enables multimodal large language models to acquire new tasks sequentially while retaining previously learned capabilities. Many recent methods maintain task-specific LoRA experts and route each input to one or more experts at inference. Yet the task-identification problem underlying expert routing remains under-explored. We show that routing is nearly saturated on widely used MCIT benchmarks. Textual fingerprints that leak task identity and short 4--10-task sequences with few competing experts jointly obscure the long-horizon routing problem. To expose this challenge, we introduce FLEX (Fingerprint-reduced Long-horizon Expert eXamination), a 34-task long-horizon MCIT benchmark with weakened textual fingerprints. FLEX groups tasks with similar instruction and answer formats but diverse visual and knowledge domains, normalizes their outer templates, and evaluates routing over a substantially larger expert pool. Crucially, we formulate progressive-LoRA routing as soft task-as-class Multimodal Class-Incremental Learning (MCIL): each task defines an incremental routing class, whose complete score distribution supplies the LoRA mixture weights, with hard routing as a discrete special case. FLEX exposes this expanding task-identification challenge, while the MCIL formulation provides a principled interface for transferring CIL methods to expert routing. We instantiate PureLoRA as a controlled baseline and adapt four CIL methods to four MCIT frameworks without modifying their LoRA experts or generation pipelines. Our plug-in routers improve strict LoRA matching by up to 16.3 percentage points and overall MacroScore by up to 4.6 points. Code is available at: https://github.com/RINC-CL/FLEX

</details>


### [238] [Multi-Source Dynamic Graph Learning for Compound-Flood Forecasting in Managed Coastal Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01775)
*Liangjun You,Min Wu,Orlando Woods,Dongsheng Luo*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出锚定预测框架，通过状态与超前时间依赖的有界残差校正整合多站信息，在保持局部时间预测稳定性的同时，提升持续高水位平顶的预测可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测模型的全局误差指标掩盖了高水位平顶的预测缺陷，且单站记录无法完全表征分布式水文气象与运行信号，而无约束的多站信息融合会损害局部时间预测的稳定性。

Method: 构建基于多源态势表示的锚定预测框架，利用状态与超前时间依赖的有界残差校正，自适应校准站间关系与校正尺度，实现有选择的多站动态条件整合。

Result: 实验表明该方法在维持常规水文条件下高精度的同时，显著提升持续高水位平顶的预测可靠性。

Conclusion: 该框架支持洪水预警与水资源管理决策，为多站信息融合与事件尺度评估提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Compound flooding in managed coastal systems is influenced by hydrological conditions and water-management activity observed across multiple monitoring stations. Current forecasting models can capture temporal dependencies with low average errors, but global error metrics may conceal poor reproduction of prolonged high-water plateaus that are relevant to flood early warning. Because hydrometeorological and operational signals are distributed across heterogeneous gages, single-site records do not fully represent high-water dynamics. Nevertheless, unconstrained fusion of cross-site signals can degrade the stability of local temporal forecasts. This work proposes an anchored forecasting framework that incorporates cross-site information through state- and lead-dependent bounded residual corrections. A multi-source regime representation constructed from hydrometeorological and operational observations adaptively calibrates inter-site relationships and correction scales, enabling targeted cross-site adjustment while preserving the local temporal forecast as a stable anchor. Beyond conventional global error statistics, we evaluate event-scale high-water characteristics through the temporal alignment of forecasted and observed high-water processes. Experiments demonstrate that selectively integrating multi-station dynamic conditions improves the prediction reliability of sustained high-water plateaus while maintaining high accuracy during routine hydrological conditions, supporting flood early warning and water-management decision support.

</details>


### [239] [Loud or Silent? A Reusable Framework for Per-Modality Failure Analysis in Multimodal Clinical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01462)
*Quang Bui,Shlok Jaiswal,Samuel Paik-Heintz,Kevin Zhou,Kaushik Madapati,Krittaphas Chaisutyakorn,Noah Dane Hebdon,Dimitrios Proios,Sebastián Andrés Cajas Ordóñez,Kacper Dobek,Boya Zhang,Aly Dhedhi,Ahram Han,Kushul Reddy Palakala,Rahul Gorijavolu,Jacques Kpodonu,Leo Anthony Celi*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一个模型无关的模态失败分析框架，用于在部署中缺失模态时，按样本分类失败类型、构建模态互补矩阵并区分响亮/静默失败，并在超声心动图和心电图上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态临床模型常假设所有模态存在，但实际部署中模态可能缺失；现有评估仅关注准确率下降，未回答哪个模态导致失败、失败是有预警还是静默发生。

Method: 给定N个模态嵌入、掩码感知探针和标签，输出逐样本失败分类、逐模态互补矩阵（归因错误）以及基于部署可观测信号的响亮/静默丢失剖面。

Result: 在合成数据上恢复种植的模态主导性和互补子集；在MIMIC-IV配对队列的冻结嵌入上，丢弃超声心动图时错误率几乎翻倍（n=245）。

Conclusion: 该框架可复用且模型无关，能验证逐样本归因；同时发现超声心动图与心电图的窄重叠本身是临床部署的重要发现。

Abstract: Multimodal clinical models are usually judged on accuracy with every modality present, but deployment removes modalities; an echocardiogram is often unavailable where an ECG is routine. Two questions then matter beyond the size of the accuracy loss: which modality was responsible, and whether the model fails loudly or silently once that modality is dropped. The distinction is per-example and modality-level, and is separate from post-hoc feature attribution (e.g. SHAP). Models are replaced often; the evaluation that answers these questions is reused. We present a model-agnostic modality-failure framework: given N modality embeddings, any mask-aware probe, and labels, it returns a per-example failure taxonomy, a per-modality complementarity matrix that attributes error to modalities, and a loud-vs-silent dropout profile separating monitorable failures from those that pass unflagged far from the decision boundary, using only deployment-observable signals. We release it as a small, unit-tested harness and validate it against planted ground truth. Across seeds it recovers that planted modality dominance and complementary subset, reports per-modality loud-vs-silent rates, and scales to a three-modality complementarity matrix; because the planted structure is known by construction, this validates recovery of per-example attribution rather than clinical performance. We then instantiate the framework on frozen EchoJEPA and HuBERT-ECG embeddings for LVEF and the EF <= 40% HFrEF gate over a paired MIMIC-IV cohort, where on the held-out test split (n = 245) dropping echo nearly doubles error. The narrow echo-to-ECG overlap that bounds cohort size is itself a deployment finding for cardiac foundation models. All of our work can be found at https://github.com/criticaldata/PRIMED-AI.

</details>


### [240] [Where Reasoning Diverges: Localized Multi-Agent Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01463)
*Weijun Gao,Xiang Ding,Tiancheng Xing,Haoyang Liu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出局部化多智能体辩论（LMAD）协议，将智能体轨迹表示为类型化节点，定位最早冲突并仅针对局部段落进行辩论，通过受保护扩展维护已接受步骤，在四个多跳问答基准上以单一固定配置和十种骨干模型取得最高平均裁判准确率，比最强常规基线高出最多7.20个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体辩论在分歧仅涉及少数中间主张时仍交换完整推理轨迹，导致计算冗余且可能重新打开已解决的步骤。

Method: LMAD将智能体轨迹表示为类型化节点，检测最早冲突点，并限制辩论仅针对相应的局部轨迹段；通过受保护的解决方案扩展共享已提交状态，使后续冲突在不重开已接受步骤的前提下得到处理。

Result: 在四个多跳问答基准上，使用十种来自四个模型家族的骨干模型评估单一固定LMAD配置，其宏平均裁判准确率在全部十种骨干上最优，相比最强常规基线最多提升7.20个百分点。

Conclusion: 局部化冲突处理可显著提升多智能体辩论的准确性与效率，为多跳推理提供更稳健的推理时协议。

Abstract: Multi-agent debate commonly exchanges complete reasoning traces even when disagreements concern only a few intermediate claims. We introduce Localized Multi-Agent Debate (LMAD), an inference-time protocol that represents agent traces as typed nodes, locates their earliest conflict, and restricts debate to the corresponding local segments. Guarded resolution extends a shared committed state so that later conflicts can be addressed without reopening accepted steps. We evaluate a single fixed LMAD configuration on four multi-hop question-answering benchmarks using ten backbones from four model families. Our method achieves the highest macro-averaged judge accuracy across all ten backbones, outperforming the strongest conventional baseline by up to 7.20 percentage points.

</details>


### [241] [LEAP: Lean Environment-Feedback via Adaptive Pruning for Code RL in GPU Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01804)
*Tankun Li,Zhi Chen,Yaohua Tang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: LEAP是一个针对低层系统编程（如CUDA内核生成）的多轮强化学习框架，通过难度条件剪枝和基于排名的奖励设计，解决了二进制奖励稀疏和编译延迟高的问题，实现了更快的收敛和更强的调试能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL框架（如GRPO）依赖规则验证沙箱，在低层系统编程中面临二进制通过/失败奖励导致的信号稀疏、多轮环境反馈的编译延迟高以及轨迹间奖励稀释等严重挑战，亟需可扩展且计算高效的方法。

Method: LEAP引入难度条件剪枝（DCP），动态跳过简单或完全失败的任务，聚焦高价值复杂任务；同时提出基于排名的奖励公式，通过GRPO组内成对锦标赛结果推导尺度无关的相对优势，自动惩罚简单任务上的token低效并强化困难分布的梯度。

Result: 实验表明LEAP在首轮熟练度和多轮调试恢复力上表现优异，且比未剪枝的多轮基线收敛更快，验证了其在低层代码RL中的实用性和高效性。

Conclusion: LEAP为低层硬件对齐的多轮RL提供了一种可扩展且计算高效的实用范式，有效缓解了信号稀疏和延迟问题，未来可推广至更多低层编程任务。

Abstract: Post-training large language models (LLMs) via reinforcement learning (RL) has significantly advanced code generation capabilities. To bypass the heavy memory footprint of critic networks, current state-of-the-art frameworks leverage critic-free paradigms like Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) tied to rule-based verification sandboxes. However, applying these frameworks to low-level systems programming, such as CUDA kernel generation-presents severe challenges: binary pass/fail rewards introduce severe signal sparsity, while multi-turn environmental feedback loops suffer from prohibitive compilation latencies and reward dilution across trajectories. In this work, we introduce LEAP (Lean Environment-Feedback via Adaptive Pruning), a scalable and computationally efficient multi-turn RL framework optimized for low-level hardware accelerator alignment. LEAP features Difficulty-Conditioned Pruning (DCP), a dynamic gating mechanism that adaptively cuts off simple and overly catastrophic tasks from multi-turn expansion, focusing resource-heavy compilation and hardware exploration exclusively on high-value, complex tasks. To fully operationalize these paths without manual hyperparameter engineering, we propose a Rank-Based Reward formulation. By deriving scale-free relative advantages from pairwise tournament outcomes within the GRPO rollout group, our method inherently penalizes token inefficiency on simple prompts while maximizing learning gradients on challenging distributions. Empirical evaluations show that LEAP achieves superior first-turn proficiency and robust multi-turn debugging resilience while converging faster than unpruned multi-turn baselines, establishing a practical paradigm for low-level code RL.

</details>


### [242] [WorldDynCache: Risk-Controlled Latent Dynamics Approximation for Diffusion World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01845)
*Leyang Chen,Junyi Wu,Shaoqiu Zhang,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出WorldDynCache，一种风险控制的潜在动力学近似框架，通过轻量级风险估计器和条件/相位感知的潜在代理，在不增加Transformer推理的情况下加速扩散世界模型，在HunyuanVoyager-13B和Aether-5B上分别实现4.92倍和2.15倍加速，且生成质量优于现有缓存方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散世界模型生成高质量未来帧，但重复的Transformer评估导致推理极慢。现有缓存方法存在缺陷：要么忽略跨步近似误差的累积，要么无法捕捉潜在演化方向随相位或条件的改变，导致生成质量下降。

Method: 包含两个核心组件：1) 轻量级潜在转移风险估计器，跟踪近似缺陷的未来累积影响，并通过精确锚点处的反事实缺陷校准预测；2) 条件与相位感知的提升潜在代理，在不额外进行Transformer评估的情况下近似潜在演化。

Result: 在HunyuanVoyager-13B和Aether-5B上分别获得4.92倍和2.15倍加速，在WorldScore、PSNR、SSIM和LPIPS指标上均达到最优生成质量，优于所有对比的缓存方法。

Conclusion: WorldDynCache通过风险控制的潜在动力学近似，有效解决了扩散世界模型推理慢且现有缓存方法忽略累积误差和相位/条件影响的问题，实现了速度与质量的双重提升。

Abstract: Diffusion world models generate high-quality futures, but re- peated transformer evaluations make inference prohibitively slow. Existing caches reuse intermediate features, selectively update tokens, or reuse and extrapolate denoising outputs ac- cording to local drift or short native-space histories. These criteria can miss both approximation-induced latent transition defects that accumulate across skipped steps and phase- or condition-dependent changes in the direction of latent evo- lution. We propose WorldDynCache, a risk-controlled latent dynamics approximation framework with two core compo- nents. First, a lightweight latent-transition risk estimator tracks the accumulated future impact of approximation defects and calibrates its predictions against counterfactual defects ob- served at exact anchors. Second, a condition- and phase- aware lifted latent surrogate approximates latent evolution without extra transformer evaluations. On HunyuanVoyager- 13B and Aether-5B, WorldDynCache achieves 4.92 times and 2.15 times speedups, respectively, while attaining the best gen- eration quality among the compared caching methods across WorldScore, PSNR, SSIM, and LPIPS.

</details>


### [243] [V-Mem: Modality-Routed Retrieval for Long-Term Multimodal Agentic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01543)
*Dingyi Kang,Dongming Jiang,Yi Li,Guanpeng Li,Bingzhe Li*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出V-Mem多模态智能体记忆系统，通过按模态路由检索和生成锚点，解决多模态对话中跨模态检索失败问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统假设查询与证据在索引空间中邻近，但多模态场景下存在模态间隙和相似性-相关性间隙，导致视觉问题检索失败。

Method: 按查询和目标证据模态划分检索路径，用轮次内匹配跨越模态间隙；用LLM生成假设字幕或富化锚点（文本加图像关键词）跨越相似性-相关性间隙。

Result: 在Mem-Gallery上LLM评判得分0.82（次优0.56），图像问题0.87（基线均低于0.47）；在LoCoMo上得分0.69（对比0.58）。

Conclusion: V-Mem有效提升多模态问答中视觉证据的检索准确性，尤其对含图像查询优势显著。

Abstract: Interaction between users and LLM agents is increasingly multimodal: conversations interleave text with images, and a later question may target either. Yet most agent memories are designed around text, and even the few that support multimodal conversations still fail on vision-related questions. We trace this failure to an assumption behind the similarity search they rely on: in the index space, a query lies close to the relevant evidence that answers it. In multimodal settings, two gaps break it. By the modality gap, a query lies closer to memory content of its own modality than to evidence in another, even in a trained joint embedding space. By the similarity-relevance gap, the content most similar to a query is often not the evidence that answers it, most acutely when a query carries both text and image and its evidence resembles neither part alone. We present V-Mem, a multimodal agentic memory system that routes retrieval by the modality of the query and that of the target evidence, both recognized from the query alone. To cross the modality gap, V-Mem organizes the conversation into rounds and returns the target-modality content from the same round as the match, without comparing across modalities. To close the similarity-relevance gap, it searches with an LLM-generated anchor that sits closer to the relevant evidence than the query does: a hypothetical caption for a text-only query seeking an image, and an enriched search anchor, the query text plus relevant keywords extracted from the query image, when the evidence is reachable only by combining the two. On Mem-Gallery, V-Mem reaches an LLM-judge score of 0.82 versus 0.56 for the second best, with the largest margin on questions carrying an image (0.87, no baseline above 0.47); on LoCoMo it scores 0.69 versus 0.58.

</details>


### [244] [Beyond Magnitude and Shape: A Direction-Aware Loss for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01857)
*Seunghan Lee,Jaehoon Lee,Jun Seo,Junhyeok Kang,Sangjun Han,Sungdong Yoo,Minjae Kim,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,Soonyoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出方向感知损失函数CosDir及其自适应变体CosDir-UW，通过余弦相似度对齐预测与目标的差分向量，在保持幅度精度的同时显著提升小幅度变化的预测方向准确率，且无需修改模型架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测损失（如MSE）仅优化幅度或形状，未显式优化变化方向；MSE训练在预测小幅度变化时方向性不足，而方向在风险管理等决策场景中与精确值同等重要。

Method: CosDir通过余弦相似度对齐预测与目标的差分向量，具备尺度不变性，在小幅度变化处保留方向梯度；CosDir-UW引入可学习权重自适应混合方向与幅度损失项，免去人工调整超参数。

Result: 超过10万次实验表明，该方法在多种数据集上始终显著提升方向准确率，同时不损害幅度预测精度，并优于多种损失函数。

Conclusion: CosDir系列为通用即插即用损失，可有效补充现有预测框架的方向性缺失，且代码已开源。

Abstract: The direction of change --- whether a series will move up or down --- is often as important as its exact value in decisiondriven applications such as risk management and financial forecasting. However, most forecasting losses optimize either point magnitude or shape and frequency structure, and none explicitly targets the direction of change. In this paper, we find that MSE-trained forecasters fail on the direction of small moves. To address this, we propose CosDir, a simple yet effective direction-aware loss that aligns the difference vectors of the prediction and the target via cosine similarity. Being scale-invariant, CosDir keeps a directional gradient on small moves, re-injecting learning signal exactly where MSE neglects it. CosDir is a lightweight, plug-in term that attaches to any backbone without architectural modification. Since the best ratio for mixing the directional and magnitude terms differs across datasets, we further propose CosDir-UW, an extension that makes this ratio adaptive by learning it during training, matching a per-dataset tuned weight with no hyperparameter. We conduct over 100K experiments, demonstrating that our method consistently and significantly improves directional accuracy while preserving magnitude accuracy, and that it outperforms various loss functions. Code is available at: https://github.com/seunghan96/cosdir.

</details>


### [245] [Emergence Invariance: From Symbolized Thought to Interface Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01548)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出符号化-子结构论题，证明大语言模型的涌现不能完全补偿其相对人类认知的缺失区别；存在一个不可消除的接口下限，仅扩大规模无法突破，必须同时改进接口本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽展现出涌现能力，但其语言优先的概率架构相对人类认知存在实质性、基质性和高层不完整性。核心问题是：涌现能否补偿每一个缺失的区别？作者试图形式化这一哲学前提，并区分规模内提升与接口改进两种根本不同的进展路径。

Method: 引入符号化-子结构论题与涌现不变性概念，建立形式框架：对于通过共享任务接口φ作用的规模索引族，满足R_s*=R_φ*+C_s，其中C_s为可随规模缩小的补偿差距，R_φ*为持续存在的接口下限。证明在固定输入律下，接口普遍信息性等价于其完备信息σ-域的细化关系；完全补偿等价于接口下限与渐近补偿差距同时为零。并通过DeepSeek V4-Flash API匹配实验验证框架预测。

Result: 实验显示：思考能力在相关区别可用时将指针追踪从0/16提升到14/16；精确观测孪生案例停留在50%构造下限；恢复关键记忆使匹配性能从50%升至100%。这些结果初步证实了规模内缩放与接口自身改进之间的预测性分离。

Conclusion: 大语言模型的能力增长来自两条独立路径：扩大规模缩小补偿差距，以及改进任务接口降低接口下限。仅依赖规模扩展无法实现完全补偿，必须同时进行接口层面的结构改进。该框架统一了接地、记忆、位置、注意力、贝叶斯继承、科学溯因和推理控制等现有研究结果。

Abstract: Language can be viewed as a formalized subset of thought: a consequence-governed symbolic structure projected from wider situated cognition. Large language models trained at scale exhibit compensatory emergence: sparse architectural primitives support in-context learning, multi-step reasoning, tool use, and chain of thought. Yet a language-first probabilistic architecture inherits substantive, substrate, and high-level incompletenesses relative to human cognition. Their coexistence makes an LLM a human-like thought-form generator that reconstructs increasingly human-like reasoning forms from an incomplete substrate. We ask whether emergence can compensate for every missing distinction. We formalize the philosophical premise as the Symbolization--Substructure Thesis and introduce emergence invariance. For a scale-indexed family acting through a shared task interface $φ$, $\mathcal{R}_s^*=\mathcal{R}_φ^*+C_s$: scale can reduce the compensation gap $C_s$, while a positive interface floor $\mathcal{R}_φ^*$ persists. We prove that, under a fixed input law, one interface is universally no less informative exactly when its completed information $σ$-field refines the other, and that total compensation occurs exactly when both the interface floor and asymptotic compensation gap vanish. The framework unifies existing results on grounding, memory, position, attention, Bayesian inheritance, scientific abduction, and reasoning control. In a matched DeepSeek V4-Flash API study, thinking improves pointer chasing from $0/16$ to $14/16$ when relevant distinctions are available; exact observational twins remain at their $50\%$ construction floor; and restoring decisive memory moves matched performance from $50\%$ to $100\%$. These results provide initial evidence for the predicted separation between scaling within an interface and refining the interface itself.

</details>


### [246] [CARE: A Cascaded Framework for Efficient and Reliable Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01885)
*Zemin Chao,Qianhui Xu,Jianhe Cen,Guangzhi Ge,Xiao Chen,Hoangzhi Wang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出CARE级联推理框架，通过轻量预过滤模型快速排除高置信度正常样本，仅对不确定样本调用复杂检测模型，在保持检测质量的同时实现2.7-4.8倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习时序异常检测模型虽准确但推理开销大，且现有方法对所有数据点统一推理浪费计算资源，因为正常样本占绝大多数且易预测，需高效筛选机制减少不必要的复杂模型调用。

Method: CARE框架由轻量预过滤模型（LPM）和复杂检测模型（CDM）组成。LPM使用残差MLP自编码器与正态条件门控机制，并引入结构注意力模块捕获通道级异常贡献，通过置信度引导的选择性路由目标优化门控网络，学习可靠的路由决策。

Result: 在八个真实世界基准上，CARE仅将不确定样本路由至CDM，相比最精确的SOTA方法实现2.7-4.8倍推理加速，同时保持有竞争力的检测质量。

Conclusion: CARE通过级联推理与选择性路由有效隔离高置信度正常样本，显著降低推理成本且不牺牲检测性能，为资源受限场景下的时序异常检测提供了高效可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: While deep learning models have achieved state-of-the-art performance in time series anomaly detection, their complex architectures incur substantial inference overhead. Existing methods typically apply a uniform inference strategy across all data points, which is inefficient given that anomalies are inherently scarce and the vast majority of temporal data consists of predictable normal patterns. To mitigate this bottleneck, we propose CARE, a model-agnostic cascaded inference framework that integrates a Lightweight Pre-filter Model (LPM) with an existing high-capacity Complex Detection Model (CDM). The LPM rapidly filters high-confidence normal samples using a Residual MLP AutoEncoder and a Normality-Conditioned Gating mechanism. Crucially, we introduce a Structure Attention module to explicitly capture channel-wise anomaly contributions, and optimize the gating network via a confidence-guided selective routing objective that learns reliable routing decisions to reduce unnecessary CDM invocations. Extensive experiments across eight real-world benchmarks demonstrate that CARE effectively isolates high-confidence normal samples. By routing only uncertain samples to the CDM, our framework achieves $2.7\times$ to $4.8\times$ inference speedup compared to the most accurate SOTA approaches, while still maintaining competitive detection quality.

</details>


### [247] [Is More Privileged Information Better? From Solution Traces to Problem-Solving Structure in Self-Distilled Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01589)
*Xuyang Zhao,Liting Zhang,Zichen Xu,Zhihu Wang,Xu Caiyue,Shiwan Zhao,Qicheng Li*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出问题空间引导的在线策略自蒸馏（PS-OPSD），用轨迹锚定的引导信息替代完整参考答案，在不改变学生采样与蒸馏目标的前提下提升数学推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统OPSD中教师提供的token级目标依赖参考答案中的私有信息，推理时学生无法获取，导致训练与推理分布不一致。

Method: 将完整参考答案替换为描述初始状态、目标条件、约束及选定状态转移路径的轨迹锚定引导，学生策略与OPSD目标保持不变。

Result: 在三个数学推理基准和1.7B至8B参数规模下，PS-OPSD取得最高的纯问题准确率；控制实验表明引导相关性与路径一致性是主要增益来源。

Conclusion: 特权信息的表示方式是OPSD中关键设计维度，PS-OPSD通过问题空间引导有效缓解了教师目标中的信息泄漏问题。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) improves reasoning by using a privileged view of a model conditioned on reference solutions to supervise a student view that observes only the question. However, the teacher-provided token-level targets may depend on reference-specific information unavailable at inference time. We propose Problem-Space-Guided OPSD (PS-OPSD), which replaces the complete solution with trajectory-grounded guidance describing the initial state, goal conditions, constraints, and a selected state-transition path. The student rollout and OPSD objective remain unchanged. Across three mathematical reasoning benchmarks and model scales ranging from 1.7B to 8B, PS-OPSD achieves the highest aggregate question-only accuracy among the compared methods. Controlled experiments further indicate that guidance relevance and path coherence contribute to these gains, highlighting the representation of privileged information as an important design choice in OPSD.

</details>


### [248] [Latent Thought Credit: Multi-Answer Credit Assignment for Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01593)
*Xuyang Zhao,Liting Zhang,Zichen Xu,Yong Chen,Wenjia Zeng,Shiwan Zhao,Qicheng Li*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Latent Thought Credit (LTC)的层次化信用分配框架，用于在潜在推理中通过多答案平均估计思想级奖励，并利用优势加权目标优化策略，提升数学推理与STEM任务准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在推理中仅凭最终答案奖励难以区分思想质量与答案采样噪声，导致信用分配困难，需要更细粒度的思想级奖励估计。

Method: 对每个提示采样多个潜在思想，固定思想后生成多个答案并平均奖励作为思想级期望；分别用思想级优势优化思想阶段、答案级优势优化答案阶段，并加入优势加权思想匹配目标以复现高信用思想；在GRPO风格的在策略框架中实现。

Result: 在数学推理和STEM多项选择任务上取得最佳平均准确率；消融与固定上下文诊断表明多答案估计降低了奖励估计误差，缓解了模糊或错误的思想级信用。

Conclusion: LTC通过层次化信用分配有效提升潜在推理性能，验证了多答案估计对思想级奖励可靠性的关键作用。

Abstract: Latent reasoning allows language models to carry out intermediate reasoning in continuous latent representations rather than fully externalizing it as discrete chains of thought. However, assigning credit to such latent thoughts from answer-only rewards is difficult: a single final answer mixes thought quality with answer-sampling noise. We propose \textbf{Latent Thought Credit (LTC)}, a hierarchical credit-assignment framework for latent reasoning. For each prompt, LTC samples multiple latent thoughts, fixes the context after each thought, and estimates thought-level expected reward by averaging rewards over multiple answers generated from that fixed context. LTC uses thought-level advantages to optimize the latent-thought phase, answer-level advantages to optimize the answer phase, and an advantage-weighted thought-matching objective that helps the policy reproduce high-credit latent thoughts. We instantiate LTC in a GRPO-style on-policy training framework and evaluate it across mathematical reasoning and STEM multiple-choice tasks. LTC achieves the best average accuracy among the compared methods, while ablations and fixed-context diagnostics show that multi-answer estimation reduces reward-estimation error and mitigates ambiguous or incorrect thought-level credit.

</details>


### [249] [HarnessCompass: Guiding Automatic Harness Evolution toward Generalizable and Effective Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01918)
*Luan Zhang,Ruochen Zhou,Dandan Song,Zhengyu Chen,Yuhang Tian,Jun Yang,Huipeng Ma,Chenhao Li,Guangyuan Feng,Xudong Li,Yizhou Jin,Yan Xu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出 HarnessCompass 框架，通过约束进化、主动反馈和组件解耦优化，在 SWE-bench Verified 上仅用 5 次迭代将 GPT-5.4 的 Pass@1 从 54% 提升至 66%，并展现出更强的跨任务和跨模型泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动 harness 进化方法存在三个问题：过拟合进化任务、仅依赖轨迹信号、联合优化导致组件间干扰，从而限制泛化和效率。

Method: 引入全局约束确保修改是任务无关的；结合轨迹信号与智能体对 harness 使用的第一人称主动反馈；将不同 harness 组件解耦优化后再整合，既减少干扰又保持协同。

Result: 在 SWE-bench Verified 上 Pass@1 从 54% 提升至 66%（5 次迭代），优于 AHE；进化后的 harness 在保留任务和其他模型上均表现出显著更强的迁移性。

Conclusion: HarnessCompass 通过约束进化、主动反馈和组件解耦实现了更高效和泛化的自动 harness 进化，为 LLM 智能体环境适配提供了新方向。

Abstract: Harness design plays a critical role in agent performance by shaping how large language models (LLMs) perceive, reason over, and act within executable environments. Recent work has proposed automatic harness evolution, which iteratively improves the harness from agent--environment interactions. However, existing methods often overfit to the evolution tasks, rely exclusively on trajectory-derived signals, and optimize harness components jointly, causing interference across components. We propose HarnessCompass, a novel automatic harness evolution framework built around constrained evolution, proactive feedback, and component-wise optimization. HarnessCompass first enforces global constraints on evolution, restricting modifications to task-agnostic harness changes that generalize beyond the evolution tasks. It then augments trajectory-derived evidence with proactive first-person feedback from the agent about harness usage, yielding richer signals for evolution. Finally, it decouples the optimization of different harness components before consolidating them into a unified harness, reducing cross-component interference while preserving component synergy. On SWE-bench Verified with GPT-5.4, HarnessCompass improves Pass@1 from 54\% to 66\% in only 5 evolution iterations, outperforming AHE in both effectiveness and evolution efficiency. In addition, the evolved harness transfers effectively to held-out tasks and other models, demonstrating substantially stronger generalization than prior automatic harness evolution methods.

</details>


### [250] [When Memory Updates but Behavior Does Not: Repairing Implicit Stale Dependencies in Personalized Agent Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01619)
*Haofei Sun,Lin He*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出StateAuditor，通过反向审计（从存储状态到草稿）并验证时间戳和引文来修复陈旧记忆，在STALE基准上将IPA/PR分数提升约5个百分点，但未证明对通用代理记忆有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强代理即使知道存储状态过期，仍可能围绕旧值规划，即隐式策略适应（IPA）差距；根源之一是草稿锚定验证只检查回答内容，而开放回答中往往未提及陈旧依赖。

Method: 从存储状态向草稿反向审计：LLM从带时间戳证据中提出候选新旧转换，确定性代码将引文钉到单一条目，验证新证据时间更新，仅允许经验证的转换触发修复。验证的是出处和时序，而非语义取代。

Result: STALE完整协议下严格单查询VTA得分0.736 vs 0.686（配对增益+5.0点，95%CI [+2.9,+7.2]），第三人模型家族评审复现增益（0.738 vs 0.680）；HorizonBench上外部增益主要来自草稿侧审计，更难的作者生命周期集无增益。对照实验（相同证据、适配器和调用预算）仅0.692，说明增益来自转换机制。

Conclusion: StateAuditor的结构性修复（反向审计加引脚验证）能显著提升陈旧记忆场景下的隐式策略适应和前提抵抗能力，但增益特定于该转换管道，不主张对通用代理记忆有效，且外部泛化有限。

Abstract: Memory-augmented agents can know that a user's stored state is outdated and still plan around the old value. The STALE benchmark calls this the implicit policy adaptation (IPA) gap. We identify one structural contributor: draft-anchored verification checks what a response says, and in an open-ended response the stale dependency is usually unsaid. StateAuditor therefore audits in the opposite direction, from stored state to draft. An LLM proposes candidate old-to-new transitions from timestamped evidence; deterministic code pins each quotation to a single entry, checks that the new evidence really is newer, and lets only these verified transitions trigger repair. What is verified is provenance and chronology - not semantic supersession. On STALE's full protocol (400 scenarios, 50-session histories, one independent response per query), strict single-query VTA scores .736 against .686 for our locked predecessor under the same judge: a +5.0-point paired gain (95% CI [+2.9, +7.2]) coming almost entirely from IPA and premise resistance (PR). The benchmark's own judge, from a third model family, reproduces the gain (.738 vs. .680). On an independent cross-family preference-evolution benchmark (HorizonBench), the full draft-audit-repair pipeline over a gold-derived structured store raises current-preference accuracy (user-clustered p<.01), though a matched control shows most of this external gain is the draft-side audit itself; a harder authored lifecycle set gives no gain, bounding the claim while false invalidation stays controlled. On STALE, by contrast, a matched control (same evidence, adapter, and call budget) scores only .692 (+0.6 over the predecessor, n.s.), attributing the STALE gain to the transition machinery rather than added context or calls. We make no claim about general-purpose agent memory.

</details>


### [251] [AOS: Adaptive Optimizer Switching via Training-State Signals for Faster Convergence and Better Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01997)
*Alok Kumar Pandey,Umang Chaturvedi,Aatish Rana,Gopi Krishna Nedanuri*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出AOS-R规则式优化器切换控制器，依据六种梯度信号在训练中动态切换AdamW/SGD-M/Lion，平均节省20%训练轮数并小幅提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一优化器难以兼顾训练初期（梯度噪声大，自适应方法更稳）与后期（需良好泛化，带动量的SGD更优），现有固定优化器策略无法适应训练阶段切换需求。

Method: 利用GNS、Hutchinson曲率迹、损失停滞、更新稳定比、GSI和LIR六种在线信号，通过规则判断在AdamW、SGD-M、Lion间切换；采用状态保持的动量迁移与400步学习率桥接避免切换精度损失。

Result: CIFAR-100/WRN-28x10上81轮达78% top-1，比AdamW少26%轮数；8个模型-数据集基准中6个取得最佳精度，平均+0.4pp，收敛速度0.80x。

Conclusion: AOS-R是一种轻量、无需调参的优化器切换策略，能在统一超参下同时提升收敛速度与最终精度，验证了阶段适配优化器的有效性。

Abstract: Single-optimizer training is a poor fit for the distinct phases of deep network optimization: adaptive methods handle noisy early gradients well but overshoot flat minima, while SGD with momentum generalizes better in the late phase but converges slowly early on.
  We introduce AOS-R (Adaptive Optimizer Switching, Rule-Based), a lightweight controller that monitors six online gradient-space signals -- gradient noise scale (GNS), Hutchinson curvature trace, loss stagnation, update stability ratio, gradient stability index (GSI), and loss improvement ratio (LIR) -- and switches among AdamW, SGD-M, and Lion as the optimization landscape evolves. State-preserving momentum transfer and a 400-step learning-rate bridge prevent accuracy degradation at every transition point.
  On CIFAR-100/WRN-28x10, AOS-R reaches 78% top-1 in 81 epochs -- 26% fewer than AdamW (109), 43% fewer than SGD-M (143), and 16% fewer than Lion (96). Across eight model-dataset benchmarks, AOS-R achieves best accuracy on 6 of 8 combinations with a mean +0.4 pp gain and 0.80x convergence speedup over AdamW under a single shared hyperparameter configuration.

</details>


### [252] [GISAgentBench: A Practitioner-Sourced Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Agents on GIS Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01645)
*Abhinav Pothuri,Zhe Jiang,Zelin Xu,Di Yang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出GISAgentBench基准，包含349个真实GIS多步任务，提供可执行参考轨迹和精确真值输出，严格评估发现最强智能体仅完成32.7%任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GIS智能体基准多来自教科书或LLM生成，规模小、轨迹浅且缺乏真值输出，导致评估依赖代理信号，无法准确反映任务正确性。

Method: 从GIS Stack Exchange收集349个真实任务，在六个地理区域实现于公共数据上；每个任务附带可执行参考轨迹和精确真值输出文件，采用严格、确定性、容差感知的输出匹配评估。

Result: 评估六个LLM模型，最佳智能体在严格容差评分下仅完成32.7%任务，但多数模型输出接近真值。

Conclusion: 真实GIS工作流对现有LLM智能体仍具挑战，本基准提供更可靠的评估方式，推动更有效的自动化方法发展。

Abstract: Geographic Information System (GIS) professionals rely on multi-step spatial analysis workflows to support decision-making in urban planning, disaster response, and environmental monitoring. The process is tedious, time-consuming, and error-prone. While recent large language model (LLM) agents equipped with external tools have the potential to automate geospatial analysis, their ability to perform realistic GIS workflows remains largely unexplored. Existing GIS agent benchmarking datasets are mostly drawn from textbooks, tutorials, or LLM-generated seeds and remain limited in size and trajectory depth. More importantly, none provides ground truth outputs. They therefore rely on surrogate signals such as code similarity, trajectory matching, or LLM and VLM judges, which can conflate workflow resemblance with task correctness. To address this gap, we introduce GISAgentBench, a benchmark of 349 multi-step GIS tasks curated from GIS Stack Exchange and instantiated on real public data across six selected geographic areas of interest. Each task ships with an executable reference trajectory and an exact ground truth output file, enabling strict, deterministic, tolerance-aware output matching beyond LLM judging. Evaluations of six LLM models reveal that realistic GIS workflows remain challenging: the best agent completes only 32.7% of tasks under strict tolerance-aware scoring, although most models produce outputs that are close to the ground truth.

</details>


### [253] [DART: Decoded Attention over Recurrent States for Efficient Long-Context Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02032)
*Yixiao Qian,Song Chen,Pengkai Wang,Jiaxu Liu,Shengze Cai,Chao Xu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出DART架构，在Mamba-2的SSD框架中解码键值对并执行状态记忆注意力，以较小缓存代价提升检索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合架构在层级别交替使用注意力与循环模型，未探索共享记忆表示能否同时支持循环压缩与注意力检索。Mamba-2的SSD视图将状态视为压缩KV缓存，但仅解码值而不解码键，限制了检索能力。

Method: DART保留Mamba-2分块扫描产生的块状态记忆，从中解码令牌条件键和值，执行状态记忆注意力（SMA），并通过门控残差连接与原生Mamba-2输出结合。训练时复用分块扫描，SMA采用FlashAttention风格计算。

Result: 相比匹配的注意力基线，DART减少75%的长度相关推理缓存（S=256, N=128时）。相比Mamba-2，DART显著提升关联回忆和检索能力，且保持通用语言建模质量。

Conclusion: 共享记忆表示可同时支持循环压缩与注意力检索，DART为高效长上下文建模提供了新方向。

Abstract: Modern language models are built primarily from Transformers, recurrent models, and their hybrid architectures. Transformers rely on token-level attention memories, while recurrent models such as state space models (SSMs) and linear attention maintain compact recurrent states. These architectures are typically instantiated separately or interleaved at the layer level, leaving open whether a shared memory representation can support both recurrent compression and attention-style retrieval. We study this question through the state space duality (SSD) view of Mamba-2, where the SSM state can be interpreted as a compressed associative key--value (KV) cache. We observe that Mamba-2 decodes token-conditioned values from this state but does not decode token-conditioned keys. Based on this observation, we propose DART (Decoded Attention over Recurrent sTates), which retains the chunk state contributions produced by the Mamba-2 chunked scan as chunk state memories, decodes token-conditioned keys and values from these memories, and performs state-memory attention (SMA) over the resulting KV pairs. The retrieved output is then combined with the native Mamba-2 output through a gated residual connection. DART supports practical training by reusing the Mamba-2 chunked scan and implementing SMA as a FlashAttention-style computation. Our analysis and experiments show that DART substantially reduces the length-dependent inference cache compared with a matched attention baseline (e.g., $75\%$ savings when the chunk size is $S=256$ and the state size is $N=128$). Compared with Mamba-2, DART substantially improves associative recall and retrieval while preserving general language-modeling quality.

</details>


### [254] [LongCat Sparse Attention: Taming the Lightning via Streaming-aware Hierarchical Cross-Layer Indexing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01662)
*Wen Zan,Jiaqi Zhang,Jianchao Tan,Hong Liu,Cunguang Wang,Xiang Li,Duyue Ma,Guanyu Wu,Yifan Lu,Fengcun Li,Yerui Sun,Peng Pei,Yuchen Xie,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出LongCat稀疏注意力（LSA），通过流式感知索引、跨层索引和分层索引三种硬件-算法协同策略，解决DeepSeek稀疏注意力中索引开销大和内存访问不连续的问题，支持百万token上下文训练且性能与全注意力相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeepSeek稀疏注意力虽能高效建模长上下文，但其Lightning Indexer存在O(L²)计算开销，且输出导致不连续的内存访问模式，限制实际部署效率。

Method: LSA包含三个正交策略：流式感知索引将散乱KV转为硬件对齐的连续布局以实现合并HBM访问；跨层索引通过蒸馏使单层索引结果复用于连续多层以摊薄开销；分层索引采用粗到细评分逐步缩小每查询候选集以减少计算。

Result: 在69B-A3B至560B-A27B多种规模模型上，LSA在通用和长上下文基准上与全注意力性能持平；支持长达百万token的原生训练，并支撑LongCat-2.0（1.6T-A48B）开发；开源LongCat-Flash-Lite-Sparse（69B-A3B）集成LSA及更新长上下文语料。

Conclusion: LSA通过系统级优化有效缓解稀疏注意力的部署瓶颈，在保持性能的同时提升硬件效率，为超长上下文模型训练提供了可行方案，并向社区开源了相关模型和训练数据。

Abstract: DeepSeek Sparse Attention (DSA) enables efficient long-context modeling through its Lightning Indexer. However, practical deployment remains constrained by the indexer's expensive $O(L^2)$ scoring overhead and the hardware-inefficient, discontinuous memory-access patterns induced by its outputs. To address these system-level bottlenecks, we introduce LongCat Sparse Attention (LSA), a hardware-algorithm co-designed framework comprising three complementary and orthogonal strategies: (1) Streaming-Aware Indexing, which selectively converts scattered KV entries into hardware-aligned contiguous layouts to enable coalesced HBM access; (2) Cross-Layer Indexing, which amortizes indexing overhead by reusing the results produced by a single layer across consecutive layers, supported by cross-layer distillation; and (3) Hierarchical Indexing, which adopts a coarse-to-fine scoring scheme to progressively narrow the candidate set for each query, thereby substantially reducing indexing computation. Extensive scaling experiments, ranging from 69B-A3B to 560B-A27B models, demonstrate that LSA consistently achieves performance on par with full attention across both general-purpose and long-context benchmarks. Moreover, LSA supports native training with context lengths of up to one million tokens and underpins the development of LongCat-2.0 (1.6T-A48B). To facilitate further research, we also introduce and open-source LongCat-Flash-Lite-Sparse (69B-A3B), which integrates LSA into LongCat-Flash-Lite and incorporates an updated long-context training corpus.

</details>


### [255] [Allocation Before Ranking: Decoupled Token Compression for OmniLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01665)
*Zhenghui Guo,Yilin Yang,Yuanbin Man,Miao Yin,Weidong Shi,Rabimba Karanjai,Omprakash Gnawali,Chengming Zhang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出Macer，一种无需训练的OmniLLM标记压缩器，通过先分配模态预算再进行模态内归一化排名，纠正共享Top-K导致的音频偏向分配问题，在较低token成本下保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Token压缩将问题简化为单一显著性排名，但注意力分数同时决定模态容量分配和模态内token保留，共享Top-K规则继承了偏向音频的分配先验，使视频token竞争不过音频token。

Method: Macer先为音频和视频分配显式预算，然后在各模态的浅层进行分配归一化后的排名，选择保留token。

Result: 在25%保留率下，Qwen2.5-Omni-7B保留98.7%性能，3B保留97.3%；7B在25%设置下达到OmniZip在45%保留率的性能且FLOPs更低；OmniVinci-9B上比共享Top-K最高提升12.9点。

Conclusion: 先分配后排名的原则能有效提升多模态压缩的分配合理性，在降低计算成本的同时保持甚至提升下游任务性能，尤其对多模态联合处理场景。

Abstract: Token compression in OmniLLMs is typically posed as a single saliency-ranking problem: score each multimodal token, keep the top-K. We argue this abstraction is mis-specified. The same attention score simultaneously decides two things: how much retained capacity each modality receives, and which tokens within a modality are kept. A shared top-K rule therefore inherits this audio-favoring allocation prior, spending retained capacity on audio before video tokens have a chance to compete. We propose Macer, a training-free compressor that first assigns explicit audio and video budgets, then performs allocation-normalized ranking within each modality at modality-specific shallow layers. Macer significantly reduces token cost while preserving accuracy across audio-grounded, audio--video joint, visual-dominant, and video-centric benchmarks. At 25 % retention, Macer preserves 98.7 % of full-token performance on Qwen2.5-Omni-7B and 97.3 % on Qwen2.5-Omni-3B. On Qwen2.5-Omni-7B, this 25 % setting reaches OmniZip-level performance at 45 % retention while using lower FLOPs. On OmniVinci-9B, the same allocation-before-ranking principle improves over shared top-K ranking by up to 12.9 points.

</details>


### [256] [TCPO: Turn-Level Credit Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01667)
*Sicong Liao,Zhi Chen,Yaohua Tang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出TCPO，一种用于验证器引导的多轮强化学习的回合级信用分配方法，通过分数到信用转换和参考比较构建回合优势，提升推理和智能体任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮设置中验证器分数提供密集反馈但不提供密集信用，分数衡量当前输出质量，而信用应衡量当前回合如何改变改进轨迹，现有方法缺乏直接的回合级信用分配。

Method: TCPO将信用分配视为分数到信用转换，通过参考比较构建回合级优势，包括回顾性信用捕捉即时进步和回归、后见延迟信用识别非改进回合、选择性固定历史反事实估计细化高意外回合。

Result: 在数学推理、代码生成和AppWorld智能体任务上，TCPO在多种模型规模、任务领域和验证器类型下优于或匹配最强基线，达到最佳或并列最佳best-turn Pass@8，减少成功回合数并提升多轮智能体性能。

Conclusion: 分数到信用转换是验证器引导多轮策略优化的核心要素，TCPO验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Verifier-guided reinforcement learning has become a powerful paradigm for improving LLM reasoning. In multi-turn settings, models receive a verifier score after each turn and iteratively refine their outputs. Although such scores provide dense feedback, they do not directly provide dense credit: a score measures the quality of the current output, while credit should measure how the current turn changes the refinement trajectory. We propose TCPO, a turn-level credit assignment method for verifier-guided multi-turn RL. TCPO casts credit assignment as score-to-credit conversion and constructs turn-level advantages through reference-based comparisons: retrospective credit captures immediate progress and regression relative to the best prior state; hindsight delayed credit identifies non-improving turns with later payoff; and selective fixed-history counterfactual estimation refines high-surprisal turns under the same history. Experiments on math reasoning, code generation, and AppWorld agent tasks show that TCPO improves or matches the strongest baselines across model scales, task domains, and verifier types. TCPO achieves the best or tied-best best-turn Pass@8 on Qwen3-4B and DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B, reduces turns to success, and improves multi-turn agent performance. These results highlight score-to-credit conversion as a central ingredient for verifier-guided multi-turn policy optimization.

</details>


### [257] [Secrets Everywhere: Auditing Memorization in Mobility Prediction Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02052)
*Anne Josiane Kouam,Hristo Boyadzhiev,Konrad Rieck*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 首次系统审计移动预测模型的记忆化现象，提出多粒度量化框架并发现普遍记忆模式，呼吁强制隐私审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动预测模型广泛部署但存在记忆训练轨迹的隐私风险，且时空多尺度特性与语言模型不同，缺乏系统性审计方法。

Method: 引入框架量化个体位置、锚点对和子轨迹段的记忆化，构建用户参照集评估模型对训练数据的偏好。

Result: 多模型多数据集实验显示记忆化与用户规律性相关，增加推理时数据提取风险。

Conclusion: 移动预测模型存在系统性记忆泄露，需强制隐私审计以保障用户数据安全。

Abstract: Human mobility prediction models, which forecast the next location in a user's trajectory, are increasingly deployed in urban analytics, navigation, and personalized services. Yet, little is known about their potential to memorize and expose sensitive user trajectories from training data. While memorization has been extensively studied in language models, mobility prediction poses unique challenges: training sequences encode human behavior at various spatial and temporal scales, creating privacy risks at different granularities.
  In this paper, we conduct the first systematic audit of memorization in mobility prediction models. While prior work has shown that privacy leaks can arise from such models, we systematically assess and quantify memorization risks at scale. We identify key challenges, including the lack of a randomness space, the multi-scale structure of trajectories, and user-specific behavioral diversity. To address these challenges, we introduce a framework to quantify mobility memorization at different levels of granularity: individual locations, anchor pairs, and subtrajectory segments. We also develop user-grounded reference sets to assess how likely a model is to prefer training data over realistic alternatives. Our evaluation across multiple models and datasets reveals pervasive memorization patterns that correlate with user regularity and increase the risk of data extraction at inference time. Our findings call for mandatory privacy auditing in mobility prediction models.

</details>


### [258] [When Memory Becomes Authority: Benchmarking Authority Collapse at the Memory Consolidation Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01679)
*Qiuyang Zhan,Rui Zhang,Sheng Guo,Lepeng Zhao,Zhuotao Liu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 记忆整合会引入授权边界，但现有系统常丢失源权限信息导致"权威崩溃"。作者构建AuthMem-Bench基准，在49种配置中发现48种存在此问题，未授权操作率高达50.3%，而自动保留权限标签可将其降至0%且不影响任务成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续记忆系统让LLM代理跨任务适应，但整合过程可能保留内容却丢失来源权限约束，导致记忆隐含的权威超过实际允许范围，即"权威崩溃"。现有研究忽视这一风险，需系统评估其普遍性与影响。

Method: 创建受控配对基准AuthMem-Bench，固定目标声明与下游任务，仅变化来源权威级别；评估写入时崩溃、下游授权错误及自动权威保留；测试7种基于流行代理记忆系统的整合器与7种LLM骨干，覆盖受控动作测试与端到端场景。

Result: 49种配置中48种出现权威崩溃；无权限元数据时平均未授权操作率50.3%；端到端中自动预测并持久化权限标签将未授权率从16.9%降至0.0%，良意任务成功率几乎不变。

Conclusion: 记忆驱动适应不仅需保留所学内容，更需保留其复用时的授权条件；自动维护权限元数据可在不牺牲性能前提下消除未授权行为，是构建安全自适应代理的关键。

Abstract: Persistent memory allows (self-evolving) LLM agents to adapt across tasks by consolidating heterogeneous interaction histories into reusable facts, preferences, observations, and rules. Yet consolidation also imposes an implicit authorization boundary: it determines whether stored information may later be consumed as a user fact, an attested observation, or a standing instruction. We identify authority collapse, in which consolidation preserves a claim while erasing the source constraints governing its authorized use, causing the stored memory to imply greater authority than its source permits. We introduce AuthMem-Bench, a controlled paired benchmark that holds the focal claim and downstream task fixed while varying only source authority. It evaluates write-time collapse, downstream authorization errors, and automatic authority preservation. Across seven consolidators based on widely used agent-memory systems and seven LLM backbones, we observe authority collapse in 48 of 49 evaluated configurations. In a controlled action-grounded evaluation, collapsed memories without authority metadata yield a mean unauthorized-action rate of 50.3%. In an end-to-end evaluation, automatically predicted and persisted authority labels reduce the observed unauthorized-action rate from 16.9% to 0.0%, while benign task success remains essentially unchanged. These findings show that memory-driven adaptation must preserve not only what was
  learned, but also the authority under which it may be reused.

</details>


### [259] [GABench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Agents on Graph Analysis Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01684)
*Jiarui Tan,Zhongjian Zhang,YaBo Guo,Jiawei Liu,Yujie Xing,Muhan Zhang,Cheng Yang,Chuan Shi*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出GABench，一个涵盖三种图类型、四类任务和84个可执行工具的智能体图分析基准，包含10,400个可验证任务，并揭示现有LLM智能体在图分析中的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图基准覆盖有限且多采用文本问答形式，无法全面评估LLM智能体的端到端智能体能力。

Method: 构建覆盖图检索、图理论、图机器学习和图开放问答的GABench，提供84个工具并开发任务生成流水线，生成10,400个带可验证真值的任务。

Result: 发现现有LLM智能体在图分析任务上表现挣扎，harness选择显著影响性能但现有harness仍有局限，且性能更依赖工具调用质量而非数量。

Conclusion: GABench为智能体图分析提供全面评估框架，其发现为开发更有效的LLM智能体和harness提供实践指导。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly capable of planning, using tools, and interacting with external environments. They are typically supported by harnesses, which manage state and coordinate multi-step execution. Graph analysis provides a promising setting for evaluating their agentic capabilities, because it requires agents to access data and execute operations in a graph environment. However, existing graph benchmarks for LLMs provide limited coverage of graph tasks and graph types, making it difficult to comprehensively evaluate LLM agents. Moreover, they typically formulate graph analysis as text-based question answering, where graph information is directly provided in the prompt, limiting the evaluation of end-to-end agentic capabilities. To address these limitations, we introduce GABench, a comprehensive benchmark for agentic graph analysis. GABench spans three graph types and covers four graph analysis task categories: graph retrieval, graph theory, graph machine learning, and graph open-ended question answering. GABench also provides 84 executable tools for accessing graph data and performing diverse graph operations. Building on these tools, we develop an agentic graph analysis task generation pipeline and construct 10,400 tasks with verifiable ground truth.Using GABench, we evaluate a range of frontier LLMs and agent harnesses. Our experiments reveal three key findings: (1) Existing LLM agents still struggle with complex graph analysis tasks. (2) Harness choice significantly affects performance, yet existing harnesses remain limited on complex graph tasks. (3) Graph analysis depends more on tool-call quality than quantity. Our findings provide practical insights into the development and evaluation of LLM agents for graph analysis.

</details>


### [260] [Geometry-Guided Layerwise FFN Width Allocation in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02064)
*Timur Mudarisov,Mikhail Burtsev,Radu State*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出从前向传播中测量各层FFN的几何变化（移位、Gromov-Wasserstein失真、持久同调）来分配宽度预算，发现归一化几何工作量能更好预测层敏感度，且基于几何的分配策略在多个规模上优于均匀宽度和余弦衰减基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FFN占Transformer参数的大部分，但各层隐藏宽度通常恒定。作者质疑是否可以根据前向传播中每层行为来分配容量，而非均匀分配，从而更高效地利用固定预算。

Method: 将每层FFN视为对token表示云的传输，使用保持对应性的移位、Gromov-Wasserstein失真、以及原始和尺度归一化度量下的一次持久同调来量化几何变化。构建逐层近似代理函数，实现精确的固定预算优化器。

Result: 在7个预训练模型中，原始欧氏工作量与残差范数增长一致，而归一化工作量主要集中在前层。Gromov-Wasserstein工作量与基于扰动的层敏感度更一致。在128M和256M训练中，多种归一化工作量调度降低平均验证损失；在440M下，最佳几何分配优于均匀和余弦衰减，而反拓扑的原始控制更差。

Conclusion: 归一化几何工作量可作为有效的层敏感度信号，用于非均匀FFN宽度分配，在多个规模下稳定提升性能，优于均匀和手工设计的调度。

Abstract: Feed-forward networks (FFNs) account for a large fraction of Transformer parameters, yet their hidden width is usually constant across depth. We ask whether this capacity can instead be allocated from a forward-pass measurement of layer behavior. We view each FFN as transporting a cloud of token representations and quantify the induced geometric change using correspondence-preserving shift, Gromov-Wasserstein distortion, and degree-one persistent homology under raw and scale-normalized metrics. A layerwise approximation surrogate yields an exact fixed-budget optimizer. Across seven pretrained language models, raw Euclidean work largely tracks residual-norm growth, whereas normalized work is predominantly front-loaded. Gromov-Wasserstein work is more consistently associated with perturbation-based layer sensitivity than the finite-sample topological estimate. In paired 128M and 256M training runs, several normalized-work schedules reduce mean validation loss relative to both uniform width and a hand-designed cosine taper. With the amplified paired differences at 440M, the best geometry-based allocations improve over uniform substantially larger than the cosine taper, while the anti-topological raw control is worse than uniform.

</details>


### [261] [Beyond Single-Use Tokens: Durable Authorization State for Replay-Resistant LLM Agent Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01710)
*Jinghan Xu,Longze Fan,Zeyuan Wang,Xinjin Li,Hankai Liu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出语义重放问题：大语言模型智能体在重规划、重试、委托和崩溃恢复时，即使每次使用新令牌，同一用户授权也可能被多次执行。CapLease通过持久化授权状态和事务性流程解决该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单次使用令牌仅防止令牌标识符重用，无法阻止智能体在获得新令牌后重复执行同一授权操作，导致超出授权执行预算。

Method: CapLease采用提案级和权威级防御，绑定用户确认到规范动作，并强制执行Issue-Prepare-Commit事务转换，同时依赖不可变单调状态记录授权动作、确认事件和剩余预算。

Result: 在重规划、重试、委托、并发、确认重放和崩溃恢复场景中，标识符局部令牌允许语义重发，而CapLease与有状态服务器账本能阻止重复入场，结合幂等接收端可防止重复外部效果。

Conclusion: 防重放智能体执行的关键系统需求是持久化授权状态，而非仅依赖令牌表示形式。

Abstract: Tool-using large language model agents frequently replan, retry failed operations, delegate tasks, and resume after crashes. These behaviors can cause one user authorization to be requested and executed multiple times under freshly issued token identifiers, even when each individual token is single-use. We call this failure semantic replay: exceeding the execution budget of a token-independent authorization instance rather than merely reusing an old token identifier. We show that identifier-local token consumption cannot prevent fresh reissuance unless the issuer retains monotonic durable state over the authorized action, confirmation event, and remaining execution budget. We introduce CapLease, an authorization-consumption layer that follows proposal- and authority-level defenses, binds an authenticated user confirmation to a canonical action, and enforces transactional Issue-Prepare-Commit transitions. Across LLM-agent replanning, retry, delegation, concurrency, confirmation-replay, and crash-recovery scenarios, identifier-local tokens permit fresh semantic reissuance, whereas CapLease and an equally stateful Server Ledger prevent duplicate admission and, with an idempotent sink, duplicate external effects. Our results identify durable authorization state, rather than token representation alone, as the systems requirement for replay-resistant agent execution.

</details>


### [262] [A 2-Block Architecture for Real-Time EEG Gait Decoding: A Pilot Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02083)
*Shantanu Sarkar,Saurabh Prasad,Jose L. Contreras-Vidal*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种2模块BCI架构（可训练特征提取块+PolyTVL+LSTM解码块），实现四状态步态分类，闭环部署中达到55.3%/52.7%启动成功率与70.5ms预测时间，验证实时可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有闭环下肢外骨骼EEG控制受运动伪迹、低信噪比及二值步态范式限制，无法完整刻画皮层步态复杂性。

Method: 构建2模块BCI：特征提取块含实时伪迹抑制与多域特征提取；解码块采用新提出的多项式时变层（PolyTVL）+LSTM，进行站立/启动/执行/终止四状态分类。

Result: PolyTVL+LSTM（v01）验证MCC为0.435（gap 0.187），优于所有变体；EEG特征在不同ROI与子带间判别性显著（p<0.05）。闭环测试中，Rex辅助与自主启动成功率分别为55.3%和52.7%，平均预测时间70.5ms（±41.5）。

Conclusion: 该架构在分类精度与实时性上均满足闭环应用需求，为下肢外骨骼多状态皮层控制提供可行方案，但需更大规模验证。

Abstract: Closed-loop lower-limb exoskeleton control via Electroencephalography (EEG) remains limited by motion artifacts, low signal-to-noise ratio, and binary gait formulations that fail to capture full cortical gait complexity. We propose a 2-block Brain-Computer Interface (BCI) architecture: a trainable session-specific Feature Extraction Block with real-time artifact suppression and multi-domain feature extraction, coupled with a Decoder Block built on a novel Polynomial Time-Varying Layer (PolyTVL)+LSTM for four-state gait classification (Stand, Initiate, Execute, Terminate). Ablation confirmed v01 (PolyTVL+LSTM) outperformed all variants (validation MCC: 0.435, gap: 0.187), with consistent EEG feature discriminability across ROIs and sub-bands (p<0.05). Closed-loop deployment with v01 achieved 55.3% (Rex-assisted) and 52.7% (volitional) gait initiation success, with a mean prediction time of 70.5~ms (+/-41.5), validating real-time feasibility in this pilot study.

</details>


### [263] [DAPD: Dual-Anchored Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01735)
*Jianyu Wu,Yizhou Wang,Encheng Su,Chen Tang,Shixiang Tang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出双锚定策略蒸馏（DAPD）框架，通过双路径锚定和双源锚定解决在线策略自蒸馏中的特权幻觉问题，在多个模型规模上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）虽能利用特权信息强化教师模型，但会导致学生模型学习到依赖训练时特权信息的行为，而推理时无法复现，造成特权幻觉并降低性能。其根本原因是教师与学生之间在推理时的信息不对称。

Method: 提出DAPD框架，包含两层锚定：双路径锚定（DPA）引入自条件桥梁，沿两条信息匹配路径对齐参考与 rollout 行为，防止特权依赖行为传递；双源锚定（DSA）在参考到rollout和rollout到参考两个方向应用这些路径，减少对特权参考指导的依赖，同时保持正确性监督。

Result: 实验表明DAPD显著缓解特权幻觉，在Qwen3-4B上平均提升+2.00分，且增益跨规模持续，4B提升+2.69，32B提升+2.78。

Conclusion: DAPD有效解决了OPSD中的特权幻觉问题，通过消除信息不对称实现更稳健的蒸馏性能，且在不同模型规模上均表现出一致改进。

Abstract: On-policy (self) distillation (OPSD) is increasingly adopted for language-model post-training. It strengthens the teacher with privileged information but can induce a privilege illusion: the student learns privilege-dependent behavior it cannot reproduce from its inference-time context, yet behaves as if the training-time privileged information remained available, ultimately degrading performance. In this paper, we identify information asymmetry between the privileged teacher and the student at inference as the root cause of this failure in OPSD. To resolve this asymmetry, we propose Dual-Anchored Policy Distillation (DAPD), a unified framework with two levels of anchoring. Dual-Path Anchoring (DPA) introduces a self-conditioned bridge and aligns reference and rollout behavior along two matched-information paths, preventing privilege-dependent behavior from being transferred to the inference-time student. Dual-Source Anchoring (DSA) applies these paths in both reference-to-rollout and rollout-to-reference directions, reducing reliance on privileged reference guidance while preserving correctness supervision. Extensive experiments show that DAPD significantly alleviates privilege illusion, outperforming OPSD on Qwen3-4B by +2.00 points on average across tasks. Notably, its gains persist across scales, reaching +2.69 at 4B and +2.78 at 32B.

</details>


### [264] [An AI-Based Decision-Support Pipeline for Day-Ahead Photovoltaic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02088)
*Fariba Dehghan,Sebastian Stein,Vahid Yazdanpanah,Stephanie Gauthier,Masood Nazari*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向部署的环境AI管道，用于英国充电站点的日前小时级光伏预测，通过时间戳校正、泄漏安全的太阳几何/晴空指数特征、短期大气上下文和验证学习堆叠集成，相比智能持久性基线在随机日块评估下将日间归一化RMSE降低约32%，在滚动源协议下降低9%，并优于最强单模型基线约6.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新建光伏站点数据记录短且不完善，标准日前预测面临挑战（如持久性/物理基线对校准和时序对齐敏感，单一机器学习模型可能只捕获一种数据结构并在非时序验证下夸大技能），而预测误差会影响充电可用性、储能调度和下游控制，因此需要一种部署导向的稳健预测方法。

Method: 构建部署导向的环境AI管道：校正时间戳约定，构造泄漏安全的太阳几何和晴空指数特征，添加短期大气上下文，并通过验证学习的堆叠（stacking）组合互补预测器；评估采用随机日块和滚动源两种协议，以智能持久性和晴空基线为对比。

Result: 最佳集成在随机日块评估下使日间归一化RMSE比智能持久性降低约32%，在滚动源协议下降低9%；相对最强个体机器学习基线分别降低6.6%和6.4%。

Conclusion: 物理感知的堆叠集成能在有限站点数据下支持光伏预测，但其价值依赖于模型类别、评估协议和部署上下文，需根据具体场景权衡选择。

Abstract: Reliable photovoltaic (PV) forecasts are needed for low-carbon energy systems, but newly deployed sites often have short, imperfect records. This makes standard day-ahead forecasting difficult: persistence and physical baselines can be sensitive to calibration and timestamp alignment, while single machine-learning models may capture only one structure in the data and overstate skill under non-temporal validation. We study this problem at a United Kingdom charging-station site, where PV forecast errors affect charging availability, storage scheduling, and downstream control. Using measured inverter output and publicly available meteorological inputs, we develop a deployment-oriented environmental-AI pipeline for day-ahead hourly PV forecasting. The pipeline corrects timestamp conventions, constructs leakage-safe solar-geometry and clearness-index features, adds short-term atmospheric context, and combines complementary predictors through validation-learned stacking. Against smart persistence, a clear-sky baseline that adjusts recent PV output using expected clear-sky irradiance, the best ensemble reduces daylight normalised RMSE by about 32% under random day-blocked evaluation and 9% under the stricter rolling-origin protocol. It also reduces daylight RMSE relative to the strongest individual machine-learning baseline by 6.6% and 6.4%, respectively. The results show that physics-aware stacking can support PV forecasts from limited site data, but its value depends on model class, evaluation protocol, and deployment context.

</details>


### [265] [CoEvo-Mem: Co-Evolving Retrieval Policy and Memory Bank for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01739)
*Bowen Ye,Yongchao Xu,Zhijian Li,Xiang Yin,Junkai Ma,Wenzhao Li*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出CoEvo-Mem，一个闭环框架，协同进化检索策略与记忆库，在七个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法割裂优化检索或记忆更新，忽视二者间的反馈循环：检索决定哪些记忆被使用，记忆更新又影响未来检索。

Method: 冻结LLM生成查询改写和路由先验，轻量残差路由器在线校正；检索上下文作为耦合接口，任务结果分配路由信用，轨迹反馈更新记忆值和图关系；交替更新路由器（固定记忆库）与进化记忆库（固定检索策略）以缓解非平稳性。

Result: 在七个多样化基准上取得最先进性能，验证检索-记忆协同进化的有效性。

Conclusion: 检索与记忆的闭环协同进化对长程LLM代理至关重要，CoEvo-Mem为相关研究提供新范式。

Abstract: As memories accumulate across tasks and sessions, the performance of long-term LLM agents depends jointly on query-specific retrieval and continual memory refinement. However, existing methods typically optimize either memory access, through iterative query refinement or adaptive retrieval policies, or memory evolution such as structural update. This separation overlooks a fundamental feedback loop: retrieval determines which memories receive usage signals, while updated memory bank reshape future retrieval. We propose \textbf{CoEvo-Mem}, a closed-loop framework for co-evolving the retrieval policy and memory bank. For each query, a frozen LLM generates route-specific query rewrites and a routing prior, which a lightweight residual router corrects online. The retrieved context serves as the coupling interface between the two learning processes: task outcomes assign credit to routing decisions, while trajectory-conditioned feedback updates memory values and graph relations. These updates alter how memories are ranked and selected for subsequent queries, thereby closing the feedback loop. To mitigate coupling induced non-stationarity, CoEvo-Mem alternates between updating the router with the memory bank fixed and evolving the memory bank with the retrieval policy fixed.
