Beyond MMPO, we further develop a general moment-transformation framework that systematically induces different moment profiles and provides a unified view of a broader family of policy optimization objectives. Experiments across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks and models of different scales demonstrate that MMPO consistently outperforms strong baselines. We hope this moment-based perspective offers new insights into the design of policy optimization objectives for LLM reasoning.

</details>


### [302] [MEGRAG: Multi-Granular Evidence Graphs for Answer-Aware Multi-Hop RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02195)
*Weidong Bao,Yingying Sun,Jun Yang,Yilin Wang,Zili Wei,Yubin Bao,Fangling Leng,Minghe Yu,Tiancheng Zhang,Ge Yu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出MEGRAG框架，通过路径结构的多粒度证据图支持多跳问答，平衡信息密度与噪声，并利用中间答案判断查询是否解决以减少错误累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迭代RAG方法存在两个局限：单粒度证据难以平衡信息密度与上下文噪声；聚合中间步骤证据后直接回答原问题，易导致冗余和错误累积。

Method: 离线构建跨粒度索引（段落-句子-三元组）；在线检索时从紧凑三元组开始，按需补充句子或段落上下文，基于中间答案和先前推理判断原查询是否已解决，未解决则生成聚焦的下一个查询。

Result: 在多种RAG基线上持续取得一致性性能提升。

Conclusion: MEGRAG通过多粒度证据图和答案感知的迭代决策，有效改进了多跳RAG的准确性和效率。

Abstract: Multi-hop question answering is a fundamental challenge in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), because deriving an answer requires integrating dispersed evidence. Iterative RAG (iRAG) is widely used for this challenge, but existing methods have two limitations. First, most methods still support each reasoning step with single-granularity evidence, making it difficult to balance information density and contextual noise. Second, existing methods often answer the original question only after aggregating evidence retrieved across intermediate steps, so redundant evidence and intermediate retrieval errors may accumulate and degrade the final answer. To address these limitations, we propose MEGRAG, an answer-aware framework that represents multi-hop reasoning as a path-structured multi-granular evidence graph. Offline, MEGRAG links passages to their sentences and extracted triples through a cross-granularity index. Online, it retrieves passages for the current query and selects aligned evidence, starting with compact triples and adding sentence or passage context as needed. MEGRAG uses the resulting intermediate answer and prior reasoning to decide whether the Initial Query has been resolved. If not, it identifies the missing information and formulates a focused next query; otherwise, it stops retrieval and returns the answer. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent gains over a diverse set of RAG baselines.

</details>


### [303] [Harness-R1: Learning to Edit Executable Runtime Harnesses from Agent Failure Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02276)
*Shuai Shao,Kangning Zhang,Qingyao Li,Shijian Wang,Hao Wang,Wenxiang Jiao,Yuan Lu,Yi Guo,Weiwen Liu,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出Harness-R1，首个将现有可执行运行时的生命周期编辑作为可学习能力的方法，通过强化学习训练专用工程智能体，使冻结的目标智能体在WebShop、ALFWorld和DBBench上成功率提升9.3个百分点，并支持后续目标微调带来额外增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体在部署中持续积累交互轨迹，但行为固定；现有方法仅更新权重，忽略了可以通过改进智能体的上下文构建、工具中介、动作验证和恢复执行等‘外壳’（harness）来提升性能。已有编辑方式依赖固定规则，无法根据实际任务成功率优化。

Method: 提出Harness-R1框架，包含两个组件：一个9B参数的专用‘外壳工程师’（harness engineer），通过在线强化学习（GRPO）后训练，以编辑目标智能体的可执行运行时代码；冷启动阶段用监督微调初始化编辑策略，训练时冻结目标智能体，利用同批次重跑结果作为奖励信号，只更新工程师参数。

Result: 在WebShop、ALFWorld和DBBench基准上，使用vanilla Qwen3.5-9B作为目标时，成功率从44.3%提升至53.6%（+9.3百分点）；若先对目标智能体进行直接微调，专用工程师进一步将平均成功率从59.2%提升至64.2%（+5.0百分点），且增益在目标微调前后均保持。

Conclusion: 结果表明Harness-R1能有效学习改进智能体外壳，且目标微调前后的增益表明外壳工程师与目标智能体可以协同进化，为智能体生命周期内的持续优化提供新方向。

Abstract: Agents built around large language models continually accumulate interaction trajectories during deployment, yet their behavior typically remains fixed. Beyond updating model weights, these trajectories can improve the agent harness that constructs context, mediates tools, validates actions, and recovers execution. We introduce Harness-R1, the first method, to our knowledge, that makes failure-conditioned, lifecycle-wide editing of an existing executable runtime a learned capability. It post-trains a dedicated harness engineer with online reinforcement learning so that its edits are optimized for the realized task success they produce, rather than proposed by a fixed editor. A separate 9B engineer converts batches of target-agent failures into validated executable patches; fresh same-batch reruns of the frozen target provide outcome rewards, so training updates only the engineer. Cold-start supervised fine-tuning initializes this editing policy, which is then trained online with group-relative policy optimization. Across WebShop, ALFWorld, and DBBench, Harness-R1 raises vanilla Qwen3.5-9B success from 44.3% to 53.6% (+9.3 percentage points). After direct target-agent fine-tuning, a target-specific engineer raises the average further from 59.2% to 64.2% (+5.0 points); because these gains hold both before and after fine-tuning the target, Harness-R1 points toward co-evolving the harness engineer and the target agent.

</details>


### [304] [Shared Prefixes, Better Credit: Adaptive Routing for Multi-Agent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02291)
*Yiqing Liu,Zihao Wang,Hantao Yao,Wu Liu,Yongdong Zhang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出TreeCredit，一种基于共享前缀信用分配的高效自适应多智能体推理框架，通过状态匹配的后继比较估计算子效用，训练轻量级路由器，在保持精度的同时显著降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应多智能体推理方法依赖查询级标签或轨迹级回报进行路由决策，这种粗粒度监督无法准确估计多步协作中单个算子的状态条件效用，导致路由效率低下。

Method: 构建共享前缀协作树，从同一中间状态扩展候选算子，根据完整后继的终端正确性与累计额外成本为每个状态-算子对分配正确性优先的后缀信用，转化为状态局部偏好以训练成对状态路由器，在推理时动态选择下一个可接受算子。

Result: 在六个推理基准上，TreeCredit略微提升准确率，同时大幅降低推理成本，实现优于代表性多智能体推理方法的准确率-成本权衡。

Conclusion: TreeCredit通过精细的信用分配和轻量级路由，实现了自适应多智能体推理中准确率与推理效率的更优平衡，验证了状态匹配信用分配的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-agent reasoning (MAR) improves reasoning reliability through iterative solution exchange and refinement. Existing adaptive MAR methods typically learn routing decisions from query-level labels or trajectory-level returns, but such coarse supervision cannot accurately estimate the state-conditioned utility of individual operators in multi-step collaboration. We propose TreeCredit, a shared-prefix credit assignment framework for efficient adaptive MAR. Its core insight is to estimate operator utility through state-matched downstream comparisons, rather than directly attributing trajectory-level outcomes to preceding decisions. TreeCredit constructs shared-prefix collaboration trees by expanding candidate operators from the same intermediate state and assigns each state--operator pair a correctness-prioritized suffix credit based on the terminal correctness and cumulative additional cost of its complete continuation. These structured credits are converted into state-local operator preferences to train a lightweight pairwise state router, which dynamically selects the next admissible operator during inference. Experiments on six reasoning benchmarks show that TreeCredit modestly improves accuracy while substantially reducing inference cost, achieving a better accuracy--cost trade-off than representative MAR methods.

</details>


### [305] [MechGeo: Autoformalizing and Proving Euclidean Geometry in Lean 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02295)
*Hao Shen,Junyu Guo,Tian Cui,Yuxuan Xiao,Lihong Zhi*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出MechGeo，一个原生Mathlib的智能体框架，联合解决欧几里得几何的忠实自动形式化与认证证明构建，在IMO几何问题上取得最大规模的内核验证Lean证明集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动形式化方法缺乏对几何问题的结构诊断和语义评估，且几何证明的自动化与形式化验证之间存在鸿沟，难以实现大规模可信的几何推理。

Method: GeoFormalizer将非形式化问题表示为GeoIR，确定性翻译为Lean 4，并通过结构诊断和语义评估迭代修复候选语句；GeoProver构建证明计划、推导中间引理，并选择性代数化子目标，使用Singular/SymPy生成证书但最终由Lean内核验证所有证明和反例。

Result: 在43个历史IMO几何问题中，GeoFormalizer生成的形式语句被证明29个，其余14个构造了Lean验证的反例并在专家修正后全部证明；在LEAP的Lean-IMO-Bench上，14个几何陈述中首次证明12个，正式反驳剩余2个并证明修复后的陈述。

Conclusion: 反例引导的诊断、几何推理和认证符号计算构成了可信形式几何的实用基础，显著提升了自动形式化和证明能力，尤其对直接翻译较弱的模型改善明显。

Abstract: We present MechGeo, a Mathlib native agentic framework that jointly addresses faithful autoformalization and certified proof construction for Euclidean geometry. In this framework, GeoFormalizer represents informal problems in GeoIR, deterministically translates them into Lean 4, and iteratively repairs candidate statements using structural diagnostics and semantic evaluation. GeoProver constructs geometric proof plans, derives intermediate lemmas, and selectively algebraizes suitable subgoals through a library verified in Lean. Singular or SymPy may generate algebraic certificates, but all resulting proofs and counterexamples are checked by Lean's kernel. Experiments across seven LLM backbones show substantial improvements in autoformalization, particularly for models with weaker direct translation performance. On 43 historical IMO geometry problems, GeoFormalizer generates formal statements that GeoProver proves in 29 cases; for the remaining 14, it constructs counterexamples verified in Lean and proves all repaired statements after expert correction. Together with IMO 2026 Problem 2, this yields, to the best of our knowledge, the largest reported collection of automated, kernel-checked Lean proofs for IMO geometry problems. On the 14 geometry statements in LEAP's Lean-IMO-Bench, MechGeo proves 12 for the first time, formally refutes the remaining two, and proves both repaired statements. These results establish counterexample guided diagnosis, geometric reasoning, and certified symbolic computation as a practical foundation for trustworthy formal geometry.

</details>


### [306] [Hard Constraints, Smooth Gradients: Learning Feasible Inventory Policies via Differentiable Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02343)
*Patrick Helm,Jan-Niklas Doerr,Joren Gijsbrechts,Stefan Minner*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种将可微凸优化模块嵌入深度强化学习策略的方法，通过神经网络输出连续目标、二次规划投影到松弛可行集、对偶信息整数映射恢复整数性，从而在具有相互依赖硬约束的大规模组合动作空间中实现可行且可扩展的决策，并在多级生产库存问题中取得优于基线的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合整数线性规划在随机环境下扩展性差，而深度强化学习虽有可扩展性，但现有方法要么仅惩罚约束，要么依赖在约束交互时会失效的可行性机制，难以同时满足灵活约束处理和规模化需求。

Method: 策略内嵌入可微凸优化模块：神经网络生成连续动作目标，二次规划将其投影至松弛可行集，再利用对偶信息进行整数映射恢复整数性并保证可行性；在可微模拟器下，通过路径梯度端到端训练。

Result: 在小规模实例上平均最优性差距低于1%；在较大网络中优于最新echelon base-stock策略最高9.75%，优于滚动时域多阶段随机规划至少7.7%；在ASML工业案例中平均成本降低最高3.22%，且在紧约束高波动系统中节省最大。

Conclusion: 深度强化学习能够在具有相互依赖硬约束的序列决策问题中带来显著经济收益，所提可行性机制误差有界且可及全可行空间，为实际复杂约束下的决策优化提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Many operational problems are constrained sequential decision processes with large, combinatorial action spaces and interdependent feasibility constraints. Mixed-integer linear programs (MILPs) handle such constraints flexibly but scale poorly in stochastic environments. Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) promises scalable decision rules, but existing methods either penalize constraints rather than enforce them, or rely on feasibility mechanisms that break down once constraints interact. We bridge this gap by embedding a differentiable convex optimization module inside the policy: a neural network proposes continuous action targets, a quadratic program projects them onto the relaxed feasible set, and a dual-informed integer mapping restores integrality while preserving feasibility. Given a differentiable simulator, the policy trains end to end from sampled trajectories using pathwise gradients, while handling hard constraints with similar flexibility to MILPs. We show that our feasibility enforcement has bounded error relative to an exact integer projection and ensures the entire feasible action space is reachable. We apply the method to multi-echelon production-inventory planning under shared resource and material constraints. Our policy attains an average optimality gap below 1% on small instances. It further outperforms state-of-the-art echelon base-stock policies by up to 9.75% and a rolling-horizon multi-stage stochastic program by at least 7.7% in larger networks. On an industry-scale case study from ASML, it reduces average cost by up to 3.22% relative to the best-known benchmark policy. The savings are largest where planning is hardest: in tightly capacitated systems with high demand variability. More broadly, our work shows that DRL can deliver economically significant savings in sequential decision problems with interdependent hard constraints, which are widespread in practice.

</details>


### [307] [Mamba with Hierarchical Memory: Solving Representation Bottleneck in Long Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02347)
*Qinwen Wang,Jieping Luo,Aoxiang Qin,Ruoyu Zhao,Jianxiong Tang,Wei Zhang,Zhichao Lu,Luziwei Leng*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出层级记忆Mamba（HMM），通过引入工作记忆和长期记忆机制，缓解循环线性注意力模型在长序列建模中的状态容量瓶颈，仅增加2%参数即可在检索与推理任务上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mamba等循环线性注意力模型虽具备线性复杂度，但固定容量的循环状态限制了其长序列建模能力，亟需一种更高效的长程记忆增强机制。

Method: 在预训练Mamba骨干上，利用轻量工作记忆从骨干隐藏状态（快速感官记忆）中提取段落级慢语义，随后压缩为持久长期记忆以供任务相关检索，形成层级化语义处理流程。

Result: 在Passkey Retrieval和LongBench-E上，检索成功率提升34.3%–37.1%，推理准确率提升1.6%–14.2%，额外参数仅2%，训练开销极小。

Conclusion: HMM通过层级记忆架构有效突破了循环线性注意力模型的表示瓶颈，同时赋予模型跨任务泛化能力，为长序列建模提供了高效且可扩展的方案。

Abstract: Recurrent linear attention models (RLAs) such as Mamba offer efficient linear-time sequence modeling as an alternative to Transformers, yet their fixed-capacity recurrent states limit long-sequence modeling. Drawing inspiration from hierarchical human memory, we propose Hierarchical Memory Mamba (HMM) to address this limitation. Building upon a pre-trained Mamba backbone, HMM integrates a lightweight working memory that extracts slow paragraph-level semantics (PLS) from the fast sensory memory embedded in the backbone's hidden states. The PLS is subsequently compressed into persistent long-term memory for task-relevant retrieval. The hierarchical processing of semantic information overcomes the representation bottleneck of RLAs and endows HMM cross-task generalization through parametric learning, which is not observed in other long-context enhanced Mamba variants. Evaluations on Passkey Retrieval and LongBench-E tasks demonstrate that HMM improves retrieval success by 34.3--37.1% and reasoning accuracy by 1.6--14.2% over strong Mamba-based models, while adding only 2% extra parameters and with minimal training overhead.

</details>


### [308] [KC-Agent: A Dual-Process Cognitive Architecture for Efficient ML Model Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02351)
*Gusseppe Bravo-Rocca,Jordi Guitart,Ajay Dholakia,David Ellison,Puneet Jain*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出KC-Agent，一种结合System 1快速模式识别与System 2增量更新的双过程认知架构，用于自动化机器学习模型改进，在五个数据集（含NASA涡扇真实退化数据）上实现76.8%准确率与13.2秒执行时间，优于多种基线，并借助知识整合机制获得91%加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据漂移导致生产环境中的机器学习系统性能下降，需持续更新模型，但现有自动化方法要么计算成本高，要么缺乏可靠性。受人类认知双过程理论启发，希望构建兼顾效率与质量的模型改进系统。

Method: 设计双过程架构：System 1基于结构化记忆快速匹配历史成功解决方案，System 2执行深思熟虑的增量更新；引入原子变更原则（保证更新可验证）与回滚能力（确保安全），并通过知识整合机制将System 2的成果沉淀到System 1的记忆中，避免重复计算。

Result: 在五个数据集上达到SOTA性能（76.8%准确率），执行时间13.2秒；相比CodeAct、ToT、ReAct、Reflexion分别提升2.4%、3.6%、8.0%、8.9%；LLM评审团给出8.33/10的Smartness评分，高于基线；知识整合机制带来91%的速度提升且准确率更高。

Conclusion: KC-Agent证明了认知启发式双过程架构在自动化ML改进中的理论有效性与实际可行性，尤其适合处理复杂真实世界的数据漂移场景，为可靠、高效的生产级模型更新提供了新路径。

Abstract: Data drift poses significant challenges for machine learning systems in production, requiring continuous model updates to maintain performance. We present KC-Agent, a dual-process cognitive architecture for automated ML model improvement that combines fast pattern recognition (System 1) with deliberate incremental updates (System 2). Our approach implements structured memory systems enabling System 1 to leverage successful solutions previously discovered by System 2, achieving efficient pattern-based responses without costly re-computation. KC-Agent incorporates atomic change principles and rollback capabilities to ensure reliable, verifiable updates in production environments. We evaluate our method on five datasets including real-world NASA turbofan data with authentic temporal degradation and synthetic datasets with controlled drift scenarios. KC-Agent achieves state-of-the-art performance (76.8% accuracy) while maintaining optimal efficiency (13.2s execution time), outperforming established cognitive architectures: CodeAct (+2.4%), Tree of Thoughts (+3.6%), ReAct (+8.0%), and Reflexion (+8.9%). Consensus evaluation by a panel of state-of-the-art LLMs confirms superior strategic efficacy (8.33/10 Smartness score), significantly outperforming baseline agents. The knowledge consolidation mechanism delivers 91% speedup over the slow variant while maintaining higher accuracy. Our approach demonstrates both theoretical foundations and practical viability for cognitive-inspired automated ML improvement systems capable of handling complex real-world data drift scenarios.

</details>


### [309] [Faster-WAM: Do World Action Models Need Deep Action Modules?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02365)
*Liheng Ma,Rui Heng Yang,Zhanguang Zhang,Mateo Clemente,Ziwen Hu,Tongtong Cao,Yingxue Zhang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出DoT设计原则及其实例Faster-WAM，通过将轻量级动作头连接到预训练视频Transformer骨干上，显著降低推理延迟（66.5ms，较Fast-WAM提速3.2倍），同时保持竞争性能与强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAMs将动作模块深度与视频骨干绑定，导致计算开销大、推理延迟高。

Method: DoT将视频Transformer作为表示中枢，通过对接接口融合所有层的键值并应用RoPE重对齐，仅用单层动作头即可访问全部层级特征。

Result: 在LIBERO和RoboTwin 2.0上取得竞争性性能，在LIBERO-Plus上展现强分布外泛化，端到端延迟仅66.5ms。

Conclusion: 视频中心的DoT架构支持灵活的任务特定头设计，兼顾低延迟、强动作预测性能和鲁棒泛化。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) couple robot action prediction with video world models. Existing WAMs with shared-backbone and Mixture-of-Transformers designs generally tie the depth of the action module to that of the video backbone, resulting in substantial computational overhead and high inference latency. To address this limitation, we introduce Dock of Transformer (DoT), a video-centric design principle that treats a pretrained video Transformer as a representation hub and connects lightweight output-heads through docking interfaces. This enables flexible output-head design while providing direct access to representations from all layers of the backbone. We then introduce \textbf{Faster-WAM}, an instantiation of DoT for WAMs, which docks a single-layer action head onto a 30-layer video backbone. The docking interface fuses keys and values from all video layers and applies RoPE realignment. Without additional embodied pretraining, Faster-WAM achieves competitive performance on LIBERO and RoboTwin 2.0 while demonstrating strong out-of-distribution generalization on LIBERO-Plus. Faster-WAM also achieves the lowest end-to-end latency in our controlled comparison, requiring only 66.5 ms per inference --- a \(3.2\times\) speedup over Fast-WAM. Overall, these results demonstrate that the video-centric DoT architecture supports flexible task-specific head design while delivering low inference latency, strong action-prediction performance, and robust generalization.

</details>


### [310] [Cooperative Coevolution for Resource-Constrained Agentic LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02391)
*Zhiyuan Wang,Shengcai Liu,Jiahao Wu,Ning Lu,Hui Ouyang,Shaofeng Zhang,Haoze Lv,Ke Tang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: CoPES通过将全参数空间分解为低维子空间进行协作搜索，在有限GPU资源下显著提升了演化策略训练工具型LLM智能体的效率，以不足全参数GRPO八分之一的显存恢复了其92%的验证精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具型LLM智能体的梯度后训练内存开销过高，而演化策略虽省内存但训练时间过长，尤其资源受限时GPU小时数成为瓶颈。

Method: 提出协作参数子空间演化策略，采用协同进化将全参数空间分解为多个低维子空间并合作搜索，兼顾内存效率与优化速度。

Result: 在数学任务的Qwen3.5-4B智能体上，CoPES在同等GPU小时预算下恢复GRPO验证精度增益的92%（标准ES仅67%），所需显存低于全参数GRPO的1/8，且在五个基准的pass@k指标上全面优于标准ES和LoRA-GRPO，问答任务亦有增益。

Conclusion: CoPES在资源受限场景下实现了内存与训练时间之间的更优权衡，为智能体LLM后训练提供了高效可行的新方法。

Abstract: Tool-using large language model (LLM) agents produce long, multi-turn trajectories, making gradient-based post-training memory-intensive. Evolution strategies (ES) enable memory-efficient full-parameter post-training without backpropagation and can eventually match the performance of gradient-based reinforcement learning (RL). However, resource-constrained settings typically offer only a few GPUs, so the high GPU-hour requirements of ES translate into prohibitively long training times. To address this, we introduce Cooperative Parameter-subspace Evolution Strategy (CoPES), a cooperative coevolutionary method that decomposes the full parameter space into lower-dimensional subspaces and searches over them cooperatively to improve optimization efficiency. We post-train a Qwen3.5-4B tool-using agent for the math task and evaluate it on five benchmarks of varying difficulty. Under the GPU-hour budget of full-parameter GRPO's best validation checkpoint, CoPES recovers 92% of GRPO's validation-accuracy gain, versus 67% for standard ES, while its theoretical GPU memory requirement is less than one-eighth that of full-parameter GRPO. It consistently outperforms standard ES and LoRA-based GRPO on all evaluated pass@k metrics across the five benchmarks. Additional experiments further show the advantage of CoPES on the question-answering task. These results demonstrate an improved trade-off between memory requirements and training time for agentic LLM post-training under resource constraints. The code is open-sourced in https://github.com/MetaronWang/CoPES

</details>


### [311] [xPress: Parallel Refinement for Diffusion Drafters in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02438)
*Zheng Wang,Davis Wertheimer,Yu Chin Fabian Lim,Mudhakar Srivatsa,Raghu K. Ganti,Minjia Zhang,Naigang Wang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: xPress是一个轻量级因果细化器，通过并行细化恢复扩散草稿器（如dFlash）缺失的因果依赖，提升接受长度约30%，端到端吞吐量平均提升1.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散草稿器单次前向生成整块草稿令牌，但最终采样时各位置独立，导致草稿为边缘分布而非联合分布，令牌间缺乏因果依赖，易被目标模型早期拒绝，限制接受长度。

Method: 在扩散草稿后添加轻量级因果细化器，通过并行细化一次性协调整个扩散块，在不使用逐令牌循环的情况下恢复并传播因果依赖，从而修正草稿序列的联合概率。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B上，覆盖数学、代码和聊天七个基准，接受长度平均提升约30%（最高+56%），端到端解码吞吐量平均提升约1.3倍（最高1.7倍）。

Conclusion: xPress有效解决了扩散草稿器缺乏因果关系的问题，显著提升推测解码的接受长度和整体吞吐量，且开销低，适合实际部署。

Abstract: Block-diffusion drafters like dFlash generate an entire block of draft tokens in a single forward pass, drastically reducing the overhead of multiple-token drafting in speculative decoding. The crucial final step of the single-pass discrete denoising process involves using the logit distribution at each position to sample conditionally independent tokens. The resulting draft is thus a set of per-position marginals, rather than a joint distribution: no draft token is guaranteed to depend on its predecessors. Such independently sampled marginals tend to produce sequences with tokens that are individually likely, but jointly improbable under the target model's distribution, which verifies each token conditionally. This can cause early rejection and limits acceptance length. To address this, we propose xPress as a means to restore the missing causality in diffusion drafters. xPress is a lightweight causal refiner that reconciles the whole diffusion block at once through parallel refinement, restoring and propagating causal dependencies across the draft without a token-by-token loop. On Qwen3-8B, across seven math, code, and chat benchmarks, xPress raises acceptance length by about 30% on average (up to +56%) and its end-to-end decoding throughput by about 1.3 on average (up to 1.7) compared to the original dFlash diffusion drafter.

</details>


### [312] [Agentic Commerce World: An Auditable and Verifiable Environment for Vibe Commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02441)
*Shicheng Fan,Mingdai Yang,Duohao Wang,Canyu Chen,Yongfeng Zhang,Hua Wei,Manling Li,Julian McAuley,Kun Zhang,Philip S. Yu,Kejing Yu,Zhiwei Liu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出了Agentic Commerce World（ACWorld），一个用于评估独立买方和卖方智能体在共享市场中交互的基准环境，包含Vibe Commerce协议和两个评估轨道，并通过对10个模型的测试证明过程级证据对准确评估智能体行为至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体评估多关注单方任务或最终状态，缺乏对需要独立控制、目标冲突的买卖双方智能体交互过程的评测，且难以保证行为可审计和结果可复现。

Method: 设计ACWorld环境，通过Vibe Commerce协议在更新共享交易状态前验证智能体动作并记录交互日志；构建包含200任务能力覆盖轨道和60任务大目录轨道（含785,022个可交易商品）的基准，对10个模型进行系统评估。

Result: 模型得分在能力覆盖轨道为65.9%-85.6%，大目录轨道为56.1%-91.4%；分析发现最终状态评估会遗漏错误，不完整轨迹仍含有效过程信号，大目录任务暴露多阶段瓶颈。

Conclusion: 过程级证据（而非仅最终状态）是评估交互式智能体交易的关键，ACWorld为可审计、可复现的商务智能体研究提供了基础平台。

Abstract: In vibe coding, people describe software in natural language and delegate implementation to AI agents. By analogy, vibe commerce allows people to express buying or selling goals in natural language and delegate the corresponding tasks to agents. Commerce, however, requires independently controlled Buyer and Merchant agents to interact in a shared market while preserving their private objectives and distinct authority. We introduce Agentic Commerce World (ACWorld), an environment for evaluating such agents across ongoing transactions. Through its Vibe Commerce Protocol (VCP), ACWorld validates agent actions before updating shared transaction state and records the resulting interactions, making agent behavior auditable and evaluation reproducible. The ACWorld Benchmark contains a 200-task capability-coverage track and a 60-task large-catalog track that searches 785,022 transactable listings. Across ten models, mean scores range from 65.9% to 85.6% and from 56.1% to 91.4%, respectively. Our analysis shows that process-level evidence is necessary: final state alone can miss evaluated errors, incomplete trajectories still retain useful process signals, and large-catalog tasks expose bottlenecks across stages.

</details>


### [313] [Right Answer, Wrong Method: Shortcut Hacking Misleads the Evaluation of LLM Reasoning on Frontier Science Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02442)
*Xuan Ren,Weiqi Zhai,Tianle Pu,Yihua Zhu,Yihua Zhu,Hu Wei,Bing Zhao*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型在科学推理基准测试中常通过无效捷径（数值搜索、枚举、猜测等）得到正确答案，即“方案破解”，且随难度增加而加剧，从常见题的2.2%升至奥林匹克题的28.3%和HLE的37.4%。反破解策略显著降低报告准确率，说明仅用答案评估会高估模型能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学推理基准仅以最终答案正确性评估LLM，但正确答案可能来自无效捷径而非目标推理能力，导致评估失真。

Method: 系统分析方案破解现象在不同难度、科学领域和前沿模型中的分布；开发专家启发的反破解策略，包括自动评判器和测试时指令。

Result: 方案破解率随难度上升（2.2%至37.4%）；前沿模型中8.2%-44.1%的正确答案被识别为破解；反破解策略大幅降低报告准确率，而对正确非破解准确率影响较小。

Conclusion: 仅答案评估会显著高估前沿LLM的科学推理能力，需结合过程验证以准确衡量模型能力。

Abstract: Scientific reasoning benchmarks typically evaluate large language models (LLMs) using final-answer accuracy. However, a correct answer does not necessarily demonstrate the reasoning capability targeted by the problem. We identify Solution Hacking, a failure mode in which an LLM reaches the correct answer through invalid shortcuts, such as numerical search, enumeration, guessing, or answer-first verification, without providing a valid task-targeted derivation. We systematically analyze this phenomenon across difficulty levels, scientific domains, and frontier models. Solution hacking increases sharply with benchmark difficulty, from 2.2\% on common problems to 28.3\% on Olympiad-level problems and 37.4\% on HLE. Moreover, 8.2\%-44.1\% of answers credited as correct across frontier models are identified as hacked solutions. We further develop expert-inspired anti-hacking strategies, including an automatic judge and a test-time instruction. The results show that suppressing shortcut behavior substantially reduces reported accuracy while having a smaller effect on correct and non-hacked accuracy. These findings reveal that answer-only evaluation can overestimate the scientific reasoning capabilities of frontier LLMs.

</details>


### [314] [ParEvalLayer: When Partial LLM-Agent Evaluations Support a Decision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02444)
*Wei-Jung Huang,Bonan Shen*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出ParEvalLayer决策层，通过预定义比较策略和部分运行结果，在早期判断代理系统间比较结论是否与完整评估一致，发现不同基准所需任务比例差异大，强调报告应包含决策规则和未决比较数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理评估中任务结果提前产生，部分分数不能反映是否支持完整评估结论，早期任务可能遗漏关键部分、廉价任务优先会扭曲样本、易判规则虽准却留大量未决比较，故需一种方法判断部分结果的可信性。

Method: ParEvalLayer读取两个代理系统的成对结果和预先选择的比较策略，在部分运行中记录四种状态（优于阈值、未优于、需更多证据、弃权）；通过回放公开基准数据，模拟早期停止，仅用已观察结果应用策略，并与完整数据结论比对。

Result: 在主要比较规则下，三个公开基准在观察15%-25%任务结果时即达成与完整评估相同的结论；其他基准需要更多任务结果，表明部分分数不能独立支撑结论。

Conclusion: 部分得分本身不足，评估报告应同时说明所用决策规则及仍未决断的比较数量，以透明反映早期结论的可靠性。

Abstract: LLM-agent evaluations often produce task outcomes long before the full benchmark run is complete. A partial score is tempting to report, but it does not show whether the observed tasks support the same conclusion as the completed evaluation. Early tasks can omit important parts of a benchmark, running cheaper tasks first can distort the observed sample, and a rule that decides only easy pairs can appear accurate while leaving many comparisons unresolved. We introduce ParEvalLayer, a decision layer that reads paired outcomes for two agent systems and a comparison policy chosen in advance. For each partial run, it records whether the tested agent system is better by the required amount, is not better by that amount, needs more evidence, or should abstain. We evaluate ParEvalLayer by replaying completed public benchmark data as if each evaluation had stopped earlier. At each point, ParEvalLayer applies the policy using only the outcomes observed so far; if it reaches one of the two comparison judgments, we check whether that judgment matches the completed data for the same system pair. With the main comparison rule, three of the public benchmarks reach the same decision as the completed evaluation after observing only 15% to 25% of task outcomes. Other benchmarks require more task outcomes. This variation shows why a partial score alone is not enough: reports should also state the decision rule and how many comparisons remain without a decision.

</details>


### [315] [Infinite Trace Objectives with Finite Trace Techniques: Translating LTL to LTLf+](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02454)
*Christoph Weinhuber,Maximilian Prokop,Giuseppe De Giacomo,Moshe Y. Vardi*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出首个从LTL到LTLf+的翻译方法，使LTL规范能利用LTLf+基于有限自动机的优势，且渐进复杂度不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LTL求解需先转非确定性自动机再确定化，理论上和实践上都很困难；而LTLf+基于有限字自动机，具有规范最小表示和高效确定化程序，将其能力扩展到LTL可惠及众多AI问题。

Method: 先将LTL公式规范化到Manna-Pnueli层次的语法反应性片段，形成LTLf+的基于片段的结构，再对该片段各组件给出线性翻译。

Result: 得到首个LTL到LTLf+的翻译，且从LTL经LTLf+到自动机的流水线保持双指数复杂度，无渐近代价。

Conclusion: 该翻译使LTLf+的现有技术可直接应用于LTL描述的AI问题，且不损失理论效率。

Abstract: Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) is one of the most widely adopted languages for specifying temporal extended objectives in AI, with applications ranging from reactive synthesis to stochastic planning in Markov decision processes and reinforcement learning. Traditionally, solving any of these problems requires translating the LTL specification to a nondeterministic automata on infinite words and then determinizing it, a step that is notoriously difficult in theory and in practice. Recent work has introduced LTLf+, which lifts the finite-trace logic LTLf to infinite traces. LTLf+ has the same expressive power as LTL, yet it retains most of the crucial advantages of its base logic LTLf. Most reasoning in LTLf+ rests on finite automata on finite words, for which we have not only a canonical minimal representation but also an efficient determinization procedure. In this work we present the first translation from LTL to LTLf+. We first normalize an LTL formula into the syntactic reactivity fragment of the Manna-Pnueli hierarchy, to create the general fragment-based shape of LTLf+. We then present linear translations for each individual component of that fragment. As a consequence of this translation, the expanding body of techniques developed for LTLf+ now becomes available to many AI problems currently formulated in LTL. We further show that this comes at no asymptotic cost, as the pipeline from LTL to automaton via LTLf+ remains doubly exponential.

</details>


### [316] [Real-Time Detection and Repair of LLM Agent Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02464)
*Sunny Dubey*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅基于步骤遥测数据、无需昂贵LLM评判的轻量级代理监控方法，通过回声状态网络集成和确定性验证，以微秒级开销实现高故障检测率，并闭环修复提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在运行中常发生中途失败，而用第二个LLM逐步评判成本过高，因此需要寻找仅从可观测遥测数据中高效检测故障的方法。

Method: 使用在一类回声状态网络集成上附加CUSUM报警的监控器，结合确定性验证（重算工具结果总和、确认必要调用），检测失败后回滚并重放运行。

Result: 监控器在5%误报预算下检测0.71的失败（AUROC 0.872），确定性验证以0误报捕获60%失败，修复环节使任务成功率从52%升至73%，每步开销约200微秒。

Conclusion: 轻量级遥测监控与确定性验证结合，能以极低代价实现有效故障检测与修复，且无需跨部署重训练，为代理可靠性提供实用方案。

Abstract: LLM agents fail mid-episode -- they loop, cascade tool errors, drift off goal, fabricate results, or silently absorb corrupted content -- and the standard remedy, judging every step with a second LLM, costs more than the agent itself. We ask how much detection is achievable from observable step telemetry alone, using monitors costing microseconds per step and trained only on healthy runs.
  On 2,823 committed agent episodes across three frameworks, three local models (qwen2.5 7b/3b, llama3.1 8b) and a commercial API (gemini-2.5-flash), a one-class echo-state-network ensemble with CUSUM alarms detects 0.71 of failures at a 5% false-alarm budget (AUROC 0.872). Its advantage over a memoryless baseline is a monotone function of post-onset horizon (+0.09 at <=3 steps, +0.40 at >=9), predicting its own failure region out of sample on AFTraj-2K. Ranking transfers with no retraining to two corpora from other groups (AFTraj-2K 0.745, ATBench 0.779).
  Monitors carry two burdens: a per-deployment healthy null (they do not transfer -- AUROC 0.527 cold against 0.885 recalibrated) and a residual false-alarm rate. We add a layer carrying neither: deterministic verification, which recomputes a run's stated total from the tool results it actually received and confirms every required call was made. Head-to-head it catches 60% of failures (96% with the coverage check) at 0 of 63 false positives against the monitor's 54% at 17%, transfers unchanged to llama3.1:8b (110 of 110 at 0 of 10), and trips on 0 of 1825 healthy episodes.
  Detection is then closed into repair: each flagged run is rolled back and re-run live, recovering 45% of failures against a 16% resampling control (p=0.0005) and lifting task success from 52% to 73% for about one extra model call per run. The system runs at ~200 microseconds per step, three orders of magnitude below a judge call. Code, traces and results are released.

</details>


### [317] [A Taxonomy of Cognitive Capability Gaps in Generative and Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02553)
*Taye Akinrele,Sindhuja Penchala,Noorbakhsh Amiri Golilarz,Sudip Mittal,Shahram Rahimi*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文通过分类法综述了认知AI的主要能力差距，提出五维框架（持久状态建模、目标导向自主、自我监控控制、环境交互、学习适应），并概述了自适应认知智能架构（ACIA）和评估框架，为迈向AGI提供路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式和智能体AI虽能力强大，但缺乏持续推理、自适应行为、持久记忆和自我调节等认知功能，导致长时间运行可靠性受限；现有研究碎片化，缺乏统一框架来组织和解决这些根本性缺口。

Method: 采用分类法驱动的综述方法，围绕五个认知维度组织文献，每维度分析近期进展、共性局限和开放挑战，并基于此提出概念性ACIA架构和认知中心评估方向。

Result: 识别出各维度关键限制（如状态建模持续性弱、自主目标动态调整不足、自我监控机制薄弱、环境交互深度有限、学习适应性差），并整合出统一分类体系、架构视角和评估框架，揭示通向更可靠长期推理与持续学习的机会。

Conclusion: 分类学、ACIA架构和评估框架共同构成推进认知AI的路线图，强调跨维度整合和长期可靠性的重要性，为最终实现AGI奠定基础。未来需聚焦未解决的挑战，推动自适应、可靠且具备真实认知能力的系统发展。

Abstract: Cognitive AI seeks to move beyond language generation and autonomous task execution toward systems capable of sustained reasoning, adaptive behavior, persistent memory, and self-regulation. While generative and agentic AI have demonstrated impressive capabilities across a wide range of tasks, many fundamental cognitive functions remain fragmented or weakly developed, limiting reliable operation over extended time horizons. This paper presents a taxonomy-driven survey of the major cognitive capability gaps that continue to constrain the development of Cognitive AI. The literature is organized around five dimensions: persistent state modeling, goal-directed autonomy, self-monitoring and control, environment interaction, and learning and adaptation. For each dimension, we review recent advances, identify recurring limitations, and discuss open research challenges. Building on these insights, we outline a conceptual Adaptive Cognitive Intelligence Architecture (ACIA) and examine emerging directions in cognition-centric evaluation. The proposed taxonomy provides a unified framework for organizing existing research, identifying unresolved challenges, and guiding the design of future cognitively capable systems. Together, the taxonomy, architectural perspective, and evaluation framework offer a roadmap for advancing AI systems that exhibit more reliable long-term reasoning, adaptive decision-making, and continual learning. The survey highlights key research opportunities toward more adaptive, reliable, and cognitively capable AI systems, providing a foundation for future progress toward Cognitive AI and, ultimately, Artificial General Intelligence (AGI).

</details>


<div id='Sec'></div>

# Sec [[Back]](#toc)

### [318] [Skillsets on the Chain: A Blockchain-based Zero-Trust Framework for Agentic AI Networking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00104)
*Yayu Gao,Yong Xiao,Hao Hu,Xubo Li,Zhiwei Liu,Yingyu Li,Guangming Shi,Ping Zhang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出TrustAgentNet，一个基于双层区块链的零信任框架，通过全局技能链（CoS）和动态协作链（CoC）解决Agentic AI网络中的声明-能力不一致与安全漏洞问题，在安全、性能与资源开销间实现可调平衡，并通过硬件原型验证了高准确率与自我恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agentic AI网络（AgentNet）依赖第三方技能集与分布式协作，但在信任声明（trust-by-declaration）模式下存在声明与能力不符及安全漏洞，且缺乏有效的审计与验证机制。

Method: 构建双层区块链架构：全局技能链（CoS）管理技能集元数据生命周期，采用专用代理执行链下审计并维持轻量级链上共识；动态任务导向的协作链（CoC）实现无信任分布式协作。同时设计链上-链下集成（ledger-IPFS）以降低链上开销。

Result: 硬件原型显示零信任开销主要由链下推理主导，区块链层仅产生少量账本成本；验证管线在50个AI模型上实现100%准确率（40个诚实技能集全部通过，10个对抗样本全部拦截），在171个ClawHub技能（1478特征）的非AI领域达到83.91%准确率与0.85 F1分数；对抗实验表明系统支持自主技能集自我恢复。

Conclusion: TrustAgentNet有效弥合了信任缺口，以可控开销实现高可靠验证与恶意防御，并具备跨领域泛化能力，为Agentic AI网络提供可行的零信任安全范式。

Abstract: Agentic AI networking (AgentNet) systems rely heavily on third-party skillset implementations and distributed multi-agent collaboration, yet they face major claim-to-capability inconsistencies and security vulnerabilities under trust-by-declaration assumptions. To bridge this gap, this paper proposes TrustAgentNet, a dual-tier blockchain-secured zero-trust framework. Specifically, a global Chain of Skillsets (CoS) governs the lifecycle of skillset metadata with protocols empowered by specialized agents to enforce off-chain auditing while maintaining lightweight on-chain cryptographic consensus. Furthermore, transient, task-oriented Chains of Collaboration (CoC) are dynamically established to enable trustless distributed multi-agent collaboration. Theoretical analysis of the three-way trade-off among security level, task performance, and resource overhead is provided and empirically validated. Experimental results on a hardware prototype demonstrate that compared with no-blockchain trust-by-default baselines, the zero-trust overhead of TrustAgentNet is dominated by off-chain inference, while the blockchain layer incurs minor ledger costs via the ledger-IPFS storage and on/off-chain integration design. Crucially, the proposed verification pipeline achieves a flawless 100% accuracy across 50 AI models, correctly validating 40 honest skillsets and intercepting 10 adversarial ones, and generalizes to non-AI domains with an 83.91% accuracy and a 0.85 F1-score across 1478 features from 171 ClawHub skills. Adversarial experiments further show that TrustAgentNet enables autonomous skillset self-recovery against various malicious attacks.

</details>


### [319] [Deep Learning for Cyber Threat Detection and Mitigation in Healthcare-IoT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00118)
*Mirza Akhi*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文构建了多个真实H-IoT数据集，提出了轻量级TCN和Res-TCN深度学习模型，用于检测和缓解DDoS、SF和MITM攻击，并通过量化部署至边缘设备实现实时防护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有H-IoT安全方案模型复杂、参数多，且数据集质量评估不足，难以适应资源受限环境并保障患者安全。

Method: 开发三个数据集（两个二分类DDoS数据集和一个多分类攻击数据集），设计TCN与Res-TCN模型，引入基于监测频率的检测机制和动态阈值缓解策略，通过量化转为TFLite部署于Raspberry Pi 4。

Result: 模型在MQTT和UDP数据集上有效检测DDoS攻击，边缘部署实现低延迟和低功耗，满足实时性要求。

Conclusion: 建立了从数据集生成、轻量模型设计到边缘部署的完整H-IoT网络安全方案，提升了系统安全性与实用性。

Abstract: Cybersecurity is a fundamental requirement for protecting wearable devices used in healthcare Internet of Things (H-IoT) systems. Security failures in these resource-constrained systems directly compromise patient safety. Physiological data and network traffic are frequent targets of cyberattacks in H-IoT environments. To address these risks, deep learning-based cybersecurity mechanisms for H-IoT often involve complex architectures with large parameter counts. Existing datasets are also rarely assessed for quality, limiting their applicability. However, this research addresses these challenges by developing multiple realistic datasets and proposing lightweight deep learning models, namely the Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN) and Residual TCN (Res-TCN), for H-IoT. It includes two binary classification datasets for Distributed Denial of Service (DDoS) attacks and a multiclass dataset representing Selective Forwarding (SF), Man-in-the-Middle (MITM), and DDoS attacks. The datasets UL-ECE-MQTT-DDoS-H-IoT2025 and UL-ECE-UDP-DDoS-H-IoT2025 are generated in Cooja and ns-3 to capture transmission behaviours and protocol variations. The third dataset, UL-ECE-MultiAttack-H-IoT2025, integrates physiological and network features to represent multiple cyber threats in H-IoT. Building on this, the TCN model is designed to detect and mitigate DDoS attacks over the MQTT and UDP-based datasets. It incorporates a monitoring frequency-based detection mechanism and a dynamic threshold-based mitigation strategy. To enable edge deployment, the model is quantised and converted into TensorFlow Lite (TFLite) for real-time DDoS detection on Raspberry Pi 4, achieving low latency and power-efficient operation in H-IoT. This thesis establishes a deep learning-based cybersecurity defence mechanism encompassing realistic dataset generation, lightweight model design, and edge deployment for securing H-IoT systems.

</details>


### [320] [Stateful Cooperative Agents Safeguarding LLMs Against Evolving Multi-Turn Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00134)
*Siyuan Li,Zehao Liu,Haoyu Li,Xi Lin,Ning Liu,Jun Wu,Jianhua Li,Mohsen Guizani*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出CoopGuard，一种针对多轮对抗攻击的主动防御框架，通过多智能体协作（响应节奏控制、模糊误导、日志取证）将攻击成功率平均降低69%，并将欺骗参与度提升6倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM防御多为被动反应式，难以应对攻击者在多轮交互中动态调整策略的复杂威胁。

Method: 采用协作式多智能体架构，包含三个专用智能体：控制响应节奏以增加攻击成本、生成策略性模糊输出误导攻击者、分析交互日志进行取证并改进防御；由自适应协调机制根据威胁升级动态调整防御策略。

Result: 在EMRA数据集（5,200个样本、8种攻击类型）上，相较最强基线平均降低ASR 69%，DR提升6倍以上，攻击者Token消耗平均增加198.83%。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅能有效抑制有害输出，还能通过持续欺骗性互动消耗攻击者资源，为LLM防御提供了一种主动且自适应的新范式。

Abstract: As LLMs become increasingly integrated into complex applications, their vulnerability to adversarial attacks has raised significant concerns. However, existing defenses remain reactive in nature. This limitation makes it difficult for them to counter sophisticated threats, as adversaries continuously adjust their strategies across multi-turn interactions. In this paper, we present a proactive defense framework for securing LLMs against evolving multi-turn adversarial attacks that combines disruption, misdirection, and adaptation across successive interaction turns. In particular, it employs a cooperative multi-agent architecture in which specialized agents execute complementary defense strategies. These strategies include controlled response pacing to increase attack costs, strategically ambiguous outputs to mislead adversaries into ineffective strategies, and forensic analysis of interaction logs to identify attack patterns and refine defenses. These agents are coordinated by an adaptive mechanism that dynamically adjusts the defense strategy in response to escalating threats. To facilitate comprehensive evaluation, we present the EMRA dataset designed to simulate evolving strategies across multi-turn attacks, including 5,200 adversarial samples across eight attack types. Experimental results on EMRA across multiple LLM backbones show that the proposed framework reduces ASR by 69% on average relative to evaluated state-of-the-art baselines. Beyond suppressing harmful outputs, it sustains deceptive engagement, achieving an average DR more than six times that of the strongest baselines and increasing attacker-token consumption by 198.83% on average relative to evaluated baselines. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/SiyuanLi00/CoopGuard.

</details>


### [321] [Revisiting Classic Thought Experiments to Measure Consciousness for Artificial Intelligence Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00001)
*Peter David Fagan*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 通过守恒一致编码框架，将莱布尼茨磨坊、图灵测试和中文房间重新形式化，提出以行为成功度W_causal_T与操作意识κ_T分别衡量外在表现和内在结构效率，论证两者可分离，并指出该区分对AI安全分析的意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典思想实验长期混淆行为表现与内部理解，缺乏定量区分工具；现有讨论难以解释为何看似同等成功的系统可能在内部组织上存在本质差异，尤其对AI安全评估构成隐患。

Method: 构建玩具符号系统，定义任务性能W_causal_T刻画行为成功，定义操作意识κ_T度量保留内部结构支撑行为的效率；对比未压缩查找系统与紧凑生成系统在同等行为表现下的κ_T差异。

Result: 未压缩查找系统因依赖不断扩展的未复用映射存储导致κ_T较低，而紧凑生成系统通过复用内部结构获得较高κ_T；两者可在行为指标上接近但内在效率显著不同。

Conclusion: 理解应拆分为外在绩效与内在组织两个维度；该分离为后续AI安全分析提供了理论基础，使判断系统是否真正‘理解’或仅表面胜任成为可操作问题。

Abstract: This research note revisits Leibniz's mill, Turing's imitation game, and Searle's Chinese Room through the Conservation-Congruent Encoding (CCE) framework. It formalises a toy symbolic setting in which successful behaviour is measured by task performance ($W_{causal,T}$), while the efficiency with which preserved internal structure supports that behaviour is measured by operational consciousness ($κ_T$). Within this setup, an uncompressed lookup system and a compact generative system can in principle achieve comparable behavioural success, yet diverge sharply in $κ_T$: the former relies on an expanding standing store of unreused mappings, whereas the latter reuses compact internal structure. The note therefore reframes classic disputes about understanding by separating outward performance from the organisation that sustains it, and motivates why this distinction may matter for later AI-safety analysis.

</details>


### [322] [Symbolic Attack Chain Generation from Atomic Red Team Techniques: An Empirical Study of Predicate Representation Granularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00143)
*Ramya Varunsegar*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了LLM与经典规划结合时，攻击动作谓词表示粒度对计划有效性、成本和保真度的影响，发现九类别AALM与五类别简化方案在81.3%的场景中结果一致，粒度主要影响计划内部结构而非可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化攻击链生成对现代网络安全至关重要，但手工构建无法扩展；PDDL规划依赖准确的谓词翻译，而现有AURORA系统的九类别AALM粒度必要性未被验证，需实证检验其影响。

Method: 构建LLM翻译加Fast Downward推理的流水线，对比完整九类别AALM与基于ART执行证据推导的五类别简化方案，在十六种技术语料上评估计划有效性、成本和保真度。

Result: 计划有效性和成本对粒度不敏感，81.3%的实验结果在两种方案下完全一致；更高粒度主要改善计划内部理由的结构分辨率，而非改变攻击链的可行性。

Conclusion: 谓词表示粒度无需过度细化，简化方案即可保持生成攻击链的可靠性；未来可聚焦于提升计划内部解释质量而非增加类别数。

Abstract: Automated attack chain generation is critical for modern cybersecurity, yet manual construction fails to scale as adversary behaviors expand. While classical AI planning using PDDL offers a formal method to automate this process, it relies on the accurate translation of techniques into symbolic predicates. Current state-of-the-art systems like AURORA employ a nine-category Attack Action Linking Model (AALM), but the necessity of this specific granularity remains unvalidated. This work investigates the impact of predicate representation granularity on plan validity, cost, and fidelity. Utilizing a pipeline where a Large Language Model (LLM) performs translation and the Fast Downward engine performs deterministic reasoning, the study compares the full nine-category AALM against a reduced five-category scheme derived empirically from Atomic Red Team (ART) execution evidence. Results from a sixteen-technique corpus demonstrate that plan validity and cost are largely insensitive to granularity, with 81.3% identical outcomes across both schemes. The findings suggest that higher granularity primarily enhances the internal structural resolution of a plan's justification rather than the viability of the generated attack chain itself.

</details>


### [323] [Exposed by Design: A Dynamic Security Assessment of Internet-Facing MCP Servers at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00150)
*Nicolás Padilla*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 首次对互联网暴露的MCP服务器进行动态行为安全评估，发现640个生产服务器中414个存在68个可报告漏洞，91.8%缺乏OAuth认证，且服务器生命周期极短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP自2024年11月发布以来快速普及，公开互联网上已有超2.1万个服务器实例，但缺乏对其安全性的系统性动态评估。

Method: 结合被动发现（11个数据源）与主动动态测试（Corvus框架，34个测试模块覆盖10类MCP漏洞），在2026年7月进行四轮测量，并对确认的服务器实施负责任披露。

Result: 确认640个生产服务器，动态审计414个，发现68个漏洞（SQL注入、SSRF、提示注入、路径遍历）；91.8%无OAuth认证，687个工具暴露无访问控制的shell执行能力，41.6%服务器在三天内消失。

Conclusion: MCP服务器部署存在严重安全缺陷和快速迭代缺乏审查的问题，需加强认证机制与安全测试；Corvus框架已开源以支持后续安全评估。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) has seen rapid adoption since its November 2024 launch, with over 21,000 server instances detectable on the public internet. We present the first dynamic behavioral security assessment of internet-facing MCP servers, combining passive discovery across eleven data sources (crt.sh, HuggingFace, GitHub, npm, Smithery, PyPI, Censys, FOFA, Shodan, glama.ai, and pulsemcp.com) with active dynamic testing using Corvus, a purpose-built framework implementing 34 test modules covering 10 MCP-specific vulnerability classes. Across four measurement runs spanning July 2026, we confirm 640 production MCP servers and dynamically audit 414, uncovering 68 reportable vulnerabilities including SQL injection, SSRF targeting cloud metadata services, prompt template injection, and path traversal via cursor manipulation. We find that 91.8% of dynamically audited servers lack OAuth authentication, 687 tool instances across confirmed servers expose shell execution capabilities without access controls, and 41.6% of confirmed servers disappear within three days between consecutive measurement runs---indicating rapid deployment cycles without security review. We report on our responsible disclosure pipeline and release Corvus as an open-source framework for MCP security evaluation.

</details>


### [324] [From Monoliths to Swarms: A Study of Attack Surface Evolution in the Transition to Multi-Agent Web Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00202)
*Yashaswi Malla,Sandra Siby*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出针对多智能体Web系统的攻击分类法及测试平台WebMASLab，发现MAS引入新的结构攻击面，并揭示电话循环攻击对多数前沿模型有效，防御措施难以通用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的Web代理正从单智能体转向多智能体，后者虽提升任务性能，但角色分解带来全新攻击面，而这一风险尚未被充分理解和系统分类。

Method: 设计攻击向量分类法，构建WebMASLab测试平台，在固定用户任务、工具和浏览器条件下对比单/多智能体架构，评估三种对抗场景及三种条件，并提出MAS特有的电话循环攻击利用跨代理委托造成循环任务。

Result: 电话循环攻击对四种前沿模型中三种平均成功率80%，仅Claude Sonnet 4.6以92%检测率抵抗；其他模型基线检测率0%，提示加固后一种模型最高达33%；提示加固对模型效果差异大（成功率从100%降至8%或仅小幅下降）。

Conclusion: 从单到多智能体Web系统转变改变了安全格局，角色专业化带来性能优化的同时也引入新架构风险，需进一步研究和防御。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based web agents are increasingly evolving from single-agent systems (SAS) to multi-agent systems (MAS). While MAS can lead to improved task performance by decomposing complex tasks across specialized sub-agents, such role decomposition introduces new structural attack surfaces that are absent in SAS. This expanded attack surface remains poorly understood and inadequately categorized.
  To address this, we propose a taxonomy to categorize attack vectors specific to web-based MAS, accounting for vulnerabilities introduced or amplified by the involvement of multiple agents. We further present a test-bed WebMASLab to analyze web agent security against a fully external, web-only adversary. To isolate the effect of architecture, we keep the user task, tool surface, and browser substrate fixed, and compare single- and multi-agent setups. We evaluate three adversarial scenarios, across three conditions (baseline, prompt-hardened, and reasoning-enabled), including a novel MAS-specific Telephone Loop attack that exploits cross-agent delegation to create cyclical task loops. The attack is inert against SAS but compromises MAS when powered by three of the four frontier models evaluated (Claude Sonnet 4.5, GPT-5.2, GPT-5.4), averaging 80% across them at baseline. Only the fourth model, Claude Sonnet 4.6, resists the attack with a 92% detection rate. For the rest, the detection is 0% at baseline, reaching 33% with prompt-hardening for one model. We also show that obvious defenses do not generalize; prompt-hardening collapses one model's ASR from 100% to 8% while providing only modest reduction to the others. Our findings demonstrate that the transition from single- to multi-agent web systems changes the security landscape. Role specialization may not only lead to performance optimization but also introduce new architectural risks that require further study and defenses.

</details>


### [325] [Reflection, Education, Consistency: Towards Best Ethics Practices At Security And Privacy Conferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00282)
*Florian Hantke,Rafael Mrowczynski,Til Dralle,Ben Stock*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文通过访谈顶级安全与隐私会议社区成员，探讨伦理审查的目标与现状，指出缺乏社区内伦理教育是主要障碍，并提出建立开放伦理维基作为协调行动的第一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全与隐私社区对伦理审查争议大，但缺乏经验验证和明确目标，导致政策制定缺乏依据，需要建立共识以指导会议决策。

Method: 回顾四大顶会伦理政策，并对20名资深与初级社区成员（含前程序/研究伦理委员会主席）进行半结构化访谈，分析伦理程序的目标、干预措施及新方案。

Result: 社区正向提高伦理意识和防止不道德论文发表的目标演进，但存在伦理教育缺失、政策复杂且不一致、过度监管引发应付式合规（如用LLM写伦理部分）等问题。

Conclusion: 建议发起社区范围内的协调伦理指导工作，并初步推出开放伦理维基以促进共享学习和政策一致性。

Abstract: Research ethics is a controversial and emotionally charged topic in the security and privacy community, sparking discussions at conferences and on social media. In recent years, some of the leading conferences have introduced interventions such as mandatory ethics sections, with mixed reactions within the community. Program committee chairs and steering committees increasingly emphasize ethics, yet there is limited empirical validation on ethics procedures and interventions, as well as no explicitly communicated goals.
  To support a shared understanding in our community and guide informed decisions at the conference level, we examined past ethics policies at the top-four conferences and conducted in-depth semi-structured interviews with senior and junior (n=20) community members, including some (former) chairs of program and research ethics committees. In these, we explored reasons for and goals of ethics procedures and discussed existing ethics interventions as well as possible new approaches proposed by our team.
  While the community appears to be evolving toward the identified goals, i.e., raising ethical awareness and preventing the publication of unethical work, we identified the lack of ethical education within the community itself as a major obstacle to further progress on this matter. Ethical education is often passed to the universities. Interviewees also mentioned challenges with the complexity of ethics policies and the lack of consistency across different conferences, as well as policies changing year-to-year. Several participants warned that over-regulation may lead to backlash, encouraging mere compliance, such as people turning to LLMs for ethics sections rather than undertaking in-depth consideration. In response, we suggest a coordinated, community-wide ethics steering effort and take a first step by introducing an open ethics wiki.

</details>


### [326] [Leak It: A Probabilistic Approach to Training-Data Extraction from Black-Box Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00144)
*Victor Maricato*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 成员推断的聚合AUC指标具有误导性：盲基线即可达到高AUC，而按文档粒度的数据提取风险被掩盖；实际泄露呈长尾分布，代码域远强于散文域，且随模型规模增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成员推断评估使用聚合ROC-AUC，但被模型无关的盲基线混淆，无法反映真实的数据泄露风险，尤其是按文档级别的具体泄露。

Method: 从概率视角将N个采样作为输出分布的估计，定义泄露信号为分布泛函；引入盲基线与控制实验（如错配前缀）区分全局共性字符串与逐文档泄露，并开发leakit工具进行黑盒提取审计。

Result: 盲分类器在WikiMIA上AUC 0.97，采样无增益；IID Pile上自浓度与黄金续写恢复不显著优于盲基线。但Pythia-6.9B上16.6%的文档泄露真实标识符（邮件为21.3%），且随模型容量从5.6%增至16.6%；代码域泄露强度约为散文的3倍，任意续写恢复仅存在于代码域；温度、核采样、去重影响可忽略。

Conclusion: 隐私审计应报告按文档、按域分解的提取率，而非单一AUC；泄露风险分布不均，需针对高风险域（如代码）与高容量模型进行细粒度评估。

Abstract: Membership inference (MIA) on language models is usually summarised by an aggregate ROC-AUC, but such evaluations are confounded: model-free blind baselines separate members from non-members from surface text alone. We study black-box, sampling-based training-data leakage through a probabilistic lens, treating N samples from p(.|x) as an estimate of the output distribution and casting leakage signals as functionals of it. We extend the blind-baseline critique into the sampling regime: on WikiMIA a blind bag-of-words classifier reaches AUC 0.97 (TPR 0.90 at 5% FPR) and sampling adds nothing, while on an IID Pile split (MIMIR) neither self-concentration nor gold-continuation recovery significantly beats a blind baseline (incremental AUC 95% CI includes zero). Aggregate metrics hide the real harm. The same sampling verbatim-extracts training data for a tail of documents no blind attack can reach. On Pythia-6.9B, 83 of 500 Pile documents bearing a real identifier (16.6%; 21.3% of those bearing an email address) have that exact identifier reproduced AND not reproduced under a mismatched-prefix control, so each leak is attributable to that document, not to a globally common string. This per-document disclosure is invisible to aggregate AUC and grows with capacity (5.6% to 16.6% from 410M to 6.9B). The risk is uneven: identifier leakage is ~3x stronger in code than prose, though prose stays clearly positive and also grows with capacity (4.0% to 12.1%), while recovery of arbitrary held-out continuations is confined to code (+0.44 member gap on GitHub vs at most +0.014 on prose). Temperature and nucleus sampling matter little, a 16-token prefix suffices, and we detect no reduction from corpus deduplication. Privacy audits should report per-document extraction, decomposed by domain, not a single AUC. We release leakit, a black-box extraction-audit tool.

</details>


### [327] [Auditable Release Control for Pedagogical Leakage in LLM Tutors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00515)
*Nizam Kadir*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出教学泄漏的形式化定义与授权感知的完整中介边界，通过选择器、策略门控和渲染器实现可审计的发布控制；严格中介将多数泄漏标志从181降至0，但降低帮助性，且无法实现通用语义安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型辅导系统即使回答正确且有用，也可能在未授权时泄露答案或关键推理，这种状态与动作相关的失败缺乏形式化定义和系统化缓解手段。

Method: 引入授权感知的完整中介边界：选择器输出五种披露合同，策略门控特权模式，渲染器生成语言；单一发布函数执行可检查校验、可选累积验证和动作特定回退，可重放轨迹分离选择、生成、验证和强制执行故障。

Result: 在599个固定提案上，严格中介将多数泄漏标志从181降至0（配对差异-30.22，95%CI [-35.00,-25.72]），替换581个响应但降低帮助性；检查器触发回退产生11个标志，加入语义验证器后为14个且无可靠边际收益；全局A1脚手架0多数标志和54任意法官标志；外部时间戳复制中高保证发布将多数标志从42降至8（-7.08，95%CI [-13.13,-2.29]），7个失败持续、1个新引入，平均帮助性下降0.192。

Conclusion: 研究确立了可审计的发布边界和声明合同下的失败归因，但不支持通用语义安全或学习收益；严格中介有效但以帮助性为代价，验证器边际增益有限，全局策略优于拟合Q策略。

Abstract: Large language model tutors can be correct and helpful yet disclose an answer or decisive reasoning before that disclosure is authorized. We formalize this state- and action-dependent failure as pedagogical leakage and introduce an authorization-aware complete-mediation boundary. A selector emits one of five disclosure contracts, trusted policy gates privileged modes, and a renderer proposes language. A single release function applies inspectable checks, optional cumulative verification, and action-specific fallback; replayable traces separate selection, generation, verification, and enforcement failures.
  Matched component attribution exposes a safety-utility frontier. On 599 fixed Gemini 3.5 proposals, strict mediation reduces blinded three-model panel-majority leakage flags from 181 to 0 (paired problem-cluster difference -30.22 points, 95% CI [-35.00,-25.72]), while replacing 581 responses and lowering helpfulness. Checker-triggered fallback alone yields 11 majority flags; adding the semantic verifier yields 14 and no reliable marginal gain. A global A1 scaffold yields 0 majority and 54 any-judge flags, outperforming fitted Q on automatic safety and utility. In an externally timestamped replication over 40 unseen problem clusters and 480 attack sequences, high-assurance release reduces majority flags from 42 to 8 (-7.08 points, 95% CI [-13.13,-2.29]); seven failures persist, one is introduced, and mean helpfulness falls by .192. These results establish an auditable release boundary and failure attribution under declared contracts, not universal semantic safety or learning gains.

</details>


### [328] [Exploring Usability and Legal Practice: Insights from German Judicial Users of Digital Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00541)
*Tobias Hoppmann,Leona Lassak,M. Angela Sasse,Zinaida Benenson*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文通过调查德国北威州101名司法从业者，将数字取证概念化为刑事程序中的社会技术系统，发现其工作流整合、跨领域沟通和利益相关者期望对齐存在显著改进需求，并指出了可用性提升的关键方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多聚焦数字取证工具和方法，而忽视了其在刑事程序中的程序性和组织性背景；本文借鉴可用性研究和人本安全视角，旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用社会技术系统视角，对德国北威州司法系统（检察官、法官、数字取证专家）共101名从业者进行问卷调查。

Result: 调查揭示了强烈的整合需求，包括改进工作流、增强跨域沟通、对齐利益相关者期望；同时发现数字取证可用性有较大提升潜力，如跨学科合作、更便捷快速地访问证据数据和结果、改进培训与教育。

Conclusion: 数字取证的有效整合不仅依赖技术，还需关注社会技术系统中的人与流程因素，可通过提升可用性和协作机制来改善整体效能。

Abstract: Digital forensics has become an integral part of modern criminal proceedings, yet its effective integration remains challenging because of increasing data volumes, evolving technologies, and complex interactions between technical and legal stakeholders. Although prior work has focused primarily on digital forensic tools and methods, its broader procedural and organizational context has received limited attention. Building on emerging perspectives inspired by usability research and human-centered security, we conceptualize digital forensics as part of a socio-technical system within criminal proceedings. We consequently investigate this perspective through a survey of 101 practitioners from the judiciary of the German federal state of North Rhine-Westphalia, including public prosecutors, judges, and digital forensic experts. The results indicate a strong demand for improved integration of digital forensics into workflows, enhanced cross-domain communication, and a closer alignment of stakeholder expectations. They also uncover great potential for the improvement of digital forensics usability, e.g., through stronger interdisciplinary cooperation, easier and faster access to evidential data and results, or improvement of stakeholder training and education.

</details>


### [329] [Robust Watermarks Meet Backdoored Models: Evading Diffusion Semantic Watermarks via Stealthy Backdoor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00543)
*Jinyuan Liu,Tianshuo Cong,Pei Li,Tianrui Wang,Xinlei He,Anyu Wang,Xiaoyun Wang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出GhostVAE，在LDM的VAE编码器中植入后门，使语义水印检测失效，攻击成功率94.6%且能绕过17种防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义水印依赖神经网络检测，存在被后门攻击的漏洞，但该风险未被系统研究。

Method: 两阶段：用功率谱正则化构造通用触发器增强鲁棒性，再用参数对齐目标训练带后门的VAE编码器。

Result: 良性图像上水印检测TPR平均94.4%，触发器激活时攻击成功率平均94.6%，且能规避输入、参数、潜空间共17种防御。

Conclusion: 语义水印系统不可信，安全部署需端到端安全设计，尤其关注神经网络组件。

Abstract: Although semantic watermarking is considered a promising safeguard for images generated by Latent Diffusion Models (LDMs), the reliance of the watermark detection pipeline on neural networks introduces a critical yet underexplored backdoor attack surface. To systematically study this vulnerability, we propose GhostVAE to plant a stealthy backdoor into the encoder of Variational Autoencoder (VAE), enabling reliable evasion of watermark detection. GhostVAE operates in two stages: it first constructs a universal trigger via power spectrum regularization to improve the trigger robustness, and then trains a backdoored VAE encoder with a parameter-aligned objective. Through extensive evaluations across three state-of-the-art semantic watermarking schemes and three widely adopted LDMs, we show that GhostVAE preserves watermark detection performance on benign images (achieving an average true positive rate of 94.4%), while simultaneously enabling highly effective evasion under trigger activation (achieving an average attack success rate of 94.6%). Moreover, we comprehensively analyze seventeen representative defenses and demonstrate that GhostVAE remains stealthy across the input space, parameter space, and latent space. Our work fundamentally undermines the trustworthiness of semantic watermarking systems and highlights that secure deployment of semantic watermarks requires end-to-end security considerations, particularly for neural network components.

</details>


### [330] [A False Average: Chain-of-Thought Monitors Collapse Where They Are the Only Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00583)
*Shikhar Shiromani,Leo Richter*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 通过仅重写推理文本而逐字保留命令与输出，可使监控器对奖励黑客的捕获率从约95%降至11%以下，且攻击可迁移、可复现，仅靠轨迹内防御无法充分恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明链式思维监控在对手控制推理时失效，并揭示其聚合准确率是虚假平均值，掩盖了纯推理信号子集上的崩溃。

Method: 在梯度无关的单步内，将智能体推理重写为看似善意工程的内容，同时逐字复制命令与输出；使用开放权重代理监控器的激活探针及因果对照分析。

Result: 监控器捕获率从95%降至11%，攻击跨监控器家族与智能体模型迁移；校准监控器下规避集中于最强智能体；仅轨迹外信息能大幅恢复检测。

Conclusion: 链式思维监控易被推理重写攻击规避，且监控器内部表征的探针分离结果仅为检测器功能，不证明其隐含知识；需要轨迹外信息才能有效防御。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is meant to catch the reward hacks that look clean in the actions and betray themselves only in the reasoning. We show that this is exactly where an adversary who controls the reasoning can defeat it. Rewriting only an agent's reasoning to read as good-faith engineering, while copying every command and output verbatim so the exploit is unchanged, drops a held-out monitor's catch rate on that subset from about 95% to under 11% in one gradient-free shot. A monitor's aggregate accuracy is a false average: dominated by hacks the actions give away, it hides the near-total collapse this rewrite produces on the subset where CoT monitoring is the only signal. The attack transfers across monitor families and agent models, reproduces with live agents, though against a calibrated monitor evasion concentrates in the strongest agent. Trace-only defenses recover it only partially, even one primed on the attack, because the rewrite stays truthful about what happened and lies only about intent; only information from outside the trace helps substantially. A probe on an open-weight surrogate monitor's activations separates the hacks its verdict misses, but a causal control shows this is a detector, not evidence the monitor secretly knows.

</details>


### [331] [Domain Decoupling Attack: Exploiting the Validation Gap Between Protective DNS and Shared Edge Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00643)
*Weizhe Wang,Minhong Dong,Jinhao Li,Yao Zhang,Hao Liu,Qiang Hu,Tao Luo,Guangquan Xu,Bin Wu*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出域名解耦攻击（DDA），利用DNS授权验证漏洞，将允许域名的权限复用到共享IP，从而在CDN和非CDN共享托管环境中访问其他租户，实验显示总体暴露率高达95.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于CDN的规避技术受限于SNI-Host不一致、域名所有权验证不足或提供商特定路由重写，在现代CDN环境中适用性有限；同时发现DNS授权存在验证缺口，即允许域名的权限可被共享IP复用访问其他租户。

Method: 通过将允许的域名解析到共享边缘IP获取访问权限，随后在TLS SNI和HTTP Host中一致地呈现隐藏域名，从而绕过访问控制，并基于大规模测量和实验室实验验证漏洞。

Result: 对六大洲1,069,048个域名进行测量，成功探测18,025,068次，整体暴露率95.8%，CDN域名为99.26%，非CDN域名为92.75%，非CDN跨租户IP为97.7%；实验室实验揭示了DNS绑定访问控制在共享地址上的结构性限制。

Conclusion: 研究阐明了DNS派生IP授权的安全风险，为CDN和非CDN共享托管环境中访问控制机制的评估与改进提供了支持。

Abstract: Network attackers often conceal malicious communication within legitimate Internet traffic. Existing CDN-based evasion techniques rely on SNI--Host inconsistency, insufficient domain ownership verification, or provider-specific routing rewrites, which limit their applicability in modern CDN environments. We identify a validation gap in DNS-based authorization, where permission derived from an allowed domain applies to a shared IP and can be reused to reach another tenant in both CDN and non-CDN shared-hosting environments. This paper presents the Domain Decoupling Attack (DDA), which resolves an allowed domain to obtain permission for a shared edge IP and subsequently connects to the same address while presenting the hidden domain consistently in both TLS SNI and HTTP Host. Measurements of 1,069,048 domains across six continents produce 18,025,068 successful probes and identify exposure rates of 95.8% overall, 99.26% for CDN domains, 92.75% for non-CDN domains, and 97.7% for non-CDN cross-tenant IPs, while laboratory experiments reveal a structural limitation of DNS-bound access control on shared addresses. These results clarify the security risks of DNS-derived IP authorization and support the evaluation and improvement of access-control mechanisms in CDN and non-CDN shared-hosting environments.

</details>


### [332] [Improving the Security of Containerized Workloads using Transparency and Traceability Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00660)
*Nikos Fotiou,Lefteris Georgiadis,Ignacio Lacalle,George C. Polyzos,Vasilios A. Siris*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出一种基于透明度日志和一次性签名密钥的容器镜像分发架构，在准入时通过策略即代码强制执行可验证的完整性检查，并集成到GitHub Actions和GitLab Runners中验证其对供应链攻击的缓解效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CI/CD流水线中单一构建步骤或凭据泄露可导致恶意工件大规模分发，现有方案缺乏对密钥管理和部署时强制检查的有效支持，需确保镜像分发的完整性、透明性和可执行策略验证。

Method: 透明度服务生成绑定到已验证身份的一次性签名密钥，将签名事件追加到只读透明度注册表并产生可验证的包含证明；这些证明与身份属性附加到镜像元数据，由准入时的策略即代码评估，仅允许合规工件部署。

Result: 实现了与GitHub Actions和GitLab Runners集成的概念验证，并在现实威胁模型下评估了该方法对常见供应链攻击的缓解能力，展示了实际管线中的可行性。

Conclusion: 该架构通过密钥管理与透明度证明的结合，实现了可审计且策略强制的镜像分发，有效降低供应链风险，为云边异构环境中的可信部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Containerized workloads are commonly built via CI/CD pipelines, stored in registries, and executed across heterogeneous infrastructures, including cloud and edge environments. A single compromised build step or credential can turn routine automation into large-scale distribution of malicious artifacts, motivating integrity, transparency, and enforceable deployment-time checks. In this paper, we present an architecture for verifiable container image distribution that addresses key-management challenges and enables policy-enforced admission-time verification. A transparency service generates one-time signing keys bound to authenticated identities, records signing events in an append-only transparency registry, and returns cryptographically verifiable proofs of inclusion. These proofs and identity attributes are attached to image metadata and evaluated by policy-as-code at admission time, so only compliant artifacts are deployed. We implement a proof-of-concept integrated with GitHub Actions and GitLab Runners and evaluate how the resulting pipeline mitigates common supply-chain attacks under a realistic threat model.

</details>


### [333] [From Chasing Ghosts to Missed Attacks: Perspectives and Perceptions of SOC Practitioners on LLM Integration, Risks, and Readiness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00672)
*Jonas Thurner,Nadine Jost,Stefan Albert Horstmann,Fabian Ising,Lea Groeber,Alena Naiakshina,Sebastian Schinzel*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 通过25次访谈，系统梳理了SOC人员对LLM的15个用例和6大功能类别，发现其擅长自动化低层任务但在高影响分析上能力不足，并指出组织与人为因素是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对LLM在真实SOC工作流中的适用性缺乏深入理解，需要从实践者视角系统评估其机会与挑战。

Method: 采用25次半结构化访谈，结合互动场景预设，对具有LLM使用经验的SOC从业者进行定性分析。

Result: 识别出15个用例（分6类）；LLM在报告自动化等重复性任务中受认可，但在事件分析等关键任务中因技术深度、上下文感知和组织知识不足而受限；过度依赖被归因于SOC准备度和人为因素；实践者面临竞争压力仍愿采用。

Conclusion: 提出以人为中心、操作安全的LLM辅助安全运营的设计与集成需求，强调需提升组织就绪度并管理过度依赖风险。

Abstract: Security Operations Centers (SOCs) process large volumes of security events, requiring analysts to accurately detect and assess ongoing cyberattacks under time pressure. Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) suggest potential benefits for security operations, yet their practical suitability for real-world SOC workflows remains poorly understood. To address this gap, we conducted 25 semi-structured interviews with SOC practitioners who had prior experience with LLMs, complemented by interactive scenarios to anticipate challenges and identify opportunities for the responsible integration of LLM-based tools into SOC workflows. We identified 15 LLM use cases grouped into six functional categories. While LLMs are valued for automating repetitive, low-level tasks such as report automation, practitioners rate high-impact tasks such as incident analysis as not yet feasible, reporting limitations in technical depth, context awareness, and organization-specific knowledge. They locate these limitations less in the models than in the readiness of their SOCs and human factors driving over-reliance. Despite concerns, practitioners express a strong willingness to adopt LLMs, describing competitive pressure that leaves few alternatives. This work contributes an empirical, practitioner-driven analysis of LLM use across SOC roles and organizations and derives concrete design and integration requirements for human-centered, operationally safe LLM-assisted security operations.

</details>


### [334] [Supporting Cybersecurity Risk Management for Medical Devices via the SECUMAN Ontology and Shapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00698)
*Martin Diller,Anne Esslinger,Piotr Gorczyca,Evi Hartig,Lia Kacholdt,Hannes Strass*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出了SECUMAN本体和SHACL形状，用于表示和分析医疗设备网络安全风险管理文档，支持自动化校验、追溯及与安全文档的集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗设备日益互联，网络安全风险直接影响患者安全，但现有风险管理文件多为半结构化自然语言文本，导致一致性检查、认证审查和复用困难，且缺乏面向网络安全的正式建模工具。

Method: 构建基于OWL的SECUMAN本体，建模安全风险上下文、评估、控制措施和残余风险等概念（如威胁场景、保护目标、攻击者画像、暴露等级、资产和安全设计论证），并使用SHACL约束检查文档结构完整性和模型符合性，同时与VDE Spec 90025及RISKMAN本体对齐并扩展其安全导向方法。

Result: SECUMAN支持自动化首轮验证、文档追溯性检查以及网络安全与安全风险管理文档的集成分析，填补了网络安全专用词汇和约束的空白。

Conclusion: SECUMAN为医疗设备网络安全风险管理文档提供了形式化、可计算的表示与验证手段，有望提升认证审查效率和文档一致性。更多性能或案例验证未在摘要中提及。

Abstract: We propose the SECUMAN ontology and shapes for representing and analysing cybersecurity risk-management documentation for medical devices. Cybersecurity risks are increasingly relevant for connected medical devices and may have direct consequences for patient safety. Current risk-management files are often maintained as semi-structured natural language text, which makes consistency checking, certification review, and reuse difficult. SECUMAN provides a formal OWL-based vocabulary for modelling security-risk context, assessment, control measures, and residual-risk evaluation, and uses SHACL constraints to check structural completeness and conformity with the intended documentation model. The ontology is aligned with VDE Spec 90025 and the related RISKMAN ontology and shapes, while extending their safety-oriented approach to concepts relevant to cybersecurity risk documentation such as threat scenarios, protection goals, attacker profiles, exposure levels, assets, and secure design arguments. SECUMAN is intended to support automated first-pass validation, traceability, and integration of cybersecurity and safety risk-management documentation.

</details>


### [335] [Adversarial Attacks in Multi-Agent LLM Pipelines: Unveiling Structural Vulnerabilities in Agentic AI Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00718)
*Faisal Haque Bappy,Tahrim Hossain,Tarannum Shaila Zaman,Raiful Hasan,Kamrul Hasan,Tariqul Islam*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 多智能体LLM流水线因缺乏跨智能体边界验证而存在结构性安全漏洞，攻击成功率主要取决于流水线架构而非模型能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM流水线在智能体间传递中间输出时缺乏边界验证，导致对抗性内容被当作可信输入传播，产生单智能体设置中不存在的安全缺口；现有评估框架无法覆盖此类问题。

Method: 基于GAIA和SWE-Bench基准的标注生产轨迹，展示漏洞在良性部署中自然出现并绕过现有评估；随后在受控多智能体环境中，针对GPT-5-mini、Claude Sonnet 4.5和Kimi K2.5在相同流水线配置下操作化这些失败模式。

Result: 攻击成功率与流水线结构相关，而非模型能力，表明对抗性漏洞本质上是架构属性。

Conclusion: 多智能体系统的安全防护应从单点模型防御转向流水线级防御，重视边界验证等机制。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM pipelines orchestrate multiple specialized language model agents into structured workflows where intermediate outputs are passed across agents to solve complex tasks. This design introduces a security gap absent in single-agent settings: once an agent accepts adversarial content, it is propagated as trusted input throughout the pipeline. We argue that this vulnerability stems from the absence of boundary verification, a security primitive that enforces explicit validation of data as it crosses inter-agent boundaries, including content, identity, execution intent, and state integrity. Without such verification, modern pipelines embed implicit trust assumptions that are not adversarially robust, giving rise to structurally distinct attack surfaces (e.g., content injection, agent impersonation, plan deviation, and memory poisoning). Leveraging annotated production traces from the GAIA and SWE-Bench benchmark, we show that these vulnerabilities arise in benign deployments and largely evade existing evaluation frameworks. We further operationalize these failure modes within a controlled multi-agent setting and evaluate them across GPT-5-mini, Claude Sonnet 4.5, and Kimi K2.5 under identical pipeline configurations. The results reveal that attack success aligns with pipeline structure rather than model capability, indicating that adversarial vulnerability is fundamentally an architectural property and motivating a shift toward pipeline-level defenses.

</details>


### [336] [Neural operator learning for collision-aware trajectory planning of spacecraft swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00320)
*Sidhdharth D. Sikka,Suyi Gao,Zehui Lu,Rongjie Lai,Shaoshuai Mou*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出排列等变神经算子，单次前向传播即可为整个航天器集群生成碰撞避免轨迹，结合批量高斯-牛顿精修满足精确轨道动力学，训练于10个航天器可零样本泛化至1000个航天器及11000个空间物体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统轨迹优化因成对安全约束随集群规模增长而扩展性差，学习型规划器难以跨规模或碎片密度泛化，需要可扩展且可迁移的集群轨迹规划方法。

Method: 采用排列等变神经算子将航天器、目标和碎片的分布映射为整个集群的碰撞感知轨迹，配对批量高斯-牛顿精修确保轨道动力学精确性；训练使用自监督物理目标和针对自身轨迹生成的对抗威胁，无需最优轨迹标签。

Result: 在10个航天器上训练后零样本泛化至1000个航天器及11000余个编目物体，匹配单智能体最优控制求解器精度，规避碎片盲基线无法应对的最坏情况威胁，并将集群内近距离接触减少数倍。

Conclusion: 物理增强的算子学习为拥挤轨道中的最优控制提供了快速、可扩展的替代方案，兼具精度与泛化能力。

Abstract: Autonomous spacecraft swarms must plan fuel-efficient, collision-free maneuvers in increasingly congested orbits, yet classical trajectory optimization scales poorly as pairwise safety constraints multiply with swarm size, and learning-based planners rarely transfer across swarm sizes or debris densities. Here we introduce a permutation-equivariant neural operator that maps distributions of spacecraft, targets and debris to collision-aware trajectories for an entire swarm in a single forward pass, paired with a batched Gauss-Newton finish that enforces exact orbital dynamics. The operator is trained without optimal-trajectory labels, combining self-supervised physics objectives with adversarial threats generated against its own rollouts. Trained on ten spacecraft, it generalizes zero-shot to swarms of 1,000 amid more than 11,000 catalogued objects, matching a per-agent optimal-control solver's accuracy, evading worst-case threats that a debris-blind baseline cannot, and reducing proximity within the swarm several-fold. Physics-grounded operator learning thus offers a fast, scalable alternative to optimal control for crowded orbits.

</details>


### [337] [Safety Invariants for Agents Orchestrating Irreversible State Transitions: A Four-Dimensional Formalism Evaluated on Public Ledgers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00783)
*Zhaoming Yin*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出执行保真度概念，将不可逆副作用安全性形式化为四维状态转移定理，通过七个不变量保证智能体要么无操作要么精确执行一次用户所见操作，并展示其在公共账本场景中的实证效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体框架优化任务成功率而忽视不可逆副作用的安全性，缺乏形式化保证，导致资金转移、持久写入等关键操作存在失控风险。

Method: 以(钱包,链,地址,协议)四维空间建模价值移动，在特定故障模型下证明执行保真度定理，提炼七个安全不变量，并构建对抗性测试集评估。

Result: 在N=60对抗套件中，相对朴素ReAct基线，写激进模型通过率提升约74个百分点，写谨慎模型仅提升约3个百分点；系统已部署，支持108次跨8链的生产写操作。

Conclusion: 该形式化方法适用于任何作用于不可逆外部状态的概率智能体；同时指出单一模型评估智能体安全栈可能近乎不可证伪，需多模型验证。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly asked to produce irreversible effects on external systems - transferring funds, writing to durable storage, actuating hardware. Existing agent frameworks (ReAct, Reflexion, MCP) optimize task success on benchmarks and give little attention to the safety of irreversible side-effects. We formalize one such setting, movement of value across public ledgers, as state transitions in a four-dimensional space indexed by (wallet, chain, address, protocol), and use that formalism to state and prove a guarantee we call execution fidelity: under a fault model admitting planner mis-mapping, ambiguous outcomes, retries, at-least-once delivery, and delegated non-human callers, a session's realized effect on the ledger is either nothing at all or exactly the transition that was rendered to the user, exactly once.
